TL;DR: 本文提出Ground-Truth Neighborhood Regularization (GTN-R)方法，用于解决时间序列基础模型(TSFM)在强化学习(RL)后训练中出现的"次优坍缩"(suboptimal collapse)问题，即RL后训练使模型输出分布偏离真实值导致性能下降。GTN-R以真实值为参考定位高质量区域，引导模型概率质量向真值邻域靠拢，从而减少次优坍缩并提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSFM在大规模预训练后展现出强大的泛化能力，RL后训练被广泛用于进一步提升其在下游任务上的性能。然而，研究发现RL后训练在某些预测区域可能逐渐使TSFM的输出分布偏离真实值（即"次优坍缩"），限制模型性能。分析指出，初始阶段难以在真实值附近采样到高质量轨迹是导致次优坍缩的重要因素。

Method: 提出GTN-R方法：以地面真值作为参考来定位高质量区域，通过正则化引导模型的概率质量向真值邻域移动，增加采样到高质量轨迹的概率。该方法设计为可灵活集成到各种TSFM的RL后训练方法中。

Result: 大量实验表明GTN-R方法有效，能够缓解次优坍缩问题并提升TSFM的预测性能，且具有良好的方法兼容性。

Conclusion: GTN-R通过利用地面真值作为高质量区域的锚点，有效解决了RL后训练中TSFM的次优坍缩问题，为TSFM的RL后训练提供了一种通用且有效的正则化策略。

Abstract: Time series forecasting (TSF) plays an important role in a wide range of real-world applications. Recently, time series foundation models (TSFMs), pretrained on large-scale datasets, have demonstrated strong generalization capabilities and emerged as an important paradigm for TSF. Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training has consequently attracted growing attention as a means of further improving their performance on downstream tasks. However, we find that, in certain forecast regions, RL post-training may gradually shift the output distributions of TSFMs away from the ground truth, thereby limiting their performance. We refer to this phenomenon as \textbf{suboptimal collapse}. Our analysis suggests that difficulty in initially sampling high-quality trajectories near the ground truth is an important contributing factor to suboptimal collapse. To address this issue, we propose Ground-Truth Neighborhood Regularization (GTN-R) for RL post-training of TSFMs. GTN-R uses the ground truth as a reference for locating high-quality regions and guides the model's probability mass toward the ground-truth neighborhood. This increases the probability of sampling high-quality trajectories, mitigates suboptimal collapse, and improves performance. Moreover, GTN-R can be flexibly integrated into various RL methods for TSFMs. Extensive experiments show its effectiveness.

</details>


### [36] [Do All LLMs Know When They're Being Harmful? A Reproducibility Study of Latent-Space Safety Probes Across Model Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08029)
*Alizishaan Khatri,Dun Li Chan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文复现并扩展了Khatri等人(2026)的研究，验证了轻量级MLP探针在8B模型最终层激活上检测有害提示的有效性。结果显示该方法在LLaMA-3.1-8B上可复现（F1差异在0.37个百分点内），且可泛化到其他模型架构（F1差异在1个百分点内），同时发现最终token隐向量不受随机种子影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原研究的两个开放问题：一是轻量级MLP探针是否有益于检测有害提示的方法能否泛化到其他模型架构和规模；二是推理过程中的非确定性对报告性能的影响程度。原研究仅使用单一8B模型（LLaMA-3.1-8B），且未系统评估随机种子对结果的影响。

Method: 复现原始的端到端MLP探针流水线，并沿两个维度扩展：(1) 在Gemma-4-E4B、Mistral-7B-v0.3、Qwen2-7B等其他模型架构和规模上训练相同的探针，使用三个基准（WildJailbreak、BeaverTails、AEGIS 2.0）进行评估；(2) 在五个随机种子下重复提取激活，测量F1分数的方差以评估非确定性的影响。

Result: 复现LLaMA模型基准的F1分数与原研究相差在0.37个百分点以内（BeaverTails上在0.2个百分点以内）。MLP探针架构可扩展到其他模型家族，F1分数与LLaMA-3.1-8B报告值相差在1个百分点以内。种子变化实验中，所有测试架构的最终token潜在向量保持一致，不随随机种子改变。

Conclusion: 轻量级MLP探针检测有害提示的方法具有良好的可复现性和跨架构泛化能力，在多个模型家族上均能取得与LLaMA-3.1-8B相近的性能；同时最终token隐向量对随机种子不敏感，表明该方法的非确定性影响极低。

Abstract: Khatri et al. (2026) [DOI: 10.1109/DSN-W70714.2026.00027] show that lightweight MLP probes on final-layer activations of a single 8B model (LLaMA-3.1-8B) detect harmful prompts at F1 competitive with guard models 1000x larger, using one probe per benchmark. We reproduce this pipeline end-to-end and extend it along two axes the original study leaves open. First, we test whether the result generalizes across other model architecture and scale by training identical probes on activations from models like Gemma-4-E4B, Mistral-7B-v0.3, and Qwen2-7B, using the three benchmarks (WildJailbreak, BeaverTails, AEGIS 2.0). Second, we test how much of the reported performance is affected by non-determinism during inference by repeating extraction under five random seeds and measuring the variance of F1 scores. Our results reproduce the original LLaMA model benchmarks within 0.37 percentage points of the original F1 scores (and within 0.2 points on BeaverTails). We find that the original MLP probe architecture extends to other model families with F1 scores within a point of the values reported for LLaMA-3.1-8B. Our experiments varying seed values reveal an interesting observation: final token latent vectors remained the same for all tested architectures irrespective of the seed values used.

</details>


### [37] [CLAM: Causal Spatial Disaggregation to Infer Local Effects From Coarse Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08064)
*Gerrit Großmann,Sumantrak Mukherjee,Sebastian J. Vollmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CLAM是一种从粗粒度观测数据中估计局部因果效应的方法，通过联合学习因果机制与分解映射，利用高分辨率上下文协变量来捕捉被独立处理这些问题时遗漏的交互作用，支持局部效应估计、反事实推理与结果分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从粗分辨率数据中学习细粒度空间模式具有挑战性，尤其在因果设置中，高分辨率效应必须从聚合的干预和结果中推断。现有方法将因果机制学习与分解映射分开处理，会遗漏两者之间的关键交互。

Method: CLAM联合学习因果机制和分解映射（disaggregation mapping），通过利用调节因果效应的粗粒度观测数据与高分辨率上下文协变量，捕捉独立处理时被遗漏的交互作用。

Result: 该方法支持局部效应估计、反事实推理和原则性的结果分解，并在多种不同设置下可靠地捕捉空间变化的因果效应。

Conclusion: CLAM对于公共卫生和环境政策等需要在广泛尺度上做出决策但同时存在显著局部异质性的应用场景具有重要价值，代码已开源。

Abstract: Learning fine-grained spatial patterns from coarse-resolution data is challenging, especially in causal settings where high-resolution effects must be inferred from aggregated interventions and outcomes. We introduce CLAM, a method for estimating localized causal effects from coarse observations by exploiting high-resolution contextual covariates that modulate these effects. By jointly learning the causal mechanism and a disaggregation mapping, CLAM captures interactions that are missed when addressing these problems independently. The method supports localized effect estimation, counterfactual reasoning, and principled outcome disaggregation, and reliably captures spatially varying causal effects across diverse settings. This is particularly relevant for applications such as public health and environmental policy, where decisions are made at broad scales despite substantial local heterogeneity. Code is available at https://github.com/gerritgr/clam

</details>


### [38] [Adaptive Symmetry Discovery for Dynamical System Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08091)
*Behrooz Tahmasebi,Melanie Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究动态系统识别中的自适应对称性发现问题，证明了当系统对未知对称群具有等变性时，可以从更短的单条轨迹中识别系统参数，并提出自动学习对称群的方法，达到与已知对称性情形相同的最优轨迹长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态系统识别旨在从观测轨迹中恢复系统参数，现实的科学系统通常具有物理定律所施加的对称性（等价于群作用的等变性）。然而现有工作大多假设对称性已知，而在实际应用中对称群往往是未知的。本文旨在解决在未知对称群条件下如何从单条轨迹高效识别动态系统的问题。

Method: 首先从理论上证明，在已知对称性的情形下，系统可以从比通用设定显著更短的单条轨迹中被识别，并且精确刻画了这一改进的量化关系。随后针对未知对称群的自适应发现场景，提出一种直接从单条轨迹学习对称群并将其融入识别流程的方法。分析工具包括群表示论和Cayley图的扩张性质。

Result: 在已知对称性设定下，系统识别所需的单条轨迹长度显著缩短，且该改进被精确刻画。在自动对称性发现设定下，提出的方法能够直接从单条轨迹学习对称群并实现与已知对称性情形相同的轨迹长度，达到最优识别效率。

Conclusion: 本文表明对称性可以显著降低动态系统识别的数据需求，并且即使对称群未知，也可以自动发现并利用对称性达到最优识别性能。所采用的群表示论与Cayley图扩张工具为动态系统中的对称性研究提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Dynamical systems model trajectory data generated by fixed underlying dynamics, with applications ranging from biology to physics. Especially in scientific settings, dynamical systems are not generic but often exhibit symmetries imposed by physical laws, formalized through equivariance with respect to group actions. The identification problem concerns recovering the parameters of a system from observed trajectories. In this work, we study adaptive symmetry discovery for dynamical system identification and address how a system can be identified from a single trajectory when it is equivariant with respect to an unknown symmetry group. To this end, we first show that for known symmetries, the system can be identified from a significantly shorter single trajectory than in the generic setting, and we precisely characterize this improvement. We then consider the automatic symmetry discovery setting, proposing a method to learn the symmetry group directly from a single trajectory and incorporate it into the identification procedure, achieving the same optimal trajectory length as in the known-symmetry case. Our analysis relies on tools from group representation theory and the expander properties of Cayley graphs, and may be of independent interest for the study of symmetries in dynamical systems.

</details>


### [39] [Support Selection Beyond Smooth DAG Exactness: Completion Geometry,Score Margins, and Selective Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08103)
*Rui Wu,Zongyuan Chen,Hong Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了光滑有向无环图（DAG）可行性约束的数学结构及其在结构学习中的后果。在DAG边界上，最小环补全生成一个平方自由单项式理想，包含精确表示的每个受限Taylor喷射。推导出孤立环的精确选择时间，并通过320条NOTEARS/DAGMA轨迹验证。此外，提出父集置信族和强制反向查询方法，在有限样本下认证骨架和碰撞标签，实验结果将DAG可行性、基于评分的支持选择和因果识别三者分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的研究针对特定约束公式展示了退化性（degeneracy），但没有隔离"光滑精确性"（smooth exactness）本身带来的后果。作者希望从数学上厘清DAG可行性约束的本质结构，并回答结构学习中支持变更（support change）应如何选择的根本问题。

Method: (1) 在DAG边界上，证明最小环补全生成平方自由单项式理想，该理想包含精确表示的每个受限Taylor喷射。 (2) 推导孤立环的精确选择时间，区分可行性仅限时间和带评分边距的动力学，分析ν>0与ν=0（对数边界层）的不同行为。 (3) 提出父集置信族和强制反向查询，用于有限样本下认证骨架和未屏蔽碰撞器标签。

Result: 最小补全为q条边时，向量残差的首个可能响应阶数为q，非负标量为2q。NOTEARS和DAGMA在边界外存在指数多个常尺度循环流形，表现出相同的排序缺失。孤立环的选择时间为T0(ε)=Θ(ε-(2ν+1))；评分边距在ν>0时于T0^-1尺度改变主导动力学，ν=0时需要对数边界层γT0 log(1/ε)→0。在320条官方轨迹上，无真值分离统计量预测选择时间的Spearman相关系数为-0.52和-0.66（置换p<10^-4）。在320次运行中，所有遗憾界均覆盖独立oracle评分审计，3042个认证骨架标签和2396个碰撞器标签无与oracle评分最优不一致，但分别有4.4%和5.5%与生成图不一致。

Conclusion: DAG可行性（feasibility）、基于评分的支持选择（support selection）和因果识别（causal identification）是三个可分离的问题。光滑可行性约束本身的结构（最小环补全的单项式理想）决定了算法的退化行为，而选择时间具有精确可推导的标度律。有限样本下的认证方法能够在不依赖生成真值的情况下保证与oracle评分最优的一致性，但与真实因果图的偏差仍然存在。

Abstract: Smooth acyclicity constraints answer whether a weighted support is a DAG, whereas structure learning asks which support change should be made. Existing analyses establish degeneracy for particular constraint formulas but do not isolate what follows from smooth exactness itself. At a DAG boundary, we show that minimal cycle completions generate a squarefree monomial ideal containing every restricted Taylor jet of an exact representation. If the smallest completion has $q$ edges, the first possible response has order $q$ for a vector residual and $2q$ for a nonnegative scalar. Exponentially many constant-scale cyclic manifolds exhibit the same lack of ranking away from the boundary for NOTEARS and DAGMA. We derive the exact selection time for an isolated cycle. When $Ψ'(h)\asymp h^ν$, the feasibility-only time is $T_0(\varepsilon)=Θ(\varepsilon^{-(2ν+1)})$; a score margin changes the leading dynamics at scale $T_0^{-1}$ for $ν>0$, while $ν=0$ has a logarithmic boundary layer requiring $γT_0\log(1/\varepsilon)\to0$. Experiments verify this law, and a truth-free separation statistic predicts selection time on 320 official NOTEARS/DAGMA trajectories (Spearman $-0.52$ and $-0.66$, permutation $p<10^{-4}$). For finite samples, a parent-set confidence family and forced-opposite queries certify skeleton and unshielded-collider labels shared by every population optimum of a frozen score. Across 320 runs, every regret bound covers an independent oracle-score audit. None of 3,042 certified skeleton or 2,396 collider labels disagrees with the oracle-score optimum, although 4.4% and 5.5%, respectively, disagree with the generating graph. These results separate DAG feasibility, score-based support selection, and causal identification.

</details>


### [40] [TSDS-Toolbox: A Toolbox for Measuring Time-Series Dataset Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08119)
*Yen-Ku Liu,Hongjie Chen,Ryan A. Rossi,Franck Dernoncourt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TSDS-Toolbox，一个统一的时间序列数据集相似性评估工具箱，支持系统化、可复现的比较，灵活的扩展性以及数据集级和序列级相似性方法的一致评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型的性能依赖于源数据集选择的相似性，但现有的时间序列数据集相似性方法的基准实现零散且难以扩展，缺乏统一的评估框架。

Method: 构建统一的TSDS-Toolbox框架，支持：(1)系统化、可复现的时间序列数据集相似性方法比较；(2)灵活扩展自定义数据集、相似性方法和下游任务；(3)通过集成的时间序列数据集缩减器，对数据集级和序列级相似性方法进行一致评估。

Result: 通过在多种实验设置下的综合实验验证了TSDS-Toolbox的有效性，工具箱已公开发布。

Conclusion: TSDS-Toolbox为时间序列数据集相似性研究提供了统一、可扩展且可复现的基准框架，有助于推动该领域的方法比较与标准化评估。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of artificial intelligence (AI) has significantly accelerated research in time-series analysis, particularly in forecasting, classification, and generation tasks. Recent models, especially foundation models, benefit from time-series dataset similarity due to its significant role in source dataset selection for fine-tuning. However, many existing implementations for benchmarking time-series dataset similarity methods are fragmented and difficult to extend. To address this, we present a unified framework, the Time-Series Dataset Similarity Toolbox (TSDS-Toolbox). Our work enables (1) systematic and reproducible comparisons of time-series dataset similarity methods; (2) flexible extensibility for users to add customized datasets, similarity methods, and downstream time-series tasks; and (3) consistent evaluation of both dataset-level and series-level similarity methods through integrated time-series dataset reducers. The effectiveness of TSDS-Toolbox is validated through comprehensive experiments under diverse experimental settings. Our toolbox is publicly available.

</details>


### [41] [Accurate Ensembles, Fragile Narratives: Multi-Scale Stacking and a Fidelity Audit of LLM-Generated Explanations for Credit Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08126)
*Gregorius Reynaldi Pratama,Kuo-Kun Tseng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个端到端的信用评分系统（多尺度Stacking集成模型+LLM生成解释），发现预测性能提升虽显著但操作收益较小，而LLM生成的解释文本严重失实：命名了与归因符号相反的特征、遗漏主导因子、引入未提供的特征。作者通过实证测量SHAP与LIME的排序不一致性和符号不稳定等问题，指出仅靠提示工程约束不足，必须对生成后的归因进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信用评分越来越依赖无法直接读取参数的黑盒模型，与监管要求的"不利决策可解释"相冲突。常见方案是用LLM基于特征归因撰写解释文本，但该方案"第二部分承诺"（LLM撰写解释）是否可靠未经严格检验。作者希望验证这一环节是否成立。

Method: 1) 构建多尺度Stacking集成：四种不同正则化的梯度提升树+残差网络，通过神经元meta-learner在袋外预测上融合。2) 在32,581条样本的公开信用数据集上评估，自设校准(ECS)和扰动稳定性(DPD)阈值。3) 将SHAP/LIME归因交给LLM生成解释，并对生成文本进行人工审计，测量归因一致性（overlap@10、Kendall tau、符号一致性）等属性。

Result: 集成模型达到测试ROC-AUC 0.9539、PR-AUC 0.9137，较最佳单模型提升ΔAUC=0.0143(p=0.016)。但在F1最优阈值下仅额外避免6个漏违约（共1,422个），成本加权损失降低不足2%。LLM解释在审计案例中命名了三个归因符号相反的风险因子、遗漏了主导驱动因子、并引入从未提供的特征。SHAP与LIME的overlap@10=0.80但tau仅0.43(p=0.18)；最敏感输入的符号一致性仅0.53（近掷硬币）。ECS=0.117、DPD=0.078均未达其设定阈值。

Conclusion: 集成的预测增益真实但操作层面收益微小；LLM解释层不可靠，且提示工程无法修复。归因本身的排序不稳定性和符号不确定性是根本瓶颈。约束性提示（grounding）是必要条件但非充分条件，必须在生成后验证归因与实际模型行为的对应关系。

Abstract: Credit scoring increasingly relies on models whose decision logic cannot be read off their parameters, in tension with supervisory expectations that adverse decisions be explainable. A common proposal closes that gap with a language model: compute feature attributions, hand them to an LLM, and let it write the rationale. We build such a system end to end and test whether the second half of the promise holds. The predictive component is a multi-scale stacking ensemble fusing four differently regularised gradient-boosting learners with a residual network through a neural meta-learner trained on out-of-fold predictions. On a public 32,581-application credit dataset it reaches test ROC-AUC 0.9539 (95% CI [0.9462, 0.9616]) and PR-AUC 0.9137, beating the best single model by Delta-AUC = 0.0143 (p = 0.016 under a conservative independence assumption). Our central finding is asymmetric. The ranking gain is real but operationally small: at the F1-optimal threshold the ensemble avoids only six additional missed defaults out of 1,422 against a tuned random forest, cutting cost-weighted loss by under 2%. The narrative layer fails in a way prompt engineering alone does not fix. In an audited case the model named three factors as risk-increasing that the supplied attributions scored as risk-reducing, omitted the dominant driver, and introduced a feature never given to it. We trace this to properties we measure rather than assume: SHAP and LIME agree on which features matter (overlap@10 = 0.80) but not on their order (tau = 0.43, p = 0.18), and the attribution sign for the model's most sensitive input is near a coin flip across applicants (modal-sign share 0.53). Calibration (ECS = 0.117) and perturbation stability (DPD = 0.078) both fall short of our own thresholds. Constrained prompting is necessary but not sufficient: grounding must be verified after generation, not assumed.

</details>


### [42] [DoGMA: A Central-Dogma-Guided Foundation Model for Multi-Omics Alignment and Multi-Task Learning in Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08148)
*Junfei Ling,Bangzheng Pu,Bingsen Xue,Tianle Li,Ruying Hu,Cheng Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DoGMA是一个受中心法则启发的多组学基础模型，通过Transformer-MoE架构中的有向注意力偏差和掩码分层组学重建预训练，引导跨组学信息流遵循中心法则的方向性，从而在泛癌表征学习、生存预测和转移预测等下游任务中展现强预测性能，证明了领域特定归纳偏置能提升多组学基础模型的鲁棒性和可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力机制允许组学间无限制的双向交互，但生命的基本逻辑（中心法则）是方向性的。这种忽视可能限制模型在异质癌症、下游任务和不完整模态设置下的迁移能力。作者主张稳健迁移需要具有领域特定归纳偏置的表征。

Method: 构建在Transformer-MoE架构上的DoGMA模型：1) 引入有向注意力机制，将组间通信偏置为遵循中心法则的信息流方向；2) 采用掩码分层组学重建（masked hierarchical omics reconstruction）预训练策略，引导模型学习与中心法则一致的组学间交互模式。

Result: 在癌症表征学习、生存预测和转移预测等多个下游任务中，DoGMA持续展现强预测性能。消融实验和分析表明性能提升来自中心法则引导的有向注意力与重建式预训练的协同作用，二者共同促进了更符合生物学规律的跨组学信息交换。

Conclusion: DoGMA证明领域特定归纳偏置能提升多组学基础模型的鲁棒性和可迁移性，为多组学表征学习中的注意力机制设计提供了新见解。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms have been widely utilized in modern deep learning, and many existing multi-omics models inherit their conventional use to allow unrestricted bidirectional interactions. However, the fundamental logic of life is directional. Existing designs often overlook the directionality suggested by the central dogma, potentially limiting transfer across heterogeneous cancers, downstream tasks, and incomplete modality settings.In this work, we present DoGMA, a central-dogma-guided foundation model for pan-cancer multi-omics analysis, arguing that robust transfer requires representations with domain-specific inductive bias. Concretely, we build it on a Transformer-MoE architecture where directed attention biases inter-omics communication toward central-dogma information flow. We further pretrain our model with masked hierarchical omics reconstruction to guide it toward learning central-dogma-consistent interactions. Across diverse downstream tasks, including cancer representation learning, survival prediction, and metastasis prediction, DoGMA consistently demonstrates strong predictive performance. Ablations and analyses further suggest that the performance gains arise from the synergy between central-dogma-guided directed attention and reconstruction-based pretraining, which together promote more biologically consistent cross-omics information exchange. Overall, DoGMA demonstrates that domain-specific inductive biases can improve the robustness and transferability of multi-omics foundation models, offering new insights into the design of attention mechanisms for multi-omics representation learning.

</details>


### [43] [Predicting blood clot growth from sparse post-onset measurements with latent neural differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08165)
*Lennon J. Shikhman,Ying Qian,He Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于潜变量神经微分方程的计算框架，用于从稀疏测量数据中推断血栓形成模型的未知参数并预测血栓生长进程。在多功能物理血栓模型中，利用四种已知生化输入（纤维蛋白原、因子IX、VIII和V）及少量早期凝血块尺寸观测，推断组织因子参数并预测后续凝血块生长。比较了七种概率方法，其中随机神经常微分方程（SNODE）性能最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血栓形成的计算模型有助于理解血栓形成机制，但其临床应用受限，因为许多模型输入难以测量，且患者特异性数据往往稀疏。需要一种能够从不完整数据中推断未知参数并进行预测的框架，以实现个性化血栓建模。

Method: 提出基于潜变量神经微分方程的框架，利用多功能物理血栓模型生成的数据进行验证。将四种已知生化输入与稀疏早期凝血块尺寸观测相结合，推断组织因子参数。对比了七种方法：SNODE、SNFDE、潜变量神经过程基线、单调概率深度集成、经验轨迹检索、PCA-脊高斯后验和Gompertz曲线检索。

Result: SNODE在推断未知输入和预测未来凝血块生长轨迹方面表现最佳；SNFDE表现相近且一致优于其他非微分模型。预测精度随观测数据增多而提高，而更长的预测时域增加了不确定性并降低精度。

Conclusion: 潜变量神经微分方程能够有效结合参数推断与凝血块生长预测（基于稀疏测量数据），为个性化血栓建模提供了有前景的基础。

Abstract: Computational models of blood clotting improve understanding of thrombus formation, but their clinical application remains limited because many model inputs are difficult to measure and patient-specific data are often sparse. We present a computational framework based on latent neural differential equations that infers unknown model parameters from sparse measurements and forecasts thrombosis progression. We demonstrate the framework using data generated from a multiphysics blood-clotting model in which clot growth is governed by the coagulation cascade and diffusion. Four known biochemical inputs (fibrinogen and factors IX, VIII, and V), together with sparse early clot-size observations, are used to infer the tissue-factor parameter and predict subsequent clot growth. We compare seven probabilistic methods: stochastic neural ordinary differential equations (SNODE), stochastic neural functional differential equations (SNFDE), a latent neural-process baseline, a monotone probabilistic deep ensemble, empirical trajectory retrieval, PCA-ridge Gaussian posterior, and Gompertz-curve retrieval. SNODE achieved the best performance in inferring the unknown input and forecasting future clot-growth trajectories. SNFDE performed similarly and consistently outperformed the other non-differential models. Prediction accuracy improved as more observations became available, whereas longer forecasting horizons increased uncertainty and decreased accuracy. Latent neural differential equations thus effectively combine parameter inference and clot-growth forecasting from sparse measurements, providing a promising foundation for personalized thrombosis modeling.

</details>

*Alejandro L. García-Navarro,Carlos Sevilla-Salcedo,Belén Rodríguez-Sánchez,Vanessa Gómez-Verdejo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DALMA是一个概率表示学习框架，通过联合建模采集特异性变异和生物学监督信息，学习生物结构化的潜在表示，在跨临床中心的MALDI-TOF质谱微生物鉴定与耐药性预测任务中实现了零样本部署的最优性能。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: DALMA采用概率表示学习框架，将域特异性重建与生物学引导的表示学习相结合。通过联合建模采集特异性和生物学监督，学习可迁移的生物结构化潜在表示，推理时无需机构特异性组件，可实现未见站点的零样本部署。

Conclusion: 生物学信息引导的表示学习为临床微生物学中稳健、可迁移的机器学习提供了有效策略，验证了利用生物学监督而非仅依赖统计对齐来提升跨机构泛化能力的可行性。

Abstract: Machine learning models for MALDI-TOF mass spectrometry have shown considerable promise for clinical microbiology tasks such as microbial identification and antimicrobial resistance prediction. However, their deployment across institutions remains limited by domain shift, as acquisition-specific variability often leads models to capture technical artifacts rather than transferable biological information. Existing representation learning approaches primarily address this problem through statistical domain alignment while largely overlooking the biological supervision naturally available in microbiology datasets. We introduce DALMA, a probabilistic representation learning framework that jointly models acquisition-specific variability and biological supervision to learn biologically structured latent representations. By combining domain-specific reconstruction with biologically guided representation learning, DALMA learns transferable representations that generalize across heterogeneous clinical centers without requiring institution-specific components at inference, enabling zero-shot deployment on previously unseen sites. We evaluate DALMA on a multi-center benchmark comprising seven datasets from three countries. DALMA consistently achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot microbial identification across two held-out clinical centers, while the learned representations also transfer effectively to antimicrobial resistance prediction. Furthermore, latent-space novelty estimation enables reliable selective prediction under previously unseen domain shifts. These results demonstrate that biologically informed representation learning provides an effective strategy for robust and transferable ML in clinical microbiology.

</details>

*Sai Srikar Boddupalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现基于置信度分数的数据清洗管线在消费信贷数据集中存在人口统计偏斜：高收入违约者被不恰当地标记为标签噪声，且即便剔除收入和利率等敏感特征后，模型仍通过贷款金额和房屋所有权等结构性代理变量维持显著的公平性差距（测试集上真阳性率差距2.56个百分点，p<0.0001）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据为中心的AI工作流普遍使用模型置信度分值来过滤噪声标签，但这种过滤惯例可能隐含公平性偏差。作者旨在揭示该惯例在不同收入群体间的不对称影响，并探究其在多大程度上由模型特征依赖所驱动，从而为受监管金融机构的数据审计提供警示。

Method: 使用LendingClub大规模消费贷款数据集（N=1,344,936），首先评估基于置信度分数的噪声过滤在不同收入违约群体间的分布不对称性（Cramer's V衡量）；接着以机会均等（equal opportunity）框架量化真阳性率差距；然后实施序贯特征遮蔽方法，分别隔离自我报告收入、机构原始利率中的制度偏倚以及去除两者后的残差；最后利用样本外签名SHAP值识别维持残差差异的结构性代理变量。

Result: 高收入违约者被识别为标签噪声的比例系统性高于低收入违约者（Cramer's V约0.03-0.07）；高、低收入违约者间真阳性率差距达16.86个百分点；剔除收入和利率后仍存在3.55个百分点（交叉验证）和2.56个百分点（测试集，Z=-4.04，p<0.0001）的残差差距；该残差主要由贷款金额和房屋所有权状态等结构性代理变量所驱动。

Conclusion: 仅仅对算法屏蔽敏感属性无法保证公平性：当机构定价决策（利率）和行为代理变量（贷款金额、房屋所有权）共同重构被移除的信号时，公平性目标依然落空。研究为受监管金融机构审计数据驱动AI工作流提供了可操作的实践启示。

Abstract: Data-centric curation pipelines frequently rely on model confidence scores to flag and filter noisy or mislabeled training instances. Evaluating this filtering convention on a large-scale consumer lending sample (LendingClub, N = 1,344,936) uncovers an underlying demographic asymmetry: high-income defaulters are disproportionately classified as label noise relative to low-income defaulters (Cramer's V approximately 0.03-0.07). Re-examining this behavior through the lens of equal opportunity [Hardt et al., 2016] reveals a far more severe discrepancy: a 16.86 percentage point gap in true positive rate (recall) between high- and low-income borrowers who ultimately defaulted. Implementing a sequential feature-blinding methodology allows us to isolate the drivers of this disparity across three distinct mechanisms: (1) direct reliance on self-reported applicant income; (2) algorithmic absorption of upstream institutional bias encoded within origination interest rates; and (3) a residual disparity (3.55 percentage points in cross-validation; 2.56 percentage points on a held-out test partition, Z = -4.04, p < 0.0001) that remains even after purging both income and interest rates from the model. Out-of-sample signed SHAP valuations demonstrate that this residual gap is maintained by structural proxies, most notably loan amount and home ownership status. These empirical findings show that simply blinding an algorithm to sensitive attributes fails to ensure fairness when institutional pricing decisions and behavioral proxy variables collectively reconstruct the omitted signals. We outline the practical implications of these findings for auditing data-centric AI workflows within regulated financial institutions.

</details>


### [46] [FreSH: Frequency-Segmented Hierarchical Multi-Expert Framework for Multivariate Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08207)
*Pingping Liu,Muyao Wang,Zijian Zhang,Tongshun Zhang,Hao Miao,Guorui Xie,Qingliang Li,Qiuzhan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FreSH是一个面向多元时间序列分类（MTSC）的频率分段层次化多专家框架，通过自适应多尺度分析、局部专家专业化与全局上下文建模相结合，配合鲁棒优化目标，在30个UEA基准数据集和真实振动数据上以更小的模型规模和更高的计算效率持续超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTSC方法难以在多尺度时间模式捕捉、细粒度表示学习与类别不平衡等现实约束之间取得平衡，且通常面临表示学习能力与计算效率之间的冲突。

Method: FreSH框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 频率分段策略，实现对时间信号的适应性多尺度分析；(2) 层次化多专家机制，将局部专业化与整体上下文建模有机结合；(3) 自适应融合策略，动态强调输入中最具信息量的成分；(4) 鲁棒优化目标，提升在不同样本难度和类别分布下的学习稳定性。

Result: 在30个UEA基准数据集和真实世界振动数据上的广泛实验表明，FreSH在分类准确率上持续优于现有最先进方法，同时显著降低了模型规模并提升了计算效率。

Conclusion: FreSH通过频率分段与多专家层次化设计，为MTSC提供了一种兼顾表示能力、计算效率与类别不平衡鲁棒性的新范式，验证了多尺度互补建模在时间序列分类中的有效性。

Abstract: Multivariate Time Series Classification (MTSC) demands models that can effectively capture complex temporal patterns across multiple scales while remaining computationally efficient. However, existing approaches generally struggle to reconcile fine-grained representation learning, especially under class imbalance and real-world constraints. In this paper, we present FreSH, a Frequency-Segmented Hierarchical Multi-Expert Framework designed to address these challenges. FreSH introduces a new perspective for MTSC by enabling adaptive, multi-scale analysis of temporal signals, allowing different aspects of the data to be modeled in a complementary and coordinated manner. By combining localized specialization with holistic context modeling, FreSH achieves strong representational capacity without incurring excessive computational overhead. An adaptive fusion strategy further enhances flexibility, enabling the model to dynamically emphasize the most informative components of the input. In addition, we incorporate a more robust optimization objective that improves learning stability across varying sample difficulties and class distributions. Extensive evaluations on 30 UEA benchmark datasets and real-world vibration data demonstrate that FreSH consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in classification accuracy, while substantially reducing model size and efficiency.

</details>


### [47] [Control-Diverse Reinforcement Fine-Tuning: Decoupling the Shared Control Bottleneck of RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08224)
*Binwen Tan,Jingchao Wang,Dengzhe Hou,Lingyu Jiang,Zeyuan Wu,Yunhan Shen,Fangzhou Lin,Kazunori Yamada,Atsushi Koike*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出后训练控制系数（Post-training Control Coefficient）来分离"激活"与"控制"，揭示激活共享但控制任务特化的现象；并据此提出控制多样化强化微调（CD-RFT），通过正则化共享控制瓶颈（Shared Control Bottleneck）改善多任务能力，在Qwen2.5-7B和Llama-3.2-3B上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习后训练的可解释性研究主要关注电路激活的强度与多样性，但基准分数无法揭示模型内部变化，也无法说明有限能力如何在多任务间分配。作者认为"被激活的电路"不一定"控制"后训练奖励增益，因此需要将激活与控制分离，建立更精确的机制性诊断框架。

Method: 借鉴代谢控制分析（MCA），定义后训练控制系数（PCC）衡量组件对奖励增益的控制程度，按任务族组织成控制矩阵并与激活幅度矩阵配对。定义共享控制瓶颈（SCB）刻画跨任务控制集中度，定义激活-控制差距（AC Gap）刻画激活与控制的一致性。提出CD-RFT，在后训练损失中加入SCB正则项，并推导一阶梯度代理以避免flash attention不兼容的二阶自动微分。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B上，CD-RFT实现了最大的控制解耦并优于匹配的GRPO基线（数学、代码、逻辑多任务）。无KL变体在pass@1上领先，带KL惩罚变体在大k的pass@k覆盖率上领先。最坏情况开销低于8%。控制解耦与能力增益可迁移到Llama-3.2-3B。

Conclusion: 共享控制瓶颈既是机制性诊断工具，也是训练正则化器。高度共享的激活可以与任务特化的控制共存，而小差距表明控制坍缩到共享方向并失去任务特化。控制解耦带来多任务能力提升，且效果具有跨模型迁移性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning post-training unlocks complex reasoning in LLMs. Yet benchmark scores reveal only whether a model improved, not what changed inside it, nor how it splits finite capability across tasks. A representative interpretability line attributes the success of RL fine-tuning to stronger and more diverse circuit activation. We challenge this activation-centered account by separating activation from control: an activated circuit need not control the post-training reward gain. Adapting Metabolic Control Analysis, we define the Post-training Control Coefficient to measure component control over reward gain and arrange these coefficients by task family into a control matrix, paired with an activation-magnitude matrix. We call cross-task control concentration the Shared Control Bottleneck and the difference between activation and control concentration the Activation-Control Gap. This reveals that highly shared activations can coexist with task-specific control, while a small gap indicates that control has collapsed onto a shared direction and lost task specificity. To reduce this collapse, we regularize the post-training loss with the Shared Control Bottleneck and propose Control-Diverse Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (CD-RFT). The exact regularizer gradient requires second-order automatic differentiation incompatible with flash attention, so we derive a first-order proxy with worst-case overhead below eight percent. On Qwen2.5-7B, CD-RFT achieves the largest control decoupling and improves multi-task capability over matched GRPO across mathematics, code, and logic. The no-KL variant leads on pass@1, and the KL-penalized variant leads on large-k pass@k coverage that KL otherwise degrades. Together, these results show that the Shared Control Bottleneck is both a mechanistic diagnostic and a training regularizer, and that control decoupling and capability gains transfer to Llama-3.2-3B.

</details>


### [48] [SAGE: SLO-Aware Adaptive Retrieval for Production RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08237)
*Muhammad Faizan Raza,Shuo,Yang,Satish Mahadevan Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE，一种基于学习的SLO感知自适应检索策略，根据查询难度动态选择检索passage数量k，在满足尾部延迟SLO的同时优化检索成本和答案质量，通过离线模仿学习训练，推理开销极低，并展现出跨数据集和跨LLM的强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的RAG系统需要在尾部延迟和基础设施成本方面满足严格的SLO，但标准检索流水线使用固定的检索预算，无法区分查询难度，导致简单查询过度检索、困难查询检索不足，迫使运维人员在答案质量与SLO合规之间做出取舍。

Method: SAGE采用学习式自适应检索策略：使用初始检索得到的轻量级特征（如分数分布、排名差距、词汇信号）作为输入，通过离线模仿学习从一个近似最优延迟-质量权衡的oracle进行训练，推理时动态决定每个查询的k值，不增加任何LLM调用且开销极小。

Result: 在Natural Questions上，5秒P95延迟SLO下SAGE达到95%的SLO合规率（最佳静态基线k=20仅30%），P95延迟降低36%，检索成本降低51%，仅损失2个百分点EM；单一策略可泛化到HotpotQA、UnSeenTimeQA及四个LLM家族（Llama、Qwen、Mistral、Gemma），一致获得+45-52点的SLO改进且无质量下降。

Conclusion: SAGE证明了基于查询难度的动态检索预算分配是可行且高效的，能够在保证答案质量的前提下显著提升SLO合规率和降低检索成本，且具备良好的跨数据集与跨模型泛化能力，为生产环境RAG系统的检索策略设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems in production operate under strict service level objectives (SLOs) on tail latency and infrastructure cost. However, standard retrieval pipelines rely on fixed retrieval budgets that ignore query difficulty, over-retrieving for easy queries and under-serving hard ones, forcing operators to trade answer quality against SLO compliance. This paper proposes SAGE, a learned SLO-aware adaptive retrieval policy that dynamically selects the number of passages k per query. SAGE uses lightweight features derived from initial retrieval (e.g., score distributions, rank gaps, lexical signals) and is trained offline via imitation learning from an oracle that approximates optimal latency-quality trade-offs. At inference, it adds no LLM calls and minimal overhead. On Natural Questions, under a 5s P95 latency SLO, SAGE achieves 95% SLO compliance versus 30% for the best static baseline (k=20), reduces P95 latency by 36% and retrieval cost by 51% with only 2 percentage points Exact Match (EM) loss. A single policy trained on Natural Questions generalizes across HotpotQA, UnSeenTimeQA, and four LLM families (Llama, Qwen, Mistral, Gemma), consistently yielding +45-52 point SLO improvements without quality degradation.

</details>


### [49] [The Replay Gap: Static Evaluation of Model Switching in LLM Agents Scores the Wrong World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08239)
*Ashritha Gonuguntla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出当前基于日志重放的agentic路由器评估方法存在根本缺陷：重放假设模型替换后轨迹其余部分不受影响，但通过分支式rollout实验证明这一假设不成立。实验显示模型交换后75%以上的早期分支在首个动作即发生偏离，重放评估器无法预测任何与成功相关的关键调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM路由器在多步agents中按步骤应用以选择最经济的模型，但现有的评估方式沿用了单轮路由器的做法——重放日志轨迹并替换另一模型的记录输出，假设其余轨迹不受影响。作者质疑这一假设在代理式（多步）场景中是否成立。

Method: 使用分支式rollout（branching rollouts）进行受控实验：在SWE-bench实时agents轨迹的受控点进行分支，重建环境后用不同模型继续每个分支，并与同模型控制分支对比以隔离采样和重放噪声。共进行六组成对运行（约900个rollouts），同时引入了log-stitching重放评估器作为对比基线，并审计了温度0"确定性"在不同量化配置（FP8 vs AWQ）下的差异。

Result: 模型交换超出匹配的控制基线+0.25到+0.66归一化编辑距离（多重校正CI排除零），重写了61-94%的分支后动作；74-77%的早期交换在第一分支后动作即发散（控制组仅6-35%），只有3%的重放状态有效。发散程度随分支深度双向递减。5次结果翻转全部出现在交换组，控制组359个分支零翻转。重放评估器在成功相关调用上全部误判，预测补丁相似度仅0.00-0.11。

Conclusion: 基于重放的评测系统在agents路由场景中评估的是错误的世界。研究发布了完整的测试框架和全部轨迹数据供社区使用。

Abstract: LLM routers promise efficiency by matching each request to the cheapest adequate model, and are increasingly applied per step inside multi-step agents. Yet agentic routers are evaluated like single-turn routers: by replaying logged trajectories and substituting another model's recorded outputs, assuming the rest of the trajectory is unaffected. We test this assumption with branching rollouts: we fork live SWE-bench agent trajectories at controlled points, rebuild the environment, continue each fork with a different model, and compare against same-model control forks that isolate sampling and replay noise. Across six paired runs (~900 rollouts), swaps exceed their matched control floors by +0.25 to +0.66 normalized edit distance (multiplicity-corrected CIs exclude zero), rewriting 61-94% of post-fork actions; 74-77% of early swaps diverge at the first post-fork action, versus 6-35% of controls, leaving only 3% of replayed states valid. Divergence decreases with fork depth in both directions. All five outcome flips we observe occur in swap arms, upgrades rescuing unsolved instances and a downgrade losing the sole solve, and zero occur across 359 control forks. Scoring these same swaps with a log-stitching replay evaluator, replay mispredicts every success-relevant outcome call and predicts patches with 0.00-0.11 similarity to reality. Auditing the noise floor, temperature-0 "determinism" is configuration-dependent: FP8-served controls diverge on over 90% of forks while AWQ-served ones remain near-identical; and under tight budgets the stronger model more often exhausts its steps without submitting. Replay-based benchmarks score the wrong world for agentic routing; we release our harness and all trajectories.

</details>


### [50] [Learning from Environmental Feedback: Credit Assignment across Multiple Timescales for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08255)
*Yifu Huo,Shunjie Xing,Chenglong Wang,Peinan Feng,Qiaozhi He,Yan Ding,Anxiang Ma,Yuxin Gao,Tongran Liu,Tong Xiao,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EFCA（Environmental Feedback-based Credit Assignment），一种多时间尺度的信用分配方法，通过利用环境交互中自然生成的过程信息（如交互历史）来为智能体RL的长时程任务提供更细粒度的反馈监督。EFCA结合短期反馈信号和中期状态历史信号，通过返回重加权机制集成，在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上显著提升了任务成功率和质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体强化学习（Agentic RL）在真实环境中常面临奖励延迟且稀疏的问题。信用分配是解决该问题的关键途径，但现有方法忽略了环境交互过程中自然产生的丰富过程信息（如交互历史），这些信息本可以为单个动作的贡献识别提供有价值的监督信号。

Method: EFCA是一种多时间尺度的信用分配方法，包含三个层次：1）长期结果信号（long-term outcome signal）作为最终目标监督；2）短期反馈信号（short-term feedback signal），捕捉当前动作的即时效果；3）中期状态历史信号（medium-term state-history signal），从近期交互中识别无效模式。后两个信号直接从环境反馈中提取，并通过返回重加权（return reweighting）机制进行集成。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop两个基准上的实验表明，EFCA相比强基线方法在任务成功率和任务质量两个指标上均实现了一致的提升，验证了环境接地（environment-grounded）的多时间尺度信用分配在长时程智能体RL中的有效性。

Conclusion: 环境交互过程中自然产生的过程信息（交互历史）是信用分配中极具价值的监督来源。通过多时间尺度的环境接地信号设计，EFCA能够有效缓解长时程智能体RL中的稀疏奖励问题，为后续研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) often suffers from delayed and sparse rewards in real-world environments. A promising solution to this challenge is credit assignment, which aims to decompose trajectory-level rewards and provide more fine-grained supervision for intermediate decisions. However, existing credit assignment approaches ignore the rich process information naturally generated during environment interaction, e.g., interaction history. We argue that such information provides valuable supervision for identifying the contribution of individual actions. To this end, we propose Environmental Feedback-based Credit Assignment (EFCA), a multi-timescale credit assignment approach for long-horizon agentic RL. EFCA complements the long-term outcome signal with two environment-grounded process signals: a short-term feedback signal that captures the immediate effect of the current action and a medium-term state-history signal that identifies ineffective patterns from recent interactions. Both signals are directly extracted from environment feedback and integrated through a return reweighting mechanism. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop demonstrate that EFCA consistently improves both task success and task quality over strong baselines, highlighting the effectiveness of environment-grounded multi-timescale credit assignment for long-horizon agentic RL.

</details>


### [51] [Opportunity Is Not Realizability: Selection-Valid Diagnostics for Multi-LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08265)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Abu Sa-Adat Mohamed Moon-Im Al Ahsan,Md Najmus Swaqeeb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文批判了Oracle routing（基于逐查询选择来衡量语言模型池潜在收益）诊断方法的两个缺陷：一是在相同样本上选出的最佳固定模型进行对比会使配对推断失效；二是全信息oracle看到了任何可部署路由器都观察不到的结局。作者分离了三个估计目标（结局oracle机会、基于预回答信号的贝叶斯最优增益、可学习路由器的留出增益），并证明了能够经受选择偏差的置信区间，包括信号信息夹逼不等式和子模互补覆盖的(1-1/e)贪心保证。在六个家族的八个检查点、四个基准测试上，选择有效区间证明总体oracle差距为9.7-30.7个点，但最强的可部署prompt路由器仅恢复其中7.5%-14.4%，同时最优策略的联合置信区间下限为零。结论：oracle机会的可实现份额很小但可证明，强路由器优于最佳固定模型，但大部分差距依然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Oracle routing诊断方法存在两个根本缺陷：首先，用同一组样本上选出的最佳固定模型作为基线会导致配对推断失去有效性；其次，全信息oracle所见的结局信息（如金标答案）是任何部署场景下路由器都无法获得的。因此需要更科学的估计框架来准确衡量语言模型池的路由潜力。

Method: 本文提出了三重估计目标分离框架——结局oracle机会（全信息上限）、贝叶斯最优路由增益（基于合法预回答信号）、以及可学习路由器的留出增益。方法论上包含三个核心工具：选择有效置信区间（在选择最佳固定模型或路由器家族最佳成员后仍保持有效）、信号信息夹逼不等式（用预回答信号夹逼oracle机会）、以及基于子模互补覆盖的(1-1/e)贪心池构建保证。

Result: 在六个模型家族、八个检查点、四个基准测试上：总体oracle差距被验证为9.7-30.7个点（所有任务上置信区间下限为正），但最强的可部署prompt路由器只能恢复其中7.5%-14.4%。更关键的是，当对十一种测试策略做同时推断时，最佳策略的置信区间下限为零，说明在统计意义上无法证明显著优于基线。

Conclusion: oracle路由机会中现实可实现的份额很小，但这一结论是可被严格验证的。强路由器确实能超越最佳固定模型，然而绝大多数oracle差距在实践上难以捕获，这对LLM池路由的期望设定了更现实的边界。

Abstract: Oracle routing measures how much a pool of language models could gain from per-query selection, but the diagnostic has two flaws: testing against a best fixed model selected on the same examples invalidates paired inference, and a full-information oracle sees outcomes no deployable router observes. We separate three estimands (outcome-oracle opportunity, the Bayes-optimal gain from a declared pre-answer signal, and the held-out gain of a learned router) and prove selection-valid confidence intervals that survive choosing the best fixed model or the best member of a router family, a signal-information sandwich, and a $(1-1/e)$ greedy guarantee for building compact pools from submodular complementary coverage. On eight checkpoints from six families over four benchmarks, selection-valid intervals certify a population oracle gap of $9.7$--$30.7$ points on every task, yet the strongest deployable prompt router recovers only $7.5$--$14.4\%$ of it, and the simultaneous interval for the best of eleven tested policies has lower limit zero throughout. The realizable share of oracle opportunity is small and certifiable: strong routers beat the best fixed model, and most of the gap remains.

</details>


### [52] [Stateful CARS: Exact Cross-History Reuse for Policy-Constrained LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08282)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Md Najmus Swaqeeb,Abu Sa-Adat Mohamed Moon-Im Al Ahsan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"Stateful CARS"方法，通过冻结状态-延续模式的银行并从提案分布中移除包含匹配抽象状态认证延续的轨迹，实现硬状态验证器条件下语言模型代理的精确采样。基于残差Doob变换实现精确采样，并证明了模式健全性、自适应精确性、独立同分布输出、几乎必然终止等理论性质。实验表明其解析分布与有效条件分布在有效性概率6×10^-8下精确匹配到10^-16，但相比官方CARS无系统优势，跨历史转移仅在内部消融中有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用的语言模型代理面临硬状态约束，这些约束的含义会随观测和先前行变化而变化。需要在满足严格状态验证器条件下进行精确采样，同时跨历史重用无效性证书。现有方法(CARS)基于观测键控，无法充分处理状态依赖的约束场景。

Method: Stateful CARS在每次尝试中冻结一组健全的状态-延续模式，并移除所有包含匹配抽象状态处被认证延续的轨迹。通过精确的残差Doob变换从生成的提案中采样。给出了可检查的未来有效性互模拟条件，并定义了基于可达完整历史乘积状态数的计算表征。

Result: 在可枚举工作流上，解析定律与有效条件分布在有效性概率6×10^-8时匹配精度达10^-16，而状态感知局部解码误差达0.97。负面比较：观测键控官方CARS在采样步骤上更优(Root/Stateful比0.942 [0.934,0.951])，Qwen比较结果为空(null, 0.99 [0.90,1.08])。跨历史转移仅在内部匹配键消融中有帮助(1.27×)。

Conclusion: 证据支持Stateful CARS实现了精确的模式诱导条件化，但在系统架构优势上不优于官方CARS。对历史依赖语言模型，可达完整历史乘积状态数可能为指数级，因此该方法不声称具有通用的有限trie可扩展性。

Abstract: Tool-using language-model agents face constraints whose meaning changes with observations and prior actions. We study exact sampling from the model distribution conditioned on a hard stateful validator while reusing invalidity certificates across histories. Stateful CARS freezes a bank of sound state--continuation schemas within each attempt and removes every trajectory containing a certified continuation at a matching abstract state. An exact residual Doob transform samples from the resulting proposal. We give a checkable future-validity bisimulation condition, prove schema soundness, adaptive exactness, i.i.d.\ outputs, almost-sure termination, monotone acceptance, and compression invariance, and characterize computation by the number of reachable full-history product states. This number can be exponential for a history-dependent language model; the evaluated method therefore makes no generic finite-trie scalability claim. On enumerable workflows, its analytic law matches the valid conditional to $10^{-16}$ at validity probability $6\times10^{-8}$, whereas state-aware local decoding can be $0.97$ away. A matched comparison is negative: observation-keyed official CARS is cheaper in sampler steps (root/Stateful ratio $0.942$ $[0.934,0.951]$), and the Qwen comparison is null ($0.99$ $[0.90,1.08]$). Cross-history transfer helps only in an internal matched-key ablation ($1.27\times$). Thus the evidence supports exact schema-induced conditioning, not a systems advantage over CARS.

</details>


### [53] [Causal State-Space Model for Causal Inference: Estimating Longitudinal Individual Treatment Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08288)
*Abisoye Abidakun,Mingjun Zhong,Georgios Leontidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对纵向观测数据中反事实结局估计的"域混淆"（domain confusion）与结果预测之间的互信息冲突问题，提出两种互补模型：CSSD利用选择性状态空间模型配合并行多步解码器消除累积滚动误差；CSSPD进一步引入对比预测编码和局部信息最大化来恢复域混淆破坏的局部协变量信息。实验证明CSSPD在MIMIC-III和癌症模拟数据上均优于Causal Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实时序推断方法依赖域混淆（对抗训练使表征对治疗分配不变），但这种不变性会压制与治疗相关的协变量信号，而这类信号对准确结果预测至关重要，形成互信息冲突。该冲突此前未被形式化，限制了模型性能上限。

Method: 1) 通过Jensen-Shannon散度上界形式化反事实预测误差与互信息冲突的关系；2) CSSD：采用选择性状态空间模型（SSM）搭配并行多步直接解码器，单次前向传播同时输出所有预测时域，消除累积生成误差；3) CSSPD：在CSSD基础上增加对比预测编码（CPC）和局部信息最大化（Local Information Maximisation），强化平衡表征的时间可预测性并恢复被域混淆破坏的局部协变量信息。

Result: 在MIMIC-III上，CSSPD在所有时域tau>=2均以O(T)编码器成本取得比Causal Transformer更低的RMSE（2步提升0.02，6步提升0.07）。在癌症模拟数据的混杂强度gamma∈{0,1,2,3,4}下，CSSPD在gamma<=3时优于CT（边际25.9%–37.0%），而CSSD取得最低总体平均RMSE（比CT降低12.7%），验证了互信息冲突分析的结论。

Conclusion: 本文首次形式化了纵向反事实推断中"平衡表征-结果预测"之间的互信息冲突，并提出了通过互补的预测性和信息论训练目标的结构化解方案。CSSD/CSSPD在真实和模拟数据上均验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Estimating counterfactual outcomes over time from longitudinal observational data is central to clinical decision support. Existing methods rely on domain confusion -- adversarial training that renders representations invariant to treatment assignment -- yet this invariance creates a mutual information conflict: it suppresses treatment-correlated covariate signals necessary for accurate outcome prediction. We formalise this tension via a Jensen-Shannon divergence bound on counterfactual prediction error and develop two complementary models. CSSD (Causal State-Space model with Direct decoder) adapts selective State Space Models with a parallel multi-step decoder that eliminates accumulated rollout error by producing all prediction horizons simultaneously in a single forward pass. CSSPD (Causal State-Space model with Predictive regularisation and Direct decoder) augments CSSD with Contrastive Predictive Coding and Local Information Maximisation to reinforce temporal predictability in the balancing representation and recover local covariate information destroyed by domain confusion. On MIMIC-III, CSSPD achieves lower counterfactual RMSE than the Causal Transformer at every horizon tau >= 2 at O(T) encoder cost, with gains from 0.02 (2-step) to 0.07 (6-step). On Cancer Simulation across confounding strengths gamma in {0,1,2,3,4}, CSSPD outperforms CT at gamma <= 3 (margins 25.9%--37.0%), and CSSD achieves the lowest overall average RMSE (12.7% reduction over CT), confirming the MI conflict analysis. To our knowledge, this is the first work to formalise the balancing-prediction MI conflict and propose a structured resolution through complementary predictive and information-theoretic training objectives.

</details>


### [54] [A Controlled Study of Feature-Based Knowledge Distillation Across Student Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08294)
*Abhinand Balachandran,Praveen Prashant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在CIFAR-100上系统研究了特征蒸馏方法对不同规模学生网络的影响。使用ResNet-50教师，比较了注意力迁移和FitNets两种特征蒸馏方法与匹配的logit蒸馏基线，发现logit蒸馏稳定提升所有学生；注意力迁移效果与模型大小无明确关系；FitNets在15次配对实验中均不如logit蒸馏，且固定辅助系数在不同学生上产生不同梯度尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏中，基于特征的蒸馏方法要求学生匹配教师中间层表示，但这种额外的约束可能对不同规模的学生模型产生不同影响。现有研究缺乏对特征蒸馏方法在不同学生架构/规模上行为一致性的系统分析，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在CIFAR-100数据集上，以ResNet-50为教师，使用宽度可控的CustomResNet家族（宽度扫描+不同深度对照）和MobileNetV2（跨架构比较）作为学生。每个学生模型分别评估每种特征蒸馏方法，并与使用相同教师、优化器设置、训练计划和随机种子的匹配logit-KD基线对比，重复多次种子。

Result: 1) Logit KD在所有学生上均优于从头训练基线；2) 注意力迁移（Attention Transfer）在CustomResNet家族内部与模型大小无明确关系，平均效应在该家族中为负、在MobileNetV2上为正；3) FitNets在全部15次配对运行中均低于logit KD，且在恒定深度宽度扫描中其差距随宽度增大而增大，但不同深度的w=48学生不遵循该趋势；4) 相同辅助系数在不同学生上产生不同梯度尺度，说明固定系数无法提供统一的训练条件。

Conclusion: 特征蒸馏方法的效果强烈依赖于学生架构和规模，不存在普适性的最优选择。logit蒸馏是更稳健的基线，而特征蒸馏方法的辅助系数需要针对具体学生架构进行调整，因为固定系数无法保证一致的训练条件。研究者在使用特征蒸馏时需要谨慎评估其与学生模型的匹配度。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation trains a smaller student to match the outputs of a larger teacher. Feature-based methods also align intermediate representations, but this extra constraint may affect students differently. We study this question on CIFAR-100 using a ResNet-50 teacher, a width-controlled CustomResNet family and MobileNetV2 as a cross-design comparison. For each student, we evaluate each feature method against a matched logit-KD run using the same teacher, optimizer settings, training schedule and seed. We repeat the main comparisons across multiple seeds.
  Logit KD improved every tested student over its scratch baseline. Attention Transfer showed no clear relationship with size inside the CustomResNet family, but its average effect was negative for that family and positive for MobileNetV2. FitNets was below logit KD in all 15 paired runs. Within the constant-depth width sweep, its gap increased for wider students, although the different-depth w=48 student did not follow this trend. Finally, the same auxiliary coefficient produced different gradient scales across students, showing that a fixed coefficient does not create a uniform training condition.

</details>


### [55] [Machine-Learning-Based Diagnostic Framework for Passive Ultrasonic Detection of Railway Wheel Defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08301)
*Aashish Shaju,Steve Southward,Mehdi Ahmadian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种基于机器学习的多类别铁路车轮缺陷诊断框架，利用被动空气耦合超声声发射信号，结合统计特征选择与随机森林分类器，实现了九种健康状态下的车轮缺陷识别，平衡准确率约0.66，宏F1分数0.65。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路车轮缺陷的可靠识别对行车安全和维护至关重要，需要开发非接触、可现场部署的自动化缺陷诊断方法。

Method: 从11个全尺寸铁路轮对（涵盖9种健康状态）收集被动空气耦合超声声发射信号；使用时域和频域特征，通过Kruskal-Wallis统计检验和互信息分析进行特征选择；采用随机森林分类器，并使用分层5折交叉验证评估模型性能。

Result: 模型在9个类别上取得了约0.66的平衡准确率和0.65的宏F1分数；衰减率、峰度、偏度和包络低频功率是最具判别力的特征，紧凑特征子集保留了大部分分类性能。

Conclusion: 研究证明了被动超声传感、统计特征选择与监督机器学习相结合用于非接触式铁路车轮缺陷分类的可行性，为未来现场可部署的检测系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Reliable identification of railway wheel defects is important for safety and maintenance. This study develops a machine-learning-based diagnostic framework for multi-class defect identification using passive air-coupled ultrasonic acoustic emission signals. Data were collected from eleven full-scale railway wheelsets representing nine health states. Time- and frequency-domain features were evaluated using Kruskal-Wallis statistical testing and mutual-information analysis to identify the most discriminative indicators. A Random Forest classifier was then trained using the selected features with stratified 5-fold cross-validation. The model achieved a balanced accuracy of approximately 0.66 and a Macro-F1 score of 0.65 across the nine classes. Decay rate, kurtosis, skewness, and envelope low-frequency power emerged as the most influential features, while a compact subset of features retained most of the classification performance. The results demonstrate the feasibility of combining passive ultrasonic sensing, statistical feature selection, and supervised machine learning for non-contact railway wheel defect classification and provide a foundation for future field-deployable inspection systems.

</details>


### [56] [The Neural Division of Labor: Biologically-Inspired Modular Architectures for Robust Neuromorphic Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08317)
*Maksim Bazhenov,Serafim Grubas,Vakhtang Putkaradze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可分解脉冲神经网络（D-SNN），通过将分类路径结构性地隔离为独立专家模块，消除全局突触纠缠，利用生物启发的推挽损失函数优化，在多个基准数据集上达到与全稠密网络相当的精度，同时大幅减少参数、脉冲率和突触操作，并具备抗灾难性遗忘和决策透明的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物神经系统通过区室化架构实现高效性和鲁棒性，而现代人工神经网络依赖全局纠缠结构，导致决策逻辑模糊且存在灾难性遗忘问题。作者希望借鉴生物系统的模块化设计来解决这些痛点。

Method: 提出D-SNN架构，将分类通路结构性地隔离为独立专家（专家之间无全局突触纠缠），采用生物启发的推挽（push-pull）损失函数进行优化训练。

Result: 在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST和CIFAR-10/100上达到与全稠密网络相当的精度；参数数量减少一个数量级；脉冲率和突触操作降低数个数量级；物理切断专家连接可天然抵抗灾难性遗忘；隔离通路产生可审计的神经信号，提升决策透明度。

Conclusion: 这种仿生、可验证的模块化架构为在资源受限的边缘环境中部署确定性神经形态智能提供了高效基础。

Abstract: Biological neural systems achieve high efficiency and robustness through compartmentalized architectures. In contrast, modern artificial neural networks rely on globally entangled structures, which obscure decision logic and suffer from catastrophic forgetting. Here, we report a Decomposable Spiking Neural Network (D-SNN) that eliminates global synaptic entanglement by structurally isolating classification pathways into independent experts. Optimized via a bio-inspired push-pull loss function, the D-SNN achieves competitive accuracies on MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and CIFAR-10/100 benchmarks. This modular approach matches the performance of fully dense networks while utilizing an order of magnitude fewer parameters. In addition, our networks operate with up to several orders of magnitude lower firing rates and fewer synaptic operations. Furthermore, physically severing connections between experts provides inherent protection against catastrophic forgetting during sequential learning. Crucially, these isolated pathways generate auditable neural signals, increasing decision transparency. This biomimetic, verifiable architecture establishes an efficient foundation for deploying deterministic neuromorphic intelligence in resource-constrained edge environments.

</details>


### [57] [Spatial Heterogeneity-Aware Multi-Hazard Susceptibility and Risk Mapping at Regional Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08321)
*Aswathi Mundayatt,Siddharth Anil,Hitanshu Seth,Jaya Sreevalsan-Nair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个空间异质性感知框架，用于印度喀拉拉邦和尼泊尔的洪水与滑坡易发性及相对风险制图。框架组合15 km × 15 km网格与区域情境分区，比较了邻近门控跨区训练（S1）与生态门控区内约束训练（S2）两种策略。S1在准确率、精确率、召回率、F1分数、AUC-ROC和PR-AUC上均优于S2，尤其在尼泊尔洪水易发性中AUC-ROC从0.728提升至0.886；S2则获得更低的Brier分数并保留区域特定差异。两种策略均成功复现洪水低地与滑坡高地格局，但易发性和风险类别存在差异，风险图一致性在喀拉拉为0.521、尼泊尔为0.711。研究表明跨区学习增强区域判别力，而区内约束保留环境差异，建议二者整合使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洪水与滑坡常共同发生，但二者与环境控制因素的关系存在空间变异性。现有研究缺乏能够同时捕捉这种空间异质性并准确绘制联合易发性和相对风险图的系统框架，因此需要开发一个空间异质性感知的方法来应对这一挑战。

Method: 研究采用15 km × 15 km网格与区域情境分区相结合的方式，构建了两种训练策略：S1（邻近门控跨区训练）允许地理上相近的模型跨情境边界分配，而S2（生态门控区内约束训练）将模型开发与分配限制在同一分区内。每种灾害使用随机森林模型和策略特定预测变量集，在空间持有的测试样本上评估。易发性表面与CRITIC加权的暴露度和脆弱性指数整合，生成灾害特定及九类双变量相对风险图。

Result: S1在两个区域两种灾害的所有主要评估指标上均高于S2，最大差异出现在尼泊尔洪水易发性上（AUC-ROC从0.728升至0.886，PR-AUC从0.512升至0.823）。S2在尼泊尔两种灾害上取得更低Brier分数，并保留了分区特定的预测变量选择、SHAP排序和响应模式差异。两种策略都复现了洪水低地与滑坡高地格局，但风险图一致性在喀拉拉为0.521、尼泊尔为0.711，且分配不一致性在所有比较中均超过数量不一致性；易发性与风险的对应关系低于0.350，表明暴露度和脆弱性改变了优先位置。

Conclusion: 跨区学习提升了区域判别能力，而区内约束学习保留了环境差异，两种策略各有优势且互补，支持将其整合用于更鲁棒的灾害易发性与风险评估。

Abstract: Floods and landslides often co-occur, but their relationships with environmental controls vary spatially. This study develops a spatial heterogeneity-aware framework for flood-landslide susceptibility and relative-risk mapping in Kerala, India, and Nepal. It combines 15 km x 15 km grid cells with region-specific contextual zones and compares proximity-gated cross-zone training (S1) and ecology-gated zone-constrained training (S2). S1 permits geographically nearby models to be assigned across contextual boundaries, whereas S2 restricts model development and assignment to the same zone. Random Forest models for each hazard use strategy-specific predictor sets and are evaluated on spatially held-out test samples. Susceptibility surfaces are integrated with CRITIC-weighted exposure and vulnerability indices to produce hazard-specific and nine-class bivariate relative-risk maps. S1 achieved higher mean accuracy, precision, recall, F1-score, AUC-ROC, and PR-AUC for both hazards and regions. The largest difference occurred for Nepal flood susceptibility, where AUC-ROC increased from 0.728 under S2 to 0.886 under S1 and PR-AUC from 0.512 to 0.823. S2 produced lower Brier scores for both Nepal hazards and retained zone-specific differences in predictor selection, SHAP rankings, and response patterns, particularly in Kerala. Both strategies reproduced flood-prone lowland and landslide-prone upland patterns but differed in susceptibility and risk classes. Bivariate risk-map agreement was 0.521 in Kerala and 0.711 in Nepal, with allocation disagreement exceeding quantity disagreement in all S1-S2 comparisons. Susceptibility-to-risk correspondence remained below 0.350, showing that exposure and vulnerability changed priority locations. Overall, cross-zone learning strengthens regional discrimination, while zone-constrained learning preserves environmental differences, supporting their integration.

</details>


### [58] [PRISM: A Predictive Protocol for Permutation Optimization via Landscape Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08344)
*Blessings Mambwe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRISM是一种预测性协议，在优化之前通过廉价的景观诊断（如一步移动自相关和适应度-距离相关性）来预测排列优化问题的搜索策略是否有效、应选用何种变异算子，并判断何时结构化搜索优于随机采样或简单替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排列优化问题中，系统组件固定但顺序影响性能，目前缺乏在优化开始前判断搜索策略有效性的方法。

Method: 提出PRISM协议，使用低成本景观诊断（一步移动自相关、适应度-距离相关性）来预测变异算子的有用性、判断结构化搜索是否优于随机采样、以及检测搜索收益有限的场景。

Result: 在合成排列景观、神经架构基准、科学机器学习管线和LLM指令排序等多个场景中，PRISM能做出可检验的预测。穷举指令排序实验显示排列本身引起显著性能变化；跨模型实验显示排序结构可跨模型家族和任务难度迁移；且指令排序在提示词优化之后仍具重要性，说明内容优化与排序优化互补。

Conclusion: PRISM不是普适更优的优化器，而是用于判断何时排列搜索有用、该用哪种表示和算子、以及何时简单替代方案更合适的框架。

Abstract: Permutation optimization arises whenever the components of a system are fixed but their ordering affects performance. We introduce PRISM, a predictive protocol for permutation optimization that measures a fitness landscape before selecting a search strategy. PRISM uses inexpensive landscape diagnostics, including one-step move autocorrelation and fitness-distance correlation, to predict useful mutation operators, identify when structured search is likely to outperform random sampling, and detect regimes in which search provides little advantage. Across synthetic permutation landscapes, neural architecture benchmarks, scientific machine learning pipelines, and large-language-model instruction ordering, the protocol makes testable predictions about search behavior before optimization begins. Exhaustive instruction-ordering experiments reveal substantial performance variation induced solely by permutation, while cross-model experiments show that useful ordering structure can transfer across model families and task difficulty. Additional experiments demonstrate that instruction ordering remains consequential after prompt wording is optimized, indicating that content optimization and ordering optimization are complementary. The results position PRISM not as a universally superior optimizer, but as a framework for determining when permutation search is useful, which representation and operator should be used, and when simpler alternatives are preferable.

</details>


### [59] [Correlation flow governs learning at criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08350)
*Andrea Combette,Nelly Pustelnik,Antoine Venaille*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文结合均值场理论和随机矩阵理论，建立了深度神经网络中相关传播与神经正切核（NTK）之间的直接联系。研究表明，在无限宽且无限深网络的序贯极限下，相关传播仅在权重-偏置方差平面上的单一临界点才能到达无穷深度。在该临界点，端到端雅可比矩阵随深度代数式地衰减，并由此证明NTK在无穷深度下与输出相关精确成正比。正交初始化比高斯初始化更有效地抑制有限尺寸修正，因此在临界性条件下对深度学习渐近动力学起核心作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络的初始化决定了信息和梯度能否在深度方向上传播，但缺乏一个统一的理论将这些性质与学习动力学联系起来。作者希望建立相关传播与NTK之间的直接理论桥梁，从而将信息传播性质与学习动力学统一在一个框架下，并厘清不同初始化系综（高斯和正交）在该极限下的作用。

Method: 结合均值场理论与随机矩阵理论，在无限宽且无限深网络的序贯极限下分析神经网络的初始化动力学。具体地：(1) 在权重-偏置方差平面中定位相关传播可达无穷深度的临界点；(2) 分析该临界点处端到端雅可比矩阵随深度的衰减行为；(3) 利用该衰减性质严格证明NTK与输出相关在无穷深度时成比例；(4) 对比高斯初始化与正交初始化下的有限尺寸修正行为并给出定量预测。

Result: 在无限深极限下，相关传播仅在权重-偏置方差平面中的一个临界点可行；该点处端到端雅可比矩阵以代数形式随深度衰减，从而证明NTK在无穷深度处精确正比于输出相关。正交初始化相对高斯初始化可抑制前导阶的有限尺寸修正。这些理论预测在有限宽、有限深的网络上得到了定量验证。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了信息传播与学习动力学之间的等价关系，证明了在临界性条件下，正交初始化在控制深度学习的渐近动力学中扮演核心角色，为该领域的初始化理论和动力学分析提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: The initialisation of deep neural networks determines whether information and gradients can propagate across depth, yet a unified theory connecting these properties to learning dynamics remains elusive. Combining mean-field theory and random matrix theory, we establish a direct link between correlation propagation and the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) that governs learning in the sequential limit of infinitely wide, infinitely deep networks. Correlation propagation to infinite depth is possible only at a single critical point in the weight-bias variance plane. At this point, we show that the end-to-end Jacobian vanishes algebraically with depth, and use this to prove that the NTK becomes exactly proportional to the output correlation at infinite depth. This equivalence between information propagation and learning dynamics had not yet been noticed. We further show that orthogonal initialisation suppresses the leading finite-size corrections present under Gaussian initialisation, clarifying the respective roles of the two initialisation ensembles in this limit. These theoretical predictions are validated quantitatively on finite-width, finite-depth networks. Together, these results demonstrate that orthogonal initialisation at criticality plays a central role in controlling the asymptotic dynamics of deep learning.

</details>


### [60] [Unimodality-Promoting Regularized Learning for Ordinal Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08359)
*Ryoya Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对序数回归中的单峰性促进正则化学习（UPRL）方法进行了深入研究，发现先前UPRL方法在促进预测条件概率分布（CPD）更接近单峰的同时，还会导致其尺度变大（即更平滑、确定性更低），从而引入与尺度相关的偏差。作者提出了更严格体现UPRL思想并能规避尺度偏差的新方法，并通过实验验证了单峰性促进确实能提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界中的序数数据往往具有条件概率分布（CPD）的单峰性特征，利用这一特性（即UPRL）有望提升预测性能，尤其在小样本训练数据下。然而先前UPRL方法在促进单峰性的同时引入了尺度相关的偏差，作者旨在厘清并解决这一问题。

Method: 首先从理论上分析并指出先前UPRL方法在促进CPD更接近单峰的同时也会使其尺度增大（即更平滑、确定性更低）；随后提出一种更严格反映UPRL思想的新方法，该方法能规避尺度相关的偏差；最后通过实验对比验证方法的有效性。

Result: 实验对比表明单峰性促进确实有助于提升预测性能。此外，提出的UPRL方法在小规模数据或更大训练集上相比先前方法表现更好，且这种实验观察可用是否存在意外尺度相关偏差来解释。

Conclusion: 先前UPRL方法存在尺度相关偏差问题，本文提出的新方法能更严格地执行单峰性促进并规避这一偏差，从而在多种数据条件下取得更优的预测性能，同时提供了理论层面的分析解释。

Abstract: Ordinal regression, also called ordinal classification, is classification of ordinal data, in which the underlying target variable is categorical and considered to have a natural ordinal relation. Previous works have indicated that, in many real-world ordinal data, the conditional probability distribution (CPD) of the target variable given a value of the explanatory variable would be unimodal in a large domain of the explanatory variable and close to be unimodal even in a remaining domain. Therefore, unimodality-promoting regularized learning (UPRL), which promotes a predicted CPD closer to be unimodal with the aim of decreasing a prediction variance without inducing much bias for ordinal data of the unimodality, is promising to improve the prediction performance especially with small-size training data. In this study, we show that previous UPRL methods promote a predicted CPD to not only become closer to be unimodal but also have a larger scale (in other words, be smoother or less-confident). Therefore, we develop a novel method that more strictly reflects the idea of UPRL and evades a scale-related bias, and verify through experimental comparison that the unimodality-promotion indeed contributes to improve the prediction performance. Additionally, while our proposed UPRL method could perform better for smaller-scale data or with larger-size training data compared to a previous UPRL method, our analysis explains this experimental observation in terms of the presence or absence of an unexpected scale-related bias.

</details>


### [61] [Exact Rank and Convex Calibration Dimension Lower Bounds for the Multi-Label F1 Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08399)
*Mingyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文精确定位了多标签分类中实例级F1度量的损失矩阵的秩为s²-s+2，并通过直接分析其贝叶斯几何结构，证明了凸校准替代损失的维度为Θ(s²)，限定了凸校准替代的维度增长阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多标签分类中F1度量是核心性能指标，其对应的损失矩阵此前仅有上界刻画（s²+1坐标仿射表示及二次维凸校准替代）。确定精确秩并厘清凸校准替代的真实维度需求，对于设计高效且可扩展的替代损失函数至关重要。

Method: 论文从两个方面入手：(1) 通过将非空得分矩阵分解为子集-关联矩阵与正定柯西矩阵的乘积，精确证明F1得分矩阵、移位损失矩阵、未移位损失矩阵的秩均为s²-s+2，列仿射维数为s²-s+1；(2) 直接分析F1的贝叶斯几何，构造使固定核心标签集的所有超集均为贝叶斯最优的分布，利用可行子空间下界推导凸校准维度下界，最终得到CCdim=Θ(s²)。

Result: 精确确定了F1相关矩阵的秩为s²-s+2，列仿射维数为s²-s+1；证明了凸校准替代损失维度满足CCdim(L^F1)≥(2/(3√3)-o(1))s²，结合已有的二次上界，得出CCdim(L^F1)=Θ(s²)。

Conclusion: F1度量的凸校准替代损失维度本质上是s的二次阶，精确秩的确定并不能直接给出校准维度的下界，需要结合贝叶斯几何分析。这一结果明确指出了F1凸校准替代的维度增长趋势，为设计高效的替代损失算法提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The instance-wise $F_1$ measure is a central performance measure for multi-label classification. For a problem with $s$ labels, it defines a $2^s\times 2^s$ loss matrix. Previous work exhibited $s^2+1$-coordinate affine and shifted low-rank representations and used them to construct quadratic-dimensional convex calibrated surrogates. We determine the exact rank. Under the convention $F_1(\varnothing,\varnothing)=1$, the $F_1$ score matrix, the shifted loss matrix, and the unshifted loss matrix all have rank $s^2-s+2$, while the column-affine dimension of the loss is $s^2-s+1$. The proof factors the nonempty score matrix through subset-incidence matrices and a positive-definite Cauchy matrix.
  Exact rank does not, by itself, lower-bound the dimension of an arbitrary convex calibrated surrogate. We therefore analyze the Bayes geometry of $F_1$ directly. We construct a distribution for which precisely all supersets of a fixed core label set are Bayes optimal, and show that the corresponding active loss columns, restricted to the witness support, have affine dimension $hn$, where $n=s-\lfloor s/3\rfloor$ and $h=\lceil(s\lfloor s/3\rfloor)^{1/2}\rceil-1$. Applying the feasible-subspace lower bound for convex calibration dimension gives \[
  \operatorname{CCdim}(L^{F_1})
  \ge \left(\frac{2}{3\sqrt{3}}-o(1)\right)s^2. \] Together with the quadratic upper bound, this establishes $\operatorname{CCdim}(L^{F_1})=Θ(s^2)$.

</details>


### [62] [Rethinking Learning-Based Influence Maximization: Simple Neural Surrogates and Native Discrete Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08406)
*Yiqiao Liao,Parinaz Naghizadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SIMBA是一个面向影响力最大化任务的扩散模型无关框架，通过轻量级神经网络代理与直接离散搜索的配对，替代了传统依赖复杂神经网络架构和连续优化的方法，显著降低求解时间同时提升影响力传播和数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于学习的影响力最大化方法过度依赖复杂的神经架构和对种子表示的连续优化，这带来了高昂的计算开销和初始化噪声问题。作者旨在挑战这一范式，探索是否可以通过轻量代理模型与高效离散搜索的组合来获得更好的性能。

Method: SIMBA包含三个关键组件：1）均匀锚定的节点嵌入，消除初始化噪声并促使学习由图拓扑和扩散模式驱动；2）浅层两层图神经网络代理，用于预测最终感染状态；3）批量多交换模拟退火算法，在无梯度和无连续松弛的条件下探索组合种子空间。

Result: SIMBA大幅缩短了求解时间（time-to-solution），同时实现了更优的影响力传播效果和更高的数据效率。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将计算资源从复杂表示学习转向有效的离散搜索，可以在影响力最大化任务上取得更优的结果，为学习型优化框架的设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Existing learning-based influence maximization frameworks rely heavily on complex neural architectures and continuous optimization over seed representations. We challenge this paradigm with SIMBA, a diffusion-model-agnostic framework pairing a lightweight neural surrogate with direct discrete search. SIMBA introduces three key components: 1) uniformly anchored node embeddings that eliminate initialization noise and encourage learning driven by graph topology and diffusion pattern, 2) a shallow two-layer graph neural network surrogate predicting final infection states, and 3) batched multi-swap simulated annealing that explores combinatorial seed space without gradients or continuous relaxation. By shifting compute from complex representation learning to effective discrete search, SIMBA drastically cuts time-to-solution while achieving superior influence spread and data efficiency. Our code is available at https://github.com/yl489/rethink-IM.

</details>


### [63] [Constrained Learning with Universally Learnable Concept Classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08414)
*Herlock SeyedAbolfazl Rahimi,Spyridon Pougkakiotis,Dionysis Kalogerias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究无限维假设类上非凸约束统计学习问题，建立了对偶算法的通用PACC（约束上近似正确）可学习性理论，保证最优性与约束满足同时达成，并引入Tikhonov复杂度与闭合-实现间隙两个核心概念来刻画最优性与可行性约束的精确可学习性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的近PACC结果无法通过任何数据量消除可行性残差，且非凸无限维设定下约束学习的通用可学习性尚无完整理论。此外，最优性受到泛化（偏好小假设类）与强Lagrangian对偶性（需要可分解性，偏好大假设类）之间的张力制约，如何在两者间取得平衡是核心难题。

Method: 将总体问题置于通用RKHS H_K上，该空间在可分解包中稠密；在逐渐增长的范数球上学习。引入Tikhonov复杂度（达到ε最优Lagrangian水平集所需的最小RKHS范数）并证明其有限性；引入闭合-实现间隙ε★∞刻画H_K从对偶化中恢复可行解的能力。在源条件下给出样本阈值的显式多项式(1/ε)界。通过Lyapunov凸性处理向量测度的强对偶性，并用Rademacher复杂度控制泛化。

Result: 证明了最优值的精确可学习性；当闭合-实现间隙为零（尤其在对偶可微条件下）得到精确可学习性，否则为残差恰为ε★∞的近PACC。证明了无分布自由阈值在无约束特化情形下已不存在，从而说明在大型假设类上通用性是对偶算法的规范框架。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了完全非凸无限维设定下约束学习的统一通用可学习性理论，通过Tikhonov复杂度调和了泛化与对偶性的张力，并以闭合-实现间隙精确刻画了可行性的可学习边界，填补了这一领域的关键理论空白。

Abstract: We study constrained statistical learning over infinite-dimensional hypothesis classes in the fully nonconvex setting, and establish universal PACC learnability of the solutions of dual algorithms: Probably Approximately Correct on Constraints, guaranteeing optimality and constraint satisfaction at once. This strengthens near-PACC results, whose feasibility residual no amount of data can remove. Optimality is caught between generalization, governed by Rademacher complexity and favoring small classes, and strong Lagrangian duality, which rests on Lyapunov convexity for vector measures and needs decomposability, a demand pulling the other way. We reconcile the two by posing the population problem over a universal RKHS $\mathcal{H}_K$, dense in a decomposable envelope, and learning over norm balls of growing radius. This yields the Tikhonov complexity $\mathfrak{T}^{\varepsilon}_{n}$, the least RKHS norm reaching an $\varepsilon$-optimal Lagrangian level set; we prove it finite, obtain exact learnability of the optimal value, and make the sample threshold explicit and polynomial in $1/\varepsilon$ under a source condition. Feasibility is harder: absent convexity the Lagrangian may not attain its infimum, and dual information pins down only an averaged constraint-risk vector, not the risks of any returned predictor. We introduce the closure-realization gap $\varepsilon^\star_\infty$, an index of how well $\mathcal{H}_K$ retrieves feasible solutions from dualization; it is a property of the problem, not of a modeling choice. Learnability is exact when $\varepsilon^\star_\infty=0$, in particular under dual differentiability, and near-PACC with residual exactly $\varepsilon^\star_\infty$ otherwise. Finally, no distribution-free threshold exists already in the unconstrained specialization, so universality is the canonical frame for dual algorithms over large hypothesis classes.

</details>


### [64] [Optimal Learning Under Tsybakov Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08416)
*Steve Hanneke,Hongao Wang,Mingyue Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了Tsybakov噪声下PAC学习的一般概念类错误保证问题中上下界相差对数因子的二十年开放问题，通过改进上界以匹配已知最优下界，建立了Tsybakov噪声学习的最优错误保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tsybakov噪声模型允许靠近决策边界的点具有极高噪声（尽管稀少），是对现实更自然的噪声建模。MN06给出的上下界之间存在对数因子的差距，这一差距成为二十年的著名开放问题。

Method: 学习算法通过自适应地将实例空间划分为不同区域，每个区域大致对应不同的噪声水平，然后返回概念类中在每个区域均满足特定错误约束的假设。该技术理念与HLZ24和Han25等近期非可实现学习工作的进展共享概念基础。

Result: 将上界改进到与最佳已知下界匹配，从而确定了Tsybakov噪声下学习的最优错误保证，解决了困扰领域二十年的开放问题。

Conclusion: 确立了Tsybakov噪声下PAC学习一般概念类的最优错误保证，填补了理论与下界之间的最终缺口。

Abstract: Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) learning [Val84] is a fundamental learning model that has been extensively investigated. In this model, $\mathcal{H} \subseteq \{0,1\}^{\mathcal{X}}$ is a concept class, and $h^*\in\mathcal{H}$ is the target concept to be learned. Having access to i.i.d. labeled examples from a distribution $\mathcal{D}$ over $\mathcal{X}\times\{0,1\}$, which admits $h^*$ as the best concept in $\mathcal{H}$, the goal is to design a learning algorithm that outputs a hypothesis having low error competitive to $h^{*}$ with high probability.
  This model was initially studied under the realizable setting, which assumes that $h^*$ has no error. A natural relaxation is to allow label noise, that is, the true label can be flipped with probability $η\in(0,1/2)$. In reality, certain labels might be extremely noisy, especially for those points near the decision boundary. Hence, it is natural to allow very noisy points, though only rarely. This is quantified by a noise model introduced by [MT99] and [Tsy04], now known as Tsybakov noise. For learning general concept classes, [MN06] gave the general upper and lower bounds for error guarantees under Tsybakov noise. However, their upper and lower bounds differ by a logarithmic factor. Resolving this gap has remained a well-known open question for the past twenty years.
  In this work, we resolve this open question by improving the upper bound to match the best known lower bound, thus establishing the optimal error guarantee for learning under Tsybakov noise. Our learning algorithm operates by adaptively partitioning the instance space into regions, roughly corresponding to different noise levels, and returning a hypothesis in the concept class satisfying a specific error constraint for each region. Our technique shares a conceptual foundation with several recent advances in non-realizable learning, such as [HLZ24] and [Han25].

</details>


### [65] [FSTC-Encoder: Feature--Spatial--Temporal Correlation Learning for Generalizable RF Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08439)
*Jing Wang,Zhu Wang,Changlong Cheng,Yifan Guo,Yin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FSTC-Encoder，通过特征、空间、时间三个维度的相关性建模统一异构RF感知表示学习，在多个跨域基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构RF感知在特征结构、空间布局和时间尺度上差异显著，现有模型难以在不同设备、环境和RF模态间复用，缺乏统一的表示学习框架。

Method: FSTC-Encoder包含三个核心模块：结构感知特征编码（适配不同信号结构）、基于集合的空间编码（聚合可变观测）、层次化时间编码（同时捕获局部变化和长程依赖）。整个框架在不同感知任务和模态间共享相同的空间-时间骨干架构，仅需调整特征配置和任务头。

Result: 在Widar3.0、CSI-Bench和XRF55上，多因子跨域协议下平均准确率92.15%；在四个附加感知任务中三项排名第一；在WiFi、毫米波雷达和RFID上保持稳定性能；通过跨RF学习将跨模态性能差距从18.85%降至12.93%。

Conclusion: FSTC-Encoder展现出高域鲁棒性、任务通用性和模态可扩展性，验证了统一异构RF表示学习的有效性。

Abstract: Heterogeneous RF sensing differs substantially in feature structure, spatial layout, and temporal scale, making existing models difficult to reuse across devices, environments, and RF modalities. We propose FSTC-Encoder, which unifies heterogeneous RF representation learning through feature, spatial, and temporal correlation modeling. Structure-aware feature encoding accommodates different signal structures, set-based spatial encoding aggregates variable observations, and hierarchical temporal encoding jointly captures local variations and long-range dependencies. Across sensing tasks and modalities, FSTC-Encoder retains the same spatial--temporal backbone architecture while varying only the feature configuration and task head. Across Widar3.0, CSI-Bench, and XRF55, FSTC-Encoder achieves 92.15% mean Accuracy under multi-factor cross-domain protocols, ranks first on three of four additional sensing tasks, remains consistently strong across WiFi, millimeter-wave radar, and RFID, and reduces the cross-modality performance gap from 18.85% to 12.93% through cross-RF learning. These results demonstrate that FSTC-Encoder achieves high domain robustness, task generality, and modality extensibility.

</details>


### [66] [MGMCL: Multi-Granularity Manifold Contrastive Learning With Neural ODEs for Cross-Subject EEG Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08440)
*Xiang Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MGMCL框架，将跨被试EEG情绪识别重构为在SPD黎曼流形上学习连续表示的问题，通过多粒度流形对比学习、流形上的神经常微分方程、Gromov-Wasserstein流形对齐和弱监督学习，在SEED、SEED-IV和DEAP三个公开数据集上取得了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨被试EEG情绪识别面临两大挑战：一是被试间存在显著的个体差异性，导致跨被试泛化困难；二是现有方法采用离散的情绪分类框架，忽略了情感在时间上的连续性。此外，现有方法在欧几里得空间中操作且仅关注边缘分布对齐，无法保留不同被试间情绪的语义结构。

Method: MGMCL框架的核心方法包括：(1) 将情绪识别重新概念化为在SPD黎曼流形上学习连续表示；(2) 在实例、情绪和轨迹三个粒度上引入多粒度流形对比学习，同时保留语义排序；(3) 使用流形上的神经常微分方程建模连续情绪动态；(4) 采用Gromov-Wasserstein流形对齐实现跨被试泛化；(5) 通过弱监督学习实现从离散标签预测连续的效价-唤醒-支配（VAD）维度。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上取得了最先进性能：SEED上准确率91.23%，SEED-IV上73.82%，DEAP上76.38%，分别比此前最优方法提升1.89%、1.66%和1.28%。

Conclusion: MGMCL通过将EEG情绪识别引入SPD黎曼流形空间，结合多粒度对比学习、连续动态建模和流形对齐，有效解决了跨被试泛化和情绪连续性问题，为情感计算领域提供了新的方法论。

Abstract: Cross-subject electroencephalogram (EEG)-based emotion recognition remains challenging due to substantial inter-individual variability and discrete formulation that overlooks affective continuity. Existing methods operate in Euclidean space and focus on marginal distribution alignment, failing to preserve the semantic structure of emotions across subjects. This article proposes MGMCL, reconceptualizing emotion recognition as learning continuous representations on symmetric positive definite (SPD) Riemannian manifolds. The frame?work introduces multi-granularity manifold contrastive learning at instance, emotion, and trajectory levels while preserving semantic ordering. Neural ordinary differential equations on manifolds model continuous emotion dynamics. Cross-subject generalization employs Gromov-Wasserstein manifold alignment. Weakly-supervised learning enables continuous valence-arousal-dominance prediction from discrete labels. Extensive experiments on three public datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art performance: 91.23% accuracy on SEED, 73.82% on SEED-IV, and 76.38% on DEAP, achieving consistent improvements of 1.89%, 1.66%, and 1.28% over previous best methods, respectively.

</details>


### [67] [No Unique Minimizer, No Problem: On the Consistency of Robust Neural Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08489)
*Subhabrata Majumdar,Anand Deo,Partha Pratim Saha,Abhik Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对交叉熵训练对标签噪声和对抗污染敏感的问题，基于S-divergence族提出了无需可辨识性假设的鲁棒神经分类器一致性理论，证明经验S-divergence最小化器在温和正则条件下收敛到总体最优等价类，并在视觉和语言基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交叉熵训练的神经网络对标签噪声和对抗污染高度敏感，现有鲁棒替代方法虽具有有界影响和抗污染能力，但在深度学习场景下因神经网络参数化不可辨识（population loss最小化器是参数等价类而非唯一点），其统计基础不够充分，缺乏一致性理论支撑。

Method: 将训练建模为非可辨识参数空间上的随机优化问题，基于S-divergence损失函数族，在无需可辨识性假设的条件下证明经验S-divergence最小化器在温和正则条件下收敛到总体最优等价类；验证三种网络架构下的一致性条件；并证明鲁棒训练算法的极限点是经验目标的驻点。

Result: 实验在视觉和语言基准数据集上验证了S-divergence训练在保持干净数据精度的同时，性能可与现有鲁棒方法相竞争。

Conclusion: S-divergence提供了一种统计基础扎实且实用的鲁棒深度分类训练框架，在非可辨识条件下仍具一致性保证，兼顾干净数据性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Neural network classifiers trained by cross-entropy minimization are highly sensitive to label noise and adversarial contamination. While robust alternatives offer bounded influence and resistance to corruption, their statistical foundations in the deep learning setting are insufficient due to a fundamental difficulty: neural parameterizations are non-identifiable, so the population loss minimizer is an equivalence class of parameters, not a unique point. We develop a consistency theory for robust neural classifiers based on the S-divergence family that requires no identifiability assumption. Casting training as stochastic optimization over a non-identifiable parameter space, we prove that empirical S-divergence minimizers converge to the population-optimal equivalence class under mild regularity conditions, and verify these conditions for three architecture choices. We further establish that limit points of the robust training algorithm are stationary points of the empirical objective. Experiments on vision and language benchmark datasets confirm that S-divergence training maintains clean-data accuracy while exhibiting performance competitive with existing robust methods.

</details>


### [68] [Out-of-Distribution Federated Distillation with Domain-Aware Proxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08525)
*Jiahao Xiao,Jiangming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种域感知代理选择框架（DPS-FD），用于解决联邦蒸馏（FD）在分布外（OOD）场景下泛化能力不足的问题，通过自适应选择代理数据来提升模型在分布偏移下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦蒸馏通过知识蒸馏在代理数据上交换软预测而非模型参数，具有通信效率高和支持异构模型协作的优点。然而，现有FD模型在分布内（In-Distribution）数据上训练后，难以适应分布外（OOD）场景，即训练与测试数据存在分布偏移时性能显著下降。这一局限性严重制约了FD在实际场景中的应用。

Method: 提出一种域感知（domain-aware）的代理选择框架，核心思想是根据任务数据与代理数据之间的分布差异，自适应地选择最合适的代理数据参与联邦蒸馏过程，从而缓解OOD场景下的分布偏移问题。

Result: 在标准基准测试上，所提出的模型在有无代理数据的OOD场景下分别取得平均82.9%和80.6%的性能提升，有效解决了分布偏移挑战。

Conclusion: 实验表明，域感知代理选择框架能显著提升联邦蒸馏模型在OOD场景下的鲁棒性和泛化能力，并公开了代码和数据以供复现。

Abstract: Federated Learning is a distributed machine learning paradigm that trains a global model by aggregating local clients without sharing private data of each client. Federated Distillation (FD) builds upon this paradigm by leveraging knowledge distillation to exchange soft predictions on proxy data instead of model parameters, enabling more efficient communication and supporting heterogeneous model collaboration. However, FD models trained on In-Distribution data are hardly adapted to Out-of-Distribution (OOD) scenarios. In this paper, we propose a domain-aware proxy selection framework to better adopt proxy data for OOD problems.
