  tension between exploration and stability, we propose \emph{Lazy
  Graph-LinUCB}, an algorithm that performs lazy updates to minimize switching
  costs while maintaining near-optimal regret. Beyond stability, we introduce
  three advanced mechanisms to exploit the underlying graph structure: (1) an
  \emph{asynchronous} update schedule that eliminates synchronization overhead
  in sparse graphs; (2) an \emph{adaptive} algorithm that learns the graph
  structure from data; and (3) a \emph{joint estimator} that leverages data
  sharing among correlated objectives to significantly tighten regret
  bounds. Empirically, we demonstrate that these structural exploitations reduce
  movement costs by more than a factor of three in heterogeneous systems while
  maintaining similar cumulative losses.

</details>


### [137] [Imaginative Generative AI: Crossing the Entropy Wall into Worlds Beyond Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09385)
*Hossein Goli,Farzan Farnia,Amin Gohari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Imaginative Generative AI (IGA)框架，将生成模型的多样性作为目标分布设计问题的一部分：在参考分布附近选择谱多样性（基于von Neumann熵）达到预设水平的分布。通过"熵墙"（Entropy Wall）概念将生成分为多样性修复（低于墙）和想象力生成（高于墙）两个阶段，并提出无需重训的推理时引导方法IGA Guidance。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI模型主要目标是模仿数据分布，该目标既不纠正生成器丢失的多样性，也无法定义生成应如何超越数据本身的多样性。作者希望将多样性纳入目标分布设计问题，而非仅仅作为训练中的隐式目标。

Method: IGA在固定表示空间中，用生成分布的核协方差算子的von Neumann熵来度量多样性（即表示引导的无参考谱多样性度量）。在参考分布附近的分布集合中，选择谱多样性达到预设水平的分布。通过KL锚定到一个预训练生成器，优化满足自洽指数倾斜关系（exponential-tilt relation），由此推导出IGA Guidance——一种无需重训的推理时方法，适用于score-based和diffusion模型（包括DDPM和DDIM采样器）。

Result: 在合成数据集和视觉基准上的实验表明：在熵墙以下能有效进行多样性修复（恢复生成器丢失的变异），在熵墙以上能进行受控的谱外推（产生超出数据多样性水平的分布）。

Conclusion: IGA将"从模仿到想象"统一为一条正则化路径：低于熵墙时进行多样性修复，高于熵墙时进行受控的想象力生成。每个预设多样性水平都对应一个明确的i.i.d.目标分布，且通过IGA Guidance可在推理时无需重训即可实现。

Abstract: Generative AI models are primarily designed to imitate the data distribution, an objective that neither corrects diversity lost by a learned generator nor defines how generation should extend beyond the diversity of the data itself. We introduce Imaginative Generative AI (IGA), a framework that makes diversity part of the target-distribution design problem: among distributions close to a reference, IGA selects one whose spectral diversity reaches a prescribed level. Diversity is measured by the von Neumann entropy of the generated distribution's kernel covariance operator in a fixed representation space, providing a reference-free representation-guided measure of how broadly probability mass occupies embedding directions. The spectral entropy of the population data distribution defines an Entropy Wall. Below the wall, IGA performs diversity repair, recovering variation that a learned generator has lost while remaining within the diversity level of the data. Beyond the wall, the data distribution itself becomes infeasible, and IGA deliberately departs from it to produce distributions with greater representation-relative spectral diversity, an operational notion of imaginative generation. These regimes form a single regularization path from imitation to imagination and define an i.i.d. target distribution at each prescribed diversity level. We develop the theory of this entropy-constrained projection and show that, under a KL anchor to a pretrained generator, the optimum satisfies a self-consistent exponential-tilt relation. This characterization leads to IGA Guidance, a retraining-free inference-time method for score-based and diffusion models, including DDPM and DDIM samplers. Experiments on synthetic and vision benchmarks demonstrate diversity repair below the Entropy Wall and controlled spectral extrapolation beyond it.

</details>


### [138] [From Objectives to What Models Learn: A Landau Theory of Invariant Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09396)
*Pinli Wang,Yue He,Peng Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从不变学习目标出发推导Landau型有效自由能的框架，将表示学习视为多模式磁化过程，通过低阶系数（目标签名）预测正则化路径上的相位边界、模式消除、残差载荷及不稳定行为，并在双线性模型、ReLU网络和矩阵扩展中验证了该框架的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不变学习旨在学习跨环境保持预测性的表示，但其目标函数沿正则化路径的行为常常不透明（opaque），缺乏对正则化如何影响学习结果的理论理解。作者希望弥合目标（objective）与行为（behavior）之间的鸿沟，建立从目标结构到正则化表型的系统性预测框架。

Method: 本文将表示学习类比为多模式磁化（multimode magnetization），从具体的不变学习目标中推导Landau型有效自由能。该方法的关键步骤包括：（1）将有效自由能的低阶系数（二次项和四次项）识别为目标签名（objective signatures），它们分别决定相位边界移动、有限强度模式消除以及后临界振幅调节；（2）在正则双线性模型中推导闭合形式的相位边界和稳态载荷，明确区分捷径模式与稳定模式的临界强度，定义选择性保留窗口；（3）通过受控实验验证预测的相位边界、载荷和正则化表型；（4）将框架扩展到单/双隐藏层ReLU网络以及矩阵形式，处理耦合集体模式并给出谱相位边界准则。

Result: 理论分析表明：有效二次修正会移动相位边界并能在有限强度下消除模式；四次修正调节后临界振幅并通常在有限强度下保留残差载荷；高阶结构控制大正则化下的非单调尾部、不稳定性和坍缩。在双线性模型中获得闭合形式的相位边界和稳态载荷，以及捷径模式与稳定模式的不同临界强度。ReLU网络实验证实这些签名在深度依赖的尺度偏移下仍能预测正则化路径的定性行为，矩阵扩展推广到耦合模式并给出谱相位边界准则。

Conclusion: 该框架将不变学习目标的低阶签名转化为对正则化表型的可预测描述，揭示了正则化变化过程中模型究竟学到什么的机制性解释。通过统一的Landau自由能视角连接了目标函数几何与学习动力学，为理解和设计不变学习中的正则化策略提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Invariant learning seeks representations that remain predictive across environments, yet the behavior of its objectives along the regularization path is often opaque. We address this objective-behavior gap by viewing representation learning as multimode magnetization and deriving, from concrete invariant-learning objectives, a Landau-type effective free energy whose low-order coefficients form objective signatures and induce distinct regularization phenotypes. Effective quadratic corrections move the phase boundary and enable finite-strength mode elimination; quartic corrections regulate post-onset amplitude and typically leave residual loading at finite strength; higher-order structure governs non-monotone tails, instability, and collapse at large regularization. In a canonical bilinear model, the theory yields closed-form phase boundaries and steady-state loadings, as well as distinct critical strengths for shortcut and stable modes that define a selective-retention window. Controlled experiments confirm the predicted phase boundaries, loadings, and regularization phenotypes. In one- and two-hidden-layer ReLU networks, the same signatures remain predictive of qualitative regularization-path behavior despite depth-dependent shifts in scale. A matrix extension generalizes the framework to coupled collective modes and yields a spectral phase-boundary criterion. Together, the framework turns low-order objective signatures into predictions of regularization phenotypes and, ultimately, of what models learn as regularization varies.

</details>


### [139] [A Mechanistic Diagnostic of Rank Collapse in Post-Norm Decoder Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09417)
*Xingjian Wang,Qingyu Han,Xiaodong Luo,Yin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对Post-Norm解码器Transformer中的秩坍塌问题进行了两阶段分析，使用token相似度作为标量状态变量，揭示了因果注意力作为前缀平均算子在初始化阶段放大token相似度，以及高相似度状态下RMSNorm反向因子收缩导致梯度几何衰减的机制，并在48层模型上验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度解码器Transformer常因Post-Norm训练对warmup和学习率高度敏感而改用Pre-Norm。尽管已有工作指出秩坍塌和梯度消失是相关症状，但因果注意力如何产生高相似度表征以及训练动态为何无法修复它们仍未被充分理解。

Method: 提出两阶段理论分析：(1) 初始化阶段，证明因果注意力近似为前缀平均算子，随深度增加token相似度，而SwiGLU分支仅提供较小的阻尼效应；(2) 训练进入高相似度状态后，预归一化残差范数增长使RMSNorm反向因子变收缩，在温和条件下导致早期层梯度几何衰减。此外，从数学上刻画了坍塌网络的性质，包括最优预测器为频率分布、损失下界较高、坍塌层梯度消失等。

Result: 在C4数据集上训练的48层解码器Transformer实验与理论预测一致：初始化阶段的相似度增长和坍塌阶段的梯度收缩均被验证，坍塌运行轨迹停留在预测的频率损失附近。

Conclusion: 研究区分了Post-Norm坍塌中的前向相似度放大与后向修复无能两大机制，同时系统刻画了坍塌网络的行为特征，为理解Post-Norm训练困难提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Deep decoder-only Transformers often replace the original Post-Norm architecture with Pre-Norm variants because Post-Norm training is highly sensitive to warmup and learning rate under conventional initialization schemes. Although prior work has identified rank collapse and gradient vanishing as related symptoms, it remains poorly understood how causal attention creates high-similarity representations and why training dynamics fail to repair them. We give a two-stage analysis of Post-Norm rank collapse using token similarity as a scalar state variable. First, at initialization, causal attention acts approximately as a prefix-averaging operator that increases token similarity across depth, while the SwiGLU branch contributes only a smaller damping effect. Second, once training enters a high-similarity regime, growth of pre-normalization residual norms makes the RMSNorm backward factor contractive; under mild conditions, gradients to earlier layers decay geometrically. As a complementary result, we characterize the properties of a collapsed network: its best predictor is frequency distribution with relatively high loss floor, and gradients in collapsed layers vanish at frequency distribution. Experiments on 48-layer decoder-only Transformers trained on C4 dataset match the predicted initialization-time similarity growth and collapse-time gradient contraction, and show that collapsed runs stay near the predicted frequency loss. Together, these results distinguish the forward similarity amplification and backward repair incapacity in Post-Norm collapse, while also characterizing the behavior of collapsed networks.

</details>


### [140] [LITEWAY: LIghtweight HAR via Temporal Efficient highWAY](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09421)
*Dominique Nshimyimana,Vitor Fortes Rey,Mengxi Liu,Bo Zhou,Paul Lukowicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LITEWAY，一种模态无关的全卷积框架，用于可穿戴设备上的多通道传感器时间序列人类活动识别（HAR），通过结构化卷积分解替代循环架构（GRU/LSTM），在16个HAR数据集上实现与现有方法相当的宏F1分数，同时将模型体积缩小4-9倍，能耗降低1.5-3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴HAR的轻量级方法通常依赖循环架构（如GRU和LSTM），这类架构并行性受限且推理延迟较高，在资源受限的设备上难以满足计算和能耗约束。需要一种更高效的时间序列建模方法。

Method: LITEWAY采用全卷积框架，通过三个核心组件实现高效时间建模：1) 轻量级卷积块；2) 跨步时间处理（strided temporal processing）；3) 卷积-注意力池化（convolution-attention pooling）。该框架以结构化卷积分解替代循环时间建模，同时保持模态无关性，适用于多通道传感器时间序列。

Result: 在16个HAR数据集上，LITEWAY相较于TinyHAR、TinierHAR和MLP-HAR：模型尺寸缩减4.06x-9.52x（Light版本）和3.87x-9.07x（Full版本）；能耗降低2.29x-3.14x（Light版本）和1.46x-2.01x（Full版本），同时保持有竞争力的宏F1分数。

Conclusion: LITEWAY证明了全卷积时间建模方法在可穿戴HAR中的有效性，能够在保持识别性能的同时显著降低模型大小和能耗，为资源受限设备上的HAR提供了一种高效且并行友好的替代方案。

Abstract: Wearable human activity recognition (HAR) remains challenging due to the computational and energy constraints of deep learning models on resource-limited devices. Existing lightweight approaches often rely on recurrent architectures (e.g., GRU and LSTM), limiting parallelism and increasing inference latency. We propose LITEWAY, a modality-agnostic, fully convolutional framework for multichannel sensor time series that replaces recurrent temporal modeling with structured convolutional decomposition. LITEWAY combines lightweight convolutional blocks, strided temporal processing, and convolution-attention pooling to efficiently capture temporal dependencies while reducing computational complexity. We evaluate LITEWAY on 16 HAR datasets against TinyHAR, TinierHAR, and MLP-HAR. LITEWAY achieves competitive macro F1 while reducing model size by 4.06x-9.52x (Light) and 3.87x-9.07x (Full) compared with TinyHAR and TinierHAR. Deployment experiments further show energy reductions of 2.29x-3.14x (Light) and 1.46x-2.01x (Full) compared with TinierHAR and MLP-HAR, highlighting efficient fully convolutional temporal modeling for wearable HAR. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/dominique-nshimyimana/liteway.

</details>


### [141] [How Simple Can It Get? From Interpretable Equations to Readable Rules for Financial Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09433)
*Adia Lumadjeng,Ilker Birbil,Erman Acar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个与常见做法相反的"可解释模型简化"框架：从单方程可解释分类器出发，逐步将其简化为剪枝单项式、方向性if-then规则、整数评分卡和计数表等更可读的金融部署形式。由于方程本身就是预测模型，可直接量化每次简化造成的损失。在四个金融数据集上发现剪枝几乎无成本，而保真度比预测性能下降更快；人工评估显示简化提升可读性，但偏好因职业背景而异。作者还从理论上推导了剪枝变化的界，并预测仅保留特征方向的规则对原始排序的保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融等受监管领域，模型必须可解释才能部署，但许多可解释分类器输出的公式包含几十个特征，监管者无法阅读，违背了可解释性的初衷。现有工作大多关注"事后解释"（post-hoc explanation），而本文采取相反方向：不追求更复杂的解释，而是将已经可解释的模型进一步简化成真正可读、可部署的形式，并直接量化简化成本。

Method: 提出一个系统的模型简化框架，包含多条逐步简化的路径：剪枝单项式（pruned monomial）、方向性if-then规则（仅保留特征效应的方向）、整数评分卡和计数表（金融领域已部署的形式）。由于基础分类器本身是解释性方程而非常见的事后解释，能够精确度量每种简化操作的损失（预测性能与保真度分离量化）。同时从理论层面推导剪枝导致的输出变化上界，并分析仅保留方向信息的规则如何影响原始模型排序的保真度。

Result: 在四个金融数据集上的实验表明：1) 剪枝的代价几乎为零；2) 保真度的下降速度快于预测性能的下降，意味着更简单的规则可以保持有效的分类能力但并不忠实复现原始模型；3) 人工评估确认简化提升了感知可读性，但对不同表示形式的偏好因职业背景（如监管、风控、技术等）而异。

Conclusion: 模型简化提供了一条从"统计上可解释"到"实践中可读、可监管"的桥梁。关键洞见是：简单性与预测性能可以共存，但不一定与模型保真度共存——这提示在部署简化模型时需要区分"模型有多好"与"模型有多忠实于原模型"两个维度。理论上界使得部分简化损失可在简化前被预测，为监管合规提供了可预期的决策依据。

Abstract: In regulated domains such as finance, a model that cannot be explained cannot be deployed, yet many interpretable classifiers defeat their own purpose by producing formulas with dozens of features that no regulator could read. We take the reverse direction. Starting from an interpretable classifier expressed as a single equation over the input features, we progressively simplify it into more readable forms, including a pruned monomial, a directional if--then rule, and the integer scorecards and tallies that finance already deploys. Because the equation is itself the predictive model rather than a post-hoc explanation we can directly quantify what is lost under each simplification. Across four financial datasets, we find that pruning is nearly free and that fidelity can erode faster than predictive performance, allowing simpler rules to remain effective classifiers without faithfully reproducing the original model. A human assessment shows that simplification improves perceived readability, while preferences for different representations vary by professional background. Beyond measuring these losses empirically, we show that some can be anticipated from the original model: we derive a bound on the change caused by pruning and predict how faithfully a rule retaining only the direction of each feature's effect preserves the original ranking.

</details>


### [142] [Depth-adaptive Inference of Looped Language Models via Continuous Depth Batching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09444)
*Kristian Schwethelm,Daniel Rueckert,Georgios Kaissis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出连续深度批处理（CDB），一种面向循环语言模型的端到端调度框架，通过在单次循环迭代粒度上调度，解决了自适应深度推理中批量处理的难题，在Ouro 1.4B和Huginn 3.5B上实现了理论上限99%的加速收益，离线吞吐量提升1.5-1.9倍，动态负载下归一化延迟降低45-90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型的核心承诺是深度自适应推理——对"简单"token使用更少的计算量，对"困难"token使用更多计算量。然而这种自适应性破坏了标准批处理机制：同一批次中的token需要不同次数的循环迭代，导致无法统一前向传播，使高效推理变得困难。尽管循环级调度曾被提出作为解决方案，但从未被端到端实现，其关键难点在于循环架构还包含非循环的边界阶段（如token嵌入和LM头），它们需要以与循环不同的频率调度。

Method: CDB在单个循环迭代粒度上进行调度，而非token级或整个循环级。具体措施包括：1) 将边界阶段和循环步骤分别放入独立的优先级队列中进行调度；2) 提前一步做出退出决策；3) 将所有调度工作与GPU计算重叠进行，避免调度开销影响推理性能。

Result: 在Ouro 1.4B和Huginn 3.5B两个模型上进行评估，CDB能够实现自适应深度加速理论上限的99%，离线吞吐量提升1.5-1.9倍，在动态服务负载下归一化延迟降低45-90%。

Conclusion: CDB证明了循环语言模型的自适应深度推理可以在高效批处理框架中实现端到端落地，通过细粒度的迭代级调度策略，能够在不牺牲批处理效率的前提下充分释放自适应计算的性能潜力。

Abstract: A main promise of looped language models (LMs) is depth-adaptive inference. By iterating a block of shared layers a variable number of times, the model can use less compute for "easy" tokens and more for "hard" ones. However, this adaptivity breaks standard batching: tokens in the same batch now require a different number of loops, so there is no unified forward pass, making efficient inference difficult. Standard inference frameworks like vLLM schedule on the token level and cannot handle this because tokens need to be removed from the batch within the forward pass. Loop-level scheduling has been proposed as a solution, but never implemented end to end. The key challenge is that looped architectures also contain non-looped boundary stages (e.g., token embedding and LM head) that must be scheduled at different frequencies than the loop. We introduce continuous depth batching (CDB), which schedules at the granularity of individual loop iterations. CDB handles boundary stages and loop steps in separate priority queues, makes exit decisions one step ahead, and overlaps all scheduling work with GPU computation. On Ouro 1.4B and Huginn 3.5B, CDB can realize up to $99\%$ of the theoretical maximum speed-up from adaptive-depth, translating to $1.5$-$1.9\times$ higher offline throughput and $45$-$90\%$ lower normalized latency under dynamic serving load.

</details>


### [143] [WDL-OPD: Weak-Driven On-Policy Distillation via Mixture-Constrained Co-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09447)
*Zehao Chen,Gongxun Li,Tianxiang Ai,Yifei Li,Zixuan Huang,Wang Zhou,Tao Huang,Fuzhen Zhuang,Xianglong Liu,Jianxin Li,Deqing Wang,Yikun Ban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WDL-OPD，一种基于混合约束的协同训练方法用于同策略蒸馏（OPD），通过锚定策略生成轨迹、辅助策略评估状态，并将两者token分布的几何混合与冻结教师进行反向KL匹配，有效解决了OPD中反馈循环不稳定的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同策略蒸馏（OPD）通过在学生自身采样的轨迹上对齐学生与教师，减少了离线蒸馏中训练与测试状态不匹配的问题。但该反馈回路存在不稳定性：每次更新同时改变策略和下一次更新所基于的状态，导致熵增长或轨迹退化。

Method: WDL-OPD包含两个可训练策略：锚定策略负责生成所有rollout，辅助策略在相同访问状态上进行评估，两者token分布的几何混合与冻结教师通过反向KL匹配。两个策略均接收梯度。冻结辅助策略可恢复为与OPD²和W2S-OPD密切相关的锚定加对比代理目标，而联合训练则产生静态delta无法表达的branch级自由度。

Result: 在Qwen3 1.7B和4B规模的实验中，WDL-OPD在四个scale-domain设置中的每一个都产生了最强的学生checkpoint。MATH500准确率在4B从0.630提升至0.685，在1.7B从0.521提升至0.585。在代码生成中，七个单策略OPD配置出现熵增长或轨迹退化，而协同训练达到独立重评估的开发分数0.637和0.375。

Conclusion: 由于部分比较在课程或初始化方面存在差异，这些结果支持"稳定性假说"而非普适性因果声明。作者提供了精确的训练算法、失败证据和受控比较矩阵，供进一步验证该假说。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) aligns a student with a teacher on trajectories sampled from the student itself, reducing the train-test state mismatch of offline distillation. The same feedback loop can nevertheless be unstable: each update changes both the policy and the states on which the next update is computed. We introduce WDL-OPD, a mixture-constrained co-training method with two trainable policies. An anchor policy generates every rollout, an auxiliary policy evaluates the same visited states, and a geometric mixture of their token distributions is matched to a frozen teacher by reverse KL. Both policies receive gradient. We show that freezing the auxiliary recovers an anchor-plus-contrast proxy target closely related to OPD$^2$ and W2S-OPD, whereas joint training creates branch-level degrees of freedom that a static delta cannot express. In recorded Qwen3 experiments at 1.7B and 4B scale, WDL-OPD produces the strongest student checkpoint in each of four scale-domain settings. It raises MATH500 accuracy from 0.630 to 0.685 at 4B and from 0.521 to 0.585 at 1.7B. In code generation, seven single-policy OPD configurations exhibit entropy growth or trajectory degradation, while co-training reaches independently re-evaluated development scores of 0.637 and 0.375. Because several comparisons differ in curriculum or initialization, these results support a stabilization hypothesis rather than a universal causal claim. We provide the exact training algorithm, failure evidence, and the controlled comparison matrix needed to test that hypothesis.

</details>


### [144] [From Approachability Residuals to Anytime-Valid Evidence: The Online Convex Geometry of Testing by Betting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09450)
*Jinze Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过支撑函数残差建立了基于赌注的序贯检验与Blackwell逼近性之间的速率显式关联，证明了精确路径恒等式，并推导出有限时间转移定理，将OCO学习器与对数财富遗憾联系起来，从而统一了确定性Blackwell博弈与被动检验两种情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将赌注型序贯检验与Blackwell逼近性这两个看似不同的框架通过精确的代数恒等式统一起来，弥补两者之间缺乏定量、有限时间连接的空白，使OCO中的后悔界限可以直接转化为检验的拒绝阈值与置信半径。

Method: 通过定义可预测法向量w_t与支撑函数残差q_t，证明距离分解的精确路径恒等式；结合单边赌注将OCO遗憾a_T与对数财富遗憾ℓ_T组合，导出有限时间转移界限；进一步构造受控随机实验，使动作满足Blackwell支撑半空间条件，从而将财富转化为自适应原假设下的e-process。

Result: 证明了精确路径恒等式dist(ḡ_T,S)=(1/T)Σq_t+Reg_T/T；当|q_t|≤B时，若目标间隙超过a_T/T+2B√((log(1/α)+ℓ_T)/T)则时间T前拒绝，否则证明相反的半径；在受控实验中，子线性OCO遗憾给出随机逼近性，而均值持续分离下指数财富速率至少为δ²/(4B²)。

Conclusion: 该连接在代数上精确、在有限时间意义上定量、且在实验可控时具有可操作性，并可通过有界两样本均值、核MMD及异质数据源等实例加以验证，为序贯检验与博弈逼近性提供了统一的定量框架。

Abstract: Betting-based sequential tests and Blackwell approachability are linked by a rate-explicit reduction through support-function residuals. For a compact convex target $S$ and vector observations $r_t$, an OCO learner selects a predictable normal $w_t$ and produces $q_t=\langle w_t,r_t\rangle-h_S(w_t)$. We prove the exact pathwise identity $$
  \dist(\bar r_T,S)
  =\frac1T\sum_{t=1}^Tq_t+\frac{\Reg_T}{T}. $$ When $|q_t|\leq B$, composing this identity with one-sided betting yields a finite-time transfer: if the OCO and log-wealth regrets are at most $a_T$ and $\ell_T$, respectively, then a target gap exceeding \[
  \frac{a_T}{T}
  +2B\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/α)+\ell_T}{T}} \] forces rejection by time $T$, while non-rejection certifies the converse radius. We then formulate a controlled stochastic experiment in which an action selected after $w_t$ satisfies Blackwell's supporting-halfspace condition for every null mean payoff. The resulting wealth is an e-process under adaptive nulls; sublinear OCO regret gives stochastic approachability, whereas persistent mean separation under an alternative gives exponential wealth at rate at least $δ^2/(4B^2)$. Deterministic Blackwell games and passive tests are, respectively, the noise-free and singleton-action cases of this protocol. Bounded two-sample means, kernel MMD, and active heterogeneous data sources instantiate the reduction. The resulting connection is exact algebraically, quantitative at finite time, and operational when experiments are controlled.

</details>


### [145] [Learning to Modulate, Not to Cycle: Soft Actor---Critic Recovers Inverter-Style Heat-Pump Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09453)
*Faizan Ahmed,Aniket Dixit,James Brusey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了住宅热泵压缩机启停循环（on-off cycling）对设备磨损的影响，提出在强化学习（RL）控制器的奖励函数中加入压缩机磨损项。在BOPTEST测试场景中对比SAC和PPO两种算法，发现SAC学习到了连续调制策略（使压缩机持续运行，实现零启停），而PPO退化为bang-bang控制反而增加循环次数。SAC在仿真器上实现了最高90.7%的热不适降低、11.5%的成本增加，并完全消除了基线循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 住宅热泵中on-off循环是压缩机磨损的主要原因，但现有的建筑RL控制器通常只优化能源成本和热舒适，忽略了学习策略导致的循环行为，这可能导致压缩机过度启停、加速设备老化。

Method: 在控制奖励函数中加入等级化的压缩机磨损项（levelised compressor-wear term），并在BOPTEST bestest hydronic热泵案例上构建相同的马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），分别使用Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) 和 Proximal Policy Optimisation (PPO) 进行训练，比较两者在压缩机循环行为上的差异。

Result: SAC学习到了连续调制策略，使压缩机持续运行（类逆变器驱动热泵工作方式），实现每天零启动；PPO则退化为bang-bang控制，循环次数超过基线。SAC在BOPTEST仿真器上使热不适降低高达90.7%，成本仅增加11.5%，同时完全消除基线循环。

Conclusion: 通过将压缩机磨损纳入强化学习奖励函数，RL控制器可以同时优化能耗、舒适度和设备寿命。SAC算法由于策略分布优势和连续动作探索能力，更适合学习平滑的连续调制控制策略，从而显著减少压缩机启停循环。

Abstract: On--off cycling is the main cause of compressor wear in residential heat pumps, yet reinforcement learning (RL) controllers for buildings typically optimise only energy cost and thermal comfort, ignoring how much the learned policy cycles. We add a levelised compressor-wear term to the control reward and study how the resulting behaviour depends on the RL algorithm. Training Soft Actor---Critic (SAC) and Proximal Policy Optimisation (PPO) on an identical Markov decision process for the BOPTEST bestest hydronic heat pump case, we find that SAC learns a continuous modulation policy that keeps the compressor permanently engaged---the operating principle of an inverter-driven heat pump---achieving zero start-ups per day, whereas PPO collapses to bang-bang control that cycles more than the baseline. On the BOPTEST emulator the SAC policy cuts thermal discomfort by up to 90.7% for an 11.5% cost increase, while eliminating all baseline cycling.

</details>


### [146] [Flow-based conditional cardiac anatomy generation for virtual cohorts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09460)
*Konstantinos Kevopoulos,Beatrice Moscoloni,Benjamin Alheit,Cameron Beeche,Julio A. Chirinos,Alexander Heinlein,Mathias Peirlinck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAN-FLOW是一种基于归一化流的条件解剖生成框架，用于生成符合性别、年龄和BMI等元数据条件的双心室虚拟解剖队列。与条件变分自编码器（cVAE）相比，CAN-FLOW通过两步策略（先学习几何潜表示，再用条件归一化流建模元数据相关分布）更好地再现了临床表型分布、元数据依赖趋势和亚组变异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏数字孪生研究正从个体化解剖复制转向虚拟队列构建，但受限于队列规模、亚组稀疏性和数据共享约束，代表性影像解剖数据难以获取。现有条件生成模型（主要是cVAE）将表征学习和元数据条件化耦合在共享的正则化潜先验中，导致虚拟队列难以保持现实且元数据依赖的解剖变异性，因此需要更优的条件生成框架。

Method: CAN-FLOW采用两步框架：(1) 首先学习仅基于几何的微分同胚心脏形状动量（shape momenta）的潜表示；(2) 然后使用条件归一化流（conditional normalizing flow）对潜表示在性别、年龄和身体质量指数（BMI）条件下的分布进行建模。模型在2,208名健康UK Biobank受试者上训练，并与多种正则化强度的cVAE进行对比。

Result: CAN-FLOW生成的随机双心室解剖结构在多个指标上优于cVAE：更准确地再现了临床表型分布、元数据依赖趋势、亚组变异性、点云覆盖率和高维形状变异性，且生成的解剖结构在随机性和合理性上均有保障。

Conclusion: CAN-FLOW作为可共享的框架，能够生成真实、随机变化且元数据条件化的双心室解剖结构，为虚拟队列构建和计算机模拟（in silico）临床试验工作流提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Cardiac digital twin research is moving from subject-specific anatomical replicas toward virtual cohorts that represent clinically relevant population subgroups. Yet access to representative imaging-derived anatomy datasets remains limited by cohort size, subgroup sparsity, and data-sharing constraints. Conditional generative models could help address this gap, but virtual cohorts are useful only if they preserve realistic, metadata-dependent anatomical variability. Existing cardiac anatomy generators largely rely on conditional variational autoencoders (cVAEs), which couple representation learning and metadata conditioning through a shared regularized latent prior. We introduce CAN-FLOW, a two-step Conditional ANatomy generation framework based on normalizing FLOWs that first learns geometry-only latent representations of diffeomorphic cardiac shape momenta and then models their sex-, age-, and body-mass-index-dependent distribution with a conditional normalizing flow. We trained CAN-FLOW on 2,208 healthy UK Biobank subjects and compared it with cVAEs across regularization strengths. CAN-FLOW generated plausible stochastic biventricular anatomies that better reproduced clinical phenotype distributions, metadata-dependent trends, subgroup variability, point-cloud coverage, and high-dimensional shape variability. Together, these results establish CAN-FLOW as a shareable framework for generating realistic, stochastically varying, metadata-conditioned biventricular anatomies for virtual cohort construction and in silico clinical trial workflows.

</details>


### [147] [MixFormer: Linear Transformer with Mixture of Memory Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09468)
*Yu Guo,Lei Duan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MixFormer是一种集成混合记忆专家(MoE)机制的线性Transformer，通过时间感知线性注意力(TALA)动态更新记忆，解决SSM在超长序列建模中输入自适应性和记忆容量受限的问题，在长序列文本和图像生成任务中取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有状态空间模型(SSM)虽然相比标准Transformer在长上下文建模中具有更高效率，但存在有限的输入自适应性和受限的记忆容量，导致在建模超长序列时产生信息丢失。作者旨在解决这些问题，同时保持线性Transformer的效率优势。

Method: MixFormer通过以下核心组件实现：(1) 混合记忆专家(MoE)机制：维护多个协作的记忆专家，每个专家持有差异化的记忆状态；(2) 时间感知线性注意力(TALA)：利用可学习的指数衰减函数和位置偏置动态更新记忆，选择性地强化重要历史信息并缓解记忆稀释。

Result: 在长序列文本和图像生成任务上的实验表明，MixFormer取得了显著的性能提升，同时为下一代网络基础设施提供了更可持续的计算骨干。

Conclusion: MixFormer通过MoE机制与时间感知注意力相结合，有效克服了现有SSM在输入自适应性和记忆容量方面的局限，为长上下文建模提供了性能更强且计算更可持续的线性Transformer方案。

Abstract: State Space Models (SSMs), as a mainstream research direction of linear Transformers, aim to achieve higher efficiency than standard Transformers in long-context modeling. However, existing SSMs suffer from limited input adaptivity and constrained memory capacity, leading to information loss when modeling ultra-long sequences. To address these limitations, we propose MixFormer, a novel linear Transformer that integrates a Mixture-of-Memory-Experts (MoE) mechanism. Specifically, the model maintains differentiated memory states through multiple collaborating memory experts and employs a novel Time-Aware Linear Attention (TALA) mechanism, which leverages learnable exponential decay functions and positional biases to dynamically update memory. This design enables the model to selectively reinforce important historical information while effectively mitigating memory dilution, substantially improving long-range dependency modeling. Experiments on long-sequence text and image generation tasks demonstrate that MixFormer not only achieves significant performance gains but also provides a more sustainable computational backbone for the next generation of web infrastructure.

</details>


### [148] [Hierarchical rank-evolving representation for physics-informed neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09483)
*Ruoyang Su,Xi-Le Zhao,Kun Li,Liang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种层次化秩自适应（HRE）表示方法，用于多元函数分解，并构建了对应的HRE-PINNs。通过将目标函数分解为小规模内张量与一组单变量函数的乘积，并自动确定张量秩，克服了现有T-PINNs依赖预指定低秩分解和手动调参的局限性，在多个高维、非线性及流体动力学问题上取得了优于现有方法的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于张量的物理信息神经网络（T-PINNs）主要依赖预先指定的低秩张量分解和手动调节的秩，这限制了其捕获多元解函数底层结构的能力，也阻碍了实际部署。需要一种能够自动确定秩结构的方法。

Method: 提出HRE（hierarchical rank-evolving）表示：将目标多元函数分解为一个规模较小的内张量与沿各模态的一组单变量函数的组合。在内张量上部署定制化的张量网络分解以捕获其底层结构，同时秩参数在分解过程中被自适应地揭示，无需手动调节。基于该表示构建了HRE-PINNs。

Result: 通过涵盖高维静态问题（Helmholtz方程和Poisson方程）、非线性时间相关问题（Klein-Gordon方程）以及复杂流体动力学问题（流混合方程和Navier-Stokes方程）的大量数值实验，HRE-PINNs在精度上持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: HRE-PINNs通过层次化结构和自适应秩确定机制，有效提升了多元函数表征能力和求解精度，更具实际应用价值，为T-PINNs的发展提供了新方向。

Abstract: Recently, tensor-based physics-informed neural networks (T-PINNs) have received increasing attention. However, existing T-PINNs still face a fundamental challenge: they mainly rely on pre-specified low-rank tensor decompositions with manually tuned ranks, which limits their ability to capture the underlying structures of multivariate solution functions and hinders their practical deployment. To address this challenge, we propose a hierarchical rank-evolving (abbreviated as HRE) representation for multivariate functions, which endows us to faithfully capture the underlying structure of the targeted multivariate function accompanying with automatic rank determination. Concretely, in the hierarchical design of HRE representation, the target multivariate function is decomposed as a small-scale inner tensor with a set of univariate functions along each mode, where a customized tensor network decomposition can be readily deployed to capture the underlying structure of the small-scale inner tensor. In HRE representation, the crucial hyperparameters, ranks, can be adaptively revealed during the decomposition, freeing us from manual rank tuning and making HRE practically applicable to real-world problems. Besides, we build the HRE-PINNs correspondingly. Extensive numerical experiments, including high-dimensional static problems (Helmholtz equation and Poisson equation), nonlinear time-dependent problems (Klein-Gordon equation), and complex fluid-dynamics problems (flow mixing equation and Navier-Stokes equation), demonstrate that HRE-PINNs consistently outperform existing state-of-the-art approaches in terms of accuracy.

</details>


### [149] [When Do Task Vectors Interfere? Mapping the Validity Boundaries of Weight-Space Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09490)
*Chencheng Zhu,Xiaoyang Li,Taotao Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过测量二维任务向量曲面上的逐对功能非加性，系统研究了任务算术中参数几何与功能几何的关系。研究发现代码+安全任务在特定提示分布下表现出比代码+数学更强的非加性，该排序在多种规模、方法及跨架构下保持，但其预测能力是粗粒度的、依赖于输入与格式条件，而非通用的合并性能预测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务算术将微调位移视为权重空间中可组合的方向，但参数相加何时能反映模型功能的可预测变化仍然不清楚。现有研究缺乏对参数几何与功能几何之间关系的系统性度量，需要澄清任务向量组合的有效性边界。

Method: 作者分离参数几何与功能几何，在二维任务向量曲面上用首token预测分布交互比率度量逐对功能非加性，该度量以输入分布为条件，并在范数匹配对照、三个训练种子和仅响应微调下评估。随后进行前瞻性六任务扩展验证，并在全参数微调（0.5B）、Qwen2.5 LoRA（至7B）和Llama-3.1-8B跨架构上进行验证，最后用外部基准进行验证。

Result: Qwen2.5-1.5B上代码+安全任务的非加性在代码及指令提示上强于代码+数学对照，但在数学提示上不成立。前瞻性六任务扩展中所有八个高-低对比均符合预测方向。该排序在全参数微调、LoRA规模扩展及跨架构审计中保持，但外部验证显示边界更尖锐：原始提示保留连续对比，指令式包装会使其坍缩，EvalPlus pass@1交互不能稳健复现。

Conclusion: 权重空间组合支持粗粒度、输入和格式条件化的功能陈述，可跨适应方法、规模和一个额外模型家族推广，但不能作为通用的合并性能预测器。

Abstract: Task arithmetic treats fine-tuning displacements as composable directions in weight space, yet it remains unclear when parameter addition reflects predictable changes in model function. We separate parameter geometry from functional geometry and measure pairwise functional non-additivity over a two-dimensional task-vector surface, using a first-token predictive-distribution interaction ratio conditioned on an input distribution and evaluated with norm-matched controls, three training seeds, and response-only fine-tuning. On Qwen2.5-1.5B, code+safety is more non-additive than the matched code+math control on code and instruction prompts, but not on math prompts. In a prospectively specified six-task expansion, all eight high-versus-low comparisons of unseen task pairs have the predicted sign. The primary ordering further persists under full-parameter fine-tuning at 0.5B, Qwen2.5 LoRA scale tests up to 7B, and a Llama-3.1-8B cross-architecture audit. External validation exposes a sharper boundary: raw public code, instruction, and safety prompts preserve the continuous contrast, whereas an instruction-style wrapper collapses it on the identical public-code prompts, and EvalPlus pass@1 interactions do not robustly reproduce it. Weight-space composition therefore supports coarse, input- and format-conditioned functional statements across adaptation methods, scales, and one additional model family, not a universal merging-performance predictor.

</details>


### [150] [Tracking the Best Strategy in an Extensive-Form Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09501)
*Stephen Pasteris,Rahul Savani,Theodore Turocy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了对抗性环境下的序贯博弈（extensive-form game）bandit问题，提出了一种算法，实现了切换遗憾（switching regret）上界为Õ((1/ρ+ρK)√(HAT))，且每回合时间复杂度仅为O(HB)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在序贯博弈的bandit学习问题中，已有的遗憾保证通常针对固定混合策略的对照序列，但对切换序列（switching sequence）的对照缺乏研究。切换遗憾提供了更细粒度的性能度量，能够更好地刻画学习者在非平稳环境下的表现。

Method: 算法以参数ρ>0作为输入，结合了序贯博弈的结构特性，在每个信息集上利用bandit反馈进行策略更新。算法通过控制切换遗憾与参数ρ之间的权衡（即1/ρ与ρK两项的平衡）来实现目标遗憾界，并在计算效率上利用树结构的局部性，将每回合时间压缩到O(HB)。

Result: 算法达到了Õ((1/ρ+ρK)√(HAT))的切换遗憾界，其中K为对照序列中的切换次数，H为最大信息集遍历深度，A为总动作数。同时每回合的时间复杂度仅为O(HB)，B为任意信息集上的最大动作数，显示出极高的计算效率。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在序贯博弈bandit框架下建立了针对混合策略切换序列的遗憾保证，并兼顾了计算效率，为对抗性序贯决策中的非平稳学习提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We consider the extensive-form bandit problem where on each trial the learner plays an extensive-form game against an oblivious adversary. We focus on the notion of switching regret, which measures the expected performance of the learner against that of any switching sequence of mixed strategies in retrospect. Our algorithm takes a parameter $ρ>0$ and achieves a switching regret of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}((1/ρ+ρK)\sqrt{H A T})$ where $K$ is the number of switches in the comparator sequence, $H$ is the maximum number of the learner's information sets that can be traversed during a play of the game and $A$ is the number of actions that the learner can possibly take. Our algorithm is extremely efficient, taking a per trial time of only $\mathcal{O}(H B)$ where $B$ is the maximum number of actions available to the learner at any of its information sets.

</details>


### [151] [Generalized Convexity and Smoothness via Conjugate Duality: Optimization Theory for Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09523)
*Binchuan Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Legendre函数与凸共轭的统一优化框架，引入H(ψ)-凸性和H(Ψ)-光滑性，统一了凸与非凸、光滑与非光滑目标，并推导了广义梯度下降和广义随机梯度下降的收敛率，通过理论与实践验证了该框架对DNN训练的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典优化理论依赖可微性、凸性、光滑性等假设，而这些假设在深度神经网络目标函数中往往不成立，导致传统优化理论无法充分解释DNN训练的强经验性能。作者旨在建立统一的优化框架来弥合理论与实践之间的鸿沟。

Method: 利用Legendre函数和凸共轭推广经典凸性与光滑性概念，分别提出H(ψ)-凸性和H(Ψ)-光滑性；通过凸共轭引入广义梯度下降（广义GD）和广义随机梯度下降（广义SGD）；将DNN训练重述为复合优化问题，并提出梯度相关因子与模型容量风险来刻画网络架构和训练配置的影响。

Result: 理论上证明了广义GD的最优学习率恰好为1，给出了两种优化器的基于梯度能量的收敛率；实证上在多种网络架构、数据集、优化器和损失函数上验证了理论界限与实际训练动态精确对齐。

Conclusion: 该框架揭示了广义光滑性与凸性之间的自然对偶关系，为DNN训练的收敛性提供了统一的理论基础，并能定量分析架构设计、批量大小和模型容量对训练收敛的影响。

Abstract: Deep neural network (DNN) training with stochastic gradient descent (SGD) and its variants achieves strong empirical performance, yet classical optimization theory does not fully explain this success. This limitation arises because conventional analyses rely on assumptions such as differentiability, convexity, or smoothness, which are often violated by DNN objectives. In this paper, we establish a unified optimization framework for DNN training by generalizing classical convexity and smoothness through Legendre functions and convex conjugation. Specifically, we introduce $\mathcal{H}(ψ)$-convexity and $\mathcal{H}(Ψ)$-smoothness, which unify convex and non-convex as well as smooth and non-smooth objectives within a single formalism and reveal a natural duality between generalized smoothness and convexity. Building on these generalized properties, we introduce generalized gradient descent (GD) and generalized SGD through convex conjugation. We theoretically prove that generalized GD admits an optimal learning rate of exactly $1$, and derive rigorous gradient-energy-based convergence rates for both proposed optimizers. We further reformulate DNN training as a composite optimization problem, demonstrating that its convergence relies on jointly reducing the gradient energy and controlling the induced norm of the network Jacobian. To characterize the practical influences of network architectures and training configurations, we introduce the gradient correlation factor and model capacity risk, and quantitatively analyze how architectural designs, batch size, and model capacity shape training convergence. Extensive experiments across diverse network architectures, datasets, optimizers, and loss functions validate our theoretical bounds and demonstrate precise alignment between our theoretical predictions and empirical training dynamics.

</details>


### [152] [Dual-Adversarial Safety Alignment: Cultivating Intrinsic Threat Comprehension in LRMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09542)
*Hongli Shen,Shaopeng Fu,Qinbo Zhang,Jian Li,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AdvSafe双对抗框架，通过显式解构对抗机制让大型推理模型内化不安全知识，仅用1K合成样本即可在几乎不损失推理效用的前提下显著提升越狱鲁棒性，并能泛化到分布外攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型推理模型（LRMs）的安全对齐方法（如直接拒绝或安全rationales）往往关注提示词模式而非内在攻击机制，导致在面对多样化越狱攻击时泛化能力差，无法在对抗鲁棒性与推理效用之间取得良好平衡。

Method: AdvSafe采用两阶段对抗博弈：(1) 对抗合成阶段，自治智能体动态构造欺骗性越狱提示，尝试攻破强教师模型；(2) 对抗提取阶段，被攻破的教师模型执行认知级反攻，对每个成功越狱解构其伪装机制，解释攻击成功原因及识别与缓解方法，最终生成紧凑的推理数据集用于训练学生模型。

Result: 仅用1K合成样本，AdvSafe对齐的LRMs在越狱鲁棒性上显著优于现有基线，几乎无效用退化；同时在对分布外提示的鲁棒性上也表现出提升，验证了学习不安全知识可获得更优的鲁棒性-效用权衡，并泛化到未见过的攻击模式。

Conclusion: AdvSafe证明通过让模型内化不安全知识的认知机制而非仅学习攻击模式，可以实现更稳健、更可泛化的安全对齐，同时保持推理能力不受损。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) achieve remarkable success on complex tasks but remain vulnerable to harmful prompts that induce unsafe outputs. Recent methods align LRMs using direct refusals or safety rationales, yet often focus on prompt patterns rather than intrinsic attack mechanisms. As a result, these pattern-centric alignments struggle to generalize across diverse jailbreaks, compromising adversarial robustness and reasoning utility. We propose AdvSafe, a dual-adversarial framework that enables LRMs to internalize unsafety knowledge by explicitly deconstructing adversarial mechanisms. This moves beyond pattern-dependent traces, fostering robust cognitive defense without compromising reasoning utility. Our pipeline operates via a two-phase adversarial game. First, in adversarial synthesis, an autonomous agent dynamically crafts deceptive jailbreak prompts, adapting its strategies to breach a strong teacher model. Second, in adversarial extraction, the breached teacher executes a cognitive counter-attack. For every successful jailbreak, the teacher unmasks the camouflage, explaining why the attack succeeds and how such prompts can be identified and mitigated. This dual-adversarial process yields a compact reasoning dataset capturing rich, generalizable unsafety knowledge. Student models trained on this dataset implicitly acquire safety alignment through intrinsic threat comprehension. Experiments show that with only 1K synthesized samples, AdvSafe-aligned LRMs achieve significantly stronger jailbreak robustness than existing baselines, with almost no utility degradation. Furthermore, AdvSafe improves robustness against out-of-distribution prompts, demonstrating that learning unsafety knowledge enables a superior robustness-utility trade-off and generalizes beyond seen attack patterns.

</details>


### [153] [Training-Free Universal Approximation by Prompting Random Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09558)
*Alexander Hsu,Rongjie Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从逼近论角度证明，单层softmax注意力网络即使权重随机且未训练，只要配备合适的软提示（soft prompt），也能以极小极大最优速率逼近任意紧流形上的Hölder函数，从而表明预训练在表达能力上并非必需。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解transformer的表达能力对于区分提示（prompting）、架构和预训练各自的作用至关重要，并有助于判断任务特定行为是必须存储在模型权重中，还是可以在推理时通过提示诱导产生。回答"提示一个transformer的表达能力有多强"这一问题具有根本性的理论意义。

Method: 作者利用softmax注意力与核方法之间的联系，为每个目标函数构造显式软提示（每个提示独立于查询），将其作为线性系统的解，使注意力logits匹配高斯核指数。在此构造下，冻结的transformer模仿经典Nadaraya-Watson核估计器。构造仅需对权重满足一个温和的秩条件，作者证明该条件在高斯初始化下几乎必然成立。此外，作者量化了提示的成本，揭示了构造的软提示token范数、提示长度与隐藏维度之间的权衡。

Result: 提示网络继承了核回归的理论保证，得到通用逼近定理，其极小极大最优速率依赖于数据的固有维度。数值实验验证了构造方法和预测的收敛速率。

Conclusion: 本文表明在逼近论意义上预训练是可选的——随机未训练的transformer配合适当提示即可实现最优逼近性能，并从理论层面刻画了提示实现的代价与权衡关系。

Abstract: How expressive is prompting a transformer? Answering this question is important for separating the roles of prompting, architecture, and pretraining in transformer models, and for determining whether task-specific behavior must be stored in model weights or can instead be induced at inference time through the prompt. We show, in an approximation-theoretic sense, that pretraining is optional: a single-layer softmax attention network with random, untrained weights can approximate any Hölder function on a compact manifold when steered by an appropriate soft prompt. Guided by the connection between softmax attention and kernel methods, we construct explicit soft prompts (a prompt per target function, independent of the query) as solutions to linear systems matching attention logits to Gaussian kernel exponents, under which the frozen transformer emulates the classical Nadaraya-Watson kernel estimator. The construction requires only a mild rank condition on the weights, which we show holds almost surely under Gaussian initialization. The prompted network inherits the theoretical guarantees of kernel regression, leading to universal approximation theorems with minimax-optimal rates that depend on the intrinsic dimension. We further quantify the cost of prompting, exposing a tradeoff between the norm of the constructed soft prompt tokens, prompt length, and hidden dimension. Numerical experiments corroborate the constructions and predicted rates.

</details>


### [154] [Hyperbolic Multimodal Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09572)
*Jiahong Liu,Ming Shen,Xiaohao Liu,Rex Ying,Menglin Yang,Tat-Seng Chua,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从几何视角研究多模态持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，建立了双曲空间中表示保持的理论基础，提出了一种保留跨模态关系结构与层次几何的持续学习框架，实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲几何因其天然捕获跨模态层次语义结构的能力，已成功应用于多模态表示学习，但其在持续学习场景下的表示行为尚未被充分探索，需要理论层面的深入理解。

Method: 首先建立双曲空间中表示保持的理论基础，证明防止遗忘需要共享双曲等距变换下的跨模态不变性；进而揭示遗忘涉及语义关系漂移与层次相关失真两个维度；据此推导出保持几何结构同时允许适应新任务的持续学习框架。

Result: 在持续多模态基准任务上的实验证实了所提出方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 双曲多模态持续学习的遗忘问题可从几何结构角度系统性理解与缓解，同时保留跨模态关系结构和层次几何是实现有效持续学习的关键。

Abstract: Hyperbolic geometry has recently emerged as a powerful representation space for multimodal learning, as it naturally captures hierarchical semantic structure across modalities. Despite this progress, how such representations behave under continual learning poses fundamentally different challenges that remain underexplored. This work provides a geometric perspective on this problem and establishes a theoretical foundation for representation preservation in hyperbolic space, showing that preventing forgetting requires cross-modal invariance under a shared hyperbolic isometry. We further show that forgetting in hyperbolic continual learning involves both semantic relation drift and hierarchy-related distortion, motivating preservation of both cross-modal relational structure and hierarchical geometry. Guided by these insights, a principled continual learning framework is derived that preserves essential geometric structure while allowing effective adaptation to new tasks. Experiments on continual multimodal benchmarks corroborate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [155] [LEED: Local Embedding Evolution Distance for over-smoothing estimation and virtual node selection in GNN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09596)
*Killian Cressant,Pedro B. Velloso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LEED（Local Embedding Evolution Distance），一种基于节点级别的局部度量指标，用于量化GNN中的过平滑（over-smoothing）问题，并利用该指标指导虚拟节点（virtual node）的选择以缓解过挤压（over-squashing）问题，在多个数据集上提升了GNN性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN面临两个根本限制：过平滑（节点表示随层数增加变得不可区分）和过挤压（长距离信息通过有限的消息传递通道被压缩）。现有指标如Dirichlet能量只能提供全局层面的过平滑描述，缺乏节点级别的分辨率，无法用于分析节点行为或指导架构改进。

Method: 提出LEED这一局部度量指标，通过追踪每个节点嵌入在层间的演化来量化过平滑。LEED在节点级别操作，能对训练过程中的表征动态进行细粒度分析，揭示全局能量指标无法观测的异构过平滑模式。这种局部性产生节点重要性分数（解释为嵌入驱动的中心性度量），并将其作为唯一准则指导局部虚拟节点（Local Virtual Nodes）的构建以缓解过挤压问题。

Result: 实验表明，LEED比Dirichlet能量提供更具信息量的诊断结果，同时保留全局评估能力；并且LEED指导的虚拟节点集成更有效，在多个数据集上提升了GNN性能。

Conclusion: LEED作为一种节点级别的局部度量，既能为过平滑问题提供更精细的诊断信息，又能作为统一准则优化虚拟节点选择策略，同时缓解过平滑和过挤压两大GNN顽疾，为GNN架构改进提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) suffer from two fundamental limitations: over-smoothing, where node representations become indistinguishable with depth, and over-squashing, where long-range information is compressed through limited message-passing channels. Existing metrics such as Dirichlet energy provide global characterizations of over-smoothing but lack the resolution to analyze node-level behavior and guide architectural improvements. In this paper, we propose LEED (Local Embedding Evolution Distance), a novel local metric that quantifies over-smoothing by tracking the evolution of individual node embeddings across layers. By operating at the node level, LEED enables fine-grained analysis of representation dynamics during training, revealing heterogeneous over-smoothing patterns that are invisible to global energy-based measures. This locality induces informative node importance scores, interpreted as embedding-driven centrality measures. We leverage LEED to design a more efficient strategy for virtual node selection. Unlike existing approaches that depend on multiple heuristic centrality measures, our method uses LEED as a unique criterion to guide the construction of Local Virtual Nodes to mitigate over-squashing. Experiments show that LEED provides more informative diagnostics than Dirichlet energy while preserving global evaluation, and enables more effective virtual node integration, improving GNN performance across datasets.

</details>


### [156] [Bayesian Symbolic Regression with Entropic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09617)
*Oussama Boussif,Mohammed Mahfoud,Younesse Kaddar,Moksh Jain,Sida Li,Damiano Fornasiere,Xiaoyin Chen,Yoshua Bengio,Esmeralda S. Whitammer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ERRLESS，一种基于最大熵强化学习的可扩展方法，用于从符号回归中的表达式后验分布中进行采样。该方法通过学习神经策略逐步构建抽象语法树来表示表达式，收敛后策略能够从后验中采样表达式。在Feynman基准上取得竞争性结果，生成的表达式简短可解释，且后验预测均值相比SMC基线具有更高的R²。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统符号回归只搜索单个最佳拟合表达式，无法捕捉关于表达式的认知不确定性。这促使采用贝叶斯视角进行不确定性量化和利用自然先验约束搜索空间，但贝叶斯方法在可扩展性和采样效率上存在挑战。

Method: ERRLESS使用最大熵强化学习学习一个神经策略，该策略通过逐步构建抽象语法树来顺序构造表达式。算法训练过程中策略收敛到后验分布，测试时通过策略的rollout来采样表达式，从而实现贝叶斯符号回归中后验采样。

Result: 在Feynman基准测试上取得具有竞争力的结果，生成的表达式简短且可解释。与SMC基线相比，ERRLESS近似得到的后验预测均值实现了更高的决定系数（R²），展示了贝叶斯视角在符号回归中的优势。

Conclusion: ERRLESS验证了最大熵强化学习作为贝叶斯符号回归中后验采样工具的有效性，既能保持表达式质量，又能实现不确定性量化，为数据有限且含噪声的自然科学场景提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Symbolic regression is the problem of finding an algebraic expression describing a stochastic dependence of a target variable on a set of inputs. Unlike forms of regression that fit parameters assuming a fixed model structure, symbolic regression is a search problem over the space of expressions, represented, for example, as abstract syntax trees using a library of operators. Symbolic regression is typically used in settings with limited, noisy data in the natural sciences. However, searching for a single best-fitting expression fails to capture the epistemic uncertainty about the expression, which motivates a Bayesian perspective that enables uncertainty quantification and specification of natural priors to constrain the search space. In this work, we propose ERRLESS (Entropy-Regularized Reinforcement Learning for Expression Structure Sampling), a scalable approach for sampling from the posterior distribution over expressions given data using maximum-entropy reinforcement learning. ERRLESS learns a neural policy that constructs expressions sequentially by building up their abstract syntax trees. At convergence, the policy samples expressions from the posterior. At test time, expressions can be sampled by rollouts of this policy. We demonstrate that ERRLESS achieves competitive results on the Feynman benchmark while producing short and interpretable expressions. Additionally, we demonstrate that the mean of the posterior predictive approximated by ERRLESS achieves a high coefficient of determination ($R^2$) compared to an SMC baseline, highlighting the benefits of the Bayesian perspective in symbolic regression.

</details>


### [157] [Satellite Trajectory Optimization via Proximal Policy Optimization for Space Debris Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09628)
*Logan Luna,Juan Ortiz Couder,Raul Alejandro Vargas-Acosta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于近端策略优化（PPO）的强化学习自主避撞策略，用于低地球轨道（LEO）和地球静止轨道（GEO）航天器的碰撞规避。在1000个确定性GEO场景中，该智能体实现了97.5%的碰撞规避成功率，远超基于规则的基线（20.7%）和脉冲delta-v规划器基线（27.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型星座的发射，轨道碎片碰撞风险日益频繁，而当前的碰撞规避实践多为人工或基于规则的方法，难以扩展到日益恶化的动态轨道环境。

Method: 使用PPO强化学习算法训练自主碰撞规避策略，并配套开发了开源高保真天体动力学仿真器用于训练和评估。仿真器模拟牛顿二体动力学，包含日/月三体摄动、燃料相关推力以及可配置碎片场。智能体通过课程学习进行训练，并使用面向生存率、投影交会距离和delta-v节约设计的塑造奖励。

Result: 在1000个确定性GEO场景中达到97.5%的成功率，显著优于规则基线（20.7%）和脉冲delta-v规划器基线（27.5%）。评估采用完全确定性流水线，包含共享种子、逐场景日志和遥测导出。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了强化学习在自主航天器碰撞规避中的巨大潜力，并提供了一套公开可用的开源框架（https://purl.org/sat-trajectory-avoidance），为轨道安全领域提供了可复现的关键工具。

Abstract: Collision avoidance systems are commonly used to avoid fragmentation events occurring in Low-Earth Orbit (LEO) and Geosynchronous Equatorial Orbit (GEO). However, these events have been growing in frequency as orbital congestion worsens with the launch of megaconstellations. Consequently, conjunction alerts and collision risks are becoming increasingly common. Current practices, which are commonly manual or rule-based, have difficulty scaling to these worsening dynamic environments. To address this intensifying situation, we propose a reinforcement-learning policy for autonomous collision avoidance, trained via Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) along with an open-source, high-fidelity astrodynamics simulator for training and evaluation. In 1,000 deterministic GEO episodes, our agent achieves a 97.5% collision avoidance success rate, outperforming traditional controllers such as a rule-based baseline (20.7% success) and an impulsive delta-v planner baseline (27.5% success). To achieve these results, we designed a simulator to train and evaluate our agent, using real-world and simulated debris. We simulate Newtonian two-body dynamics using Sun/Moon third-body perturbations, fuel-dependent thrust, and configurable debris fields. The agent is trained with curriculum learning and shaped rewards oriented toward encouraging survival, adequate projected miss distance, and delta-v conservation. Finally, our evaluation consisted of a fully deterministic pipeline, including shared seeds, per-episode logs, and telemetry exports. Our work is a publicly available framework at https://purl.org/sat-trajectory-avoidance

</details>


### [158] [Deep Learning Imputation of Missing Radius of Maximum Winds (Rmax) Values in Tropical Cyclone Best-Track Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09683)
*Swastik Agrawal,Nishkal Hundia,Ziyue Liu,Michelle Bensi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了多种数据驱动方法（1DCNN、LSTM和传统机器学习）用于热带气旋最大风速半径（Rmax）缺失记录的插补，发现包含34节风速半径（R34）显著提升性能，时序模型在样本量少约一个数量级的情况下仍取得更高相关性，迁移学习因合成数据集与观测数据分布不一致而未带来改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率性海岸灾害评估需要准确表征热带气旋参数，但现有数据集中最大风速半径（Rmax）常存在大量缺失记录，而Rmax是联合概率方法（JPM）分析中的关键变量，缺失会影响灾害评估的准确性。

Method: 评估多种数据驱动插补方法，包括一维卷积神经网络（1DCNN）、长短期记忆网络（LSTM）及传统机器学习模型；采用物理信息输入增强（加入R34）、时序建模和迁移学习策略，利用合成RAFT和STORM数据集进行预训练，使用观测IBTrACS数据进行微调。

Result: ①包含R34显著提升所有模型的性能；②时序模型比非时序模型获得更高平均相关性，尽管其训练样本少约一个数量级，表明能更好地保留跨风暴的Rmax相对变异性；③当R34不可用时，时序模型优势更明显，说明时序信息可部分补偿风暴尺寸预测因子的缺失；④迁移学习未改善性能，原因是合成数据集的Rmax分布比IBTrACS更低且变异性更小。

Conclusion: 时序深度学习模型在重建不完整的热带气旋记录方面具有潜力，同时强调了物理信息输入、观测数据可用性和分布一致性在海岸灾害评估中的重要性。

Abstract: Probabilistic coastal hazard assessments require accurate characterization of tropical cyclone (TC) parameters, yet datasets often contain missing records for the radius of maximum winds (Rmax), a key variable in Joint Probability Method analyses. This study evaluates data-driven approaches for Rmax imputation, including one-dimensional Convolutional Neural Networks (1DCNNs), Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks, and conventional machine learning models. We examine physics-informed input augmentation, temporal modeling, and transfer learning using synthetic RAFT and STORM datasets for pre-training and observational IBTrACS data for fine-tuning. Including the radius of 34-knot winds (R34) substantially improves performance across all model types. Temporal models achieve higher average correlations than non-temporal models despite using approximately an order of magnitude fewer samples, indicating better preservation of relative Rmax variability across storms. This advantage is more pronounced when R34 is unavailable, suggesting temporal information can partially compensate for missing storm-size predictors. Transfer learning does not improve performance, likely because synthetic datasets have lower and less variable Rmax distributions than IBTrACS. These findings demonstrate the potential of temporal deep learning for reconstructing incomplete TC records and highlight the importance of physics-informed inputs, observational data availability, and distributional consistency in coastal hazard assessment.

</details>


### [159] [FedOrbit: Adaptive Personalized Federated Learning for Non-IID LEO Satellite Constellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09687)
*Satwat Bashir,Tasos Dagiuklas,Muddesar Iqbal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedOrbit是一种面向低地球轨道（LEO）卫星星座的联邦学习框架，通过持续的轨道级训练、类感知分层聚合、带返回率阻尼的质量加权特征聚合，以及基于轨道间类相似度的自适应特征分解，有效应对非独立同分布（non-IID）数据和地面站可见性不规则问题。在三个遥感基准和两种non-IID划分下，FedOrbit在六种设置中的五种达到最高精度，相比最强基线最高提升16.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星星座中的联邦学习受到轨道几何因素导致的非IID数据和地面站可见性不规则的双重影响。传统的全局聚合在轨道级类分布不重叠时表现不佳，而过强的个性化在类分布重叠时又显得过度，需要在全局聚合与个性化之间寻求自适应平衡。

Method: FedOrbit包含四个关键组件：（1）持续轨道级训练，利用星际链路在轨道内进行连续训练；（2）类感知分层聚合，考虑轨道间的类分布差异进行聚合；（3）质量加权特征聚合并引入返回率阻尼，处理地面站可见性不规则问题；（4）基于轨道间类相似度的自适应特征分解，动态调整全局与个性化的程度。

Result: 在三个遥感基准和两种non-IID划分下，FedOrbit在六种设置中的五种达到了最高精度，第六种设置中与最佳结果相差仅0.9个百分点。相比最强基线，在Dirichlet划分下最高提升16.1个百分点，在病理性划分下最高提升8.6个百分点，并且六种设置中有五种取得了最小的每轨道精度差异。

Conclusion: FedOrbit通过自适应地在全局聚合与轨道级个性化之间权衡，有效解决了LEO卫星星座联邦学习中由轨道几何引发的数据异质性问题，在精度和轨道间公平性方面均优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) in Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite constellations is affected by non-IID data and irregular ground-station visibility, both driven by orbital geometry. Global aggregation performs poorly when orbit-level class distributions are disjoint, while strong personalisation can be excessive when these distributions overlap. We present FedOrbit, which combines continuous orbit-level training over inter-satellite links, class-aware hierarchical aggregation, quality-weighted feature aggregation with return-rate dampening, and adaptive feature decomposition based on inter-orbit class similarity. Across three remote-sensing benchmarks and two non-IID partitions, FedOrbit achieves the highest accuracy in five of six settings and is within $0.9$ percentage points of the best result in the sixth. The gains over the strongest baseline reach $16.1$ percentage points under Dirichlet partitioning and $8.6$ under pathological partitioning, with the smallest per-orbit accuracy spread in five of six settings.

</details>


### [160] [Confusion-Geometry Rebalancing for Long-Tailed Adversarial Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09688)
*Mengnan Zhao,Geyong Min,Lihe Zhang,Tianhang Zheng,Jie Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种针对长尾分布下对抗训练的混淆几何重平衡方法（CGRm），利用有向鲁棒错误作为训练信号，结合反馈加权鲁棒优化与图引导的边界修正，缓解类别不平衡与对抗训练偏置的双重不平衡问题，在长尾基准上取得一致鲁棒性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾分布下的对抗训练面临双重不平衡：类别不平衡使训练目标偏向头部类别，且对抗内部最大化可能进一步放大这种偏置。现有方法通过修正类别先验或调整类别级鲁棒监督来缓解，但将每个类别孤立处理，未能识别驱动长尾崩溃的决策边界。

Method: CGRm是一个即插即用框架，核心包括：1）利用周期性鲁棒评估提取源类别损失权重、类别级鲁棒系数和有向混淆几何图；2）将反馈加权鲁棒优化与图引导的边界修正耦合，引导鲁棒优化优先提升脆弱类别的鲁棒性，并锐化驱动长尾性能下降的关键决策边界。

Result: 在长尾基准数据集上的实验表明，CGRm相对于现有方法取得了一致的鲁棒性能提升，消融实验验证了各组件（损失权重、鲁棒系数、混淆几何图）的贡献。

Conclusion: CGRm通过有向混淆几何图显式建模类别间的混淆关系，将反馈加权优化与边界修正结合，有效缓解了长尾对抗训练中的双重不平衡问题，且作为即插即用框架具备良好的通用性。

Abstract: Adversarial training under long tailed distributions suffers from a dual imbalance: the class imbalance skews the training objective toward head classes, and the adversarial inner maximization may further amplify this bias. Existing methods mitigate this issue by correcting class priors or adapting class wise robust supervision, yet they treat each class in isolation and fail to identify which boundaries drive long tailed collapse. We propose a Confusion Geometry Rebalancing method (CGRm) for long tail adversarial training, a plug in framework that leverages directed robust errors as training signals. CGRm leverages periodic robust evaluations to derive source class loss weights, class wise robust coefficients, and a directed confusion geometry graph. The method then couples feedback weighted robust optimization with graph guided margin correction, thereby boosting the robustness of vulnerable classes and sharpening the critical boundaries that drive long tailed performance degradation. Experiments on long tailed benchmarks show that CGRm achieves consistent robust performance gains over existing methods, with ablations validating the contribution of each component. We provide the code in the supplement.

</details>


### [161] [Recurrent Neural Networks Beyond Time: Learning from Multiple Ordered Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09690)
*Vagan Terziyan,Artur Terziian,Oleksandra Vitko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有序结构依赖假说（OSDH）和独立结构专家原则（ISEP），并实现为结构进化RNN（SE-RNNs），通过多个有序投影独立训练序列模型再融合，以挖掘单一序列顺序无法揭示的互补结构依赖关系。实验表明该方法在存在隐藏结构依赖的合成数据集上持续受益，且框架可扩展至RNN以外的架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNN虽广泛用于序列学习，但传统上被关联于时间数据。作者观察到循环计算本质上作用于有序序列而非时间本身，因此提出：同一观测数据的多种可行排序可能揭示单一序列组织无法获取的互补结构依赖。这一观察挑战了将序列顺序视为给定的传统思路。

Method: 提出OSDH假说，认为同一观测的多种可接受排序可揭示互补的结构依赖。在此基础上提出ISEP原则，即按投影独立训练序列模型，再通过专用融合模型整合其学习到的表示。具体实现为SE-RNNs：使用传统RNN作为投影特定结构专家，保持底层循环计算不变，仅通过多投影+融合机制获得提升。

Result: 在三个结构复杂度显著不同的合成数据集上的概念验证实验表明：当存在隐藏结构依赖时，SE-RNNs一致地从多个有序投影中获益；在较简单的数据集上仍具竞争力。由于OSDH与底层序列处理模型无关，该框架可自然扩展到RNN以外的替代架构。

Conclusion: 结果支持OSDH所主张的计算视角：利用互补的有序表示来解决多样化的结构化学习问题，为超越时间序列范式的序列建模提供了通用思路。

Abstract: Recurrent neural networks (RNNs) are widely used for sequence learning, yet their application is commonly associated with temporal data, although recurrent computation fundamentally operates on ordered sequences rather than on time itself. Building on this observation, we introduce the Ordered Structural Dependency Hypothesis (OSDH), which proposes that multiple admissible orderings of the same observations may reveal complementary structural dependencies inaccessible through a single sequential organization. To operationalize this hypothesis, we propose the Independent Structural Expert Principle (ISEP), whereby projection-specific sequence models are trained independently before their learned representations are integrated through a dedicated fusion model. As a concrete realization, we present Structural Evolution RNNs (SE-RNNs), which employ conventional RNNs as projection-specific structural experts while preserving the underlying recurrent computation unchanged. Proof-of-concept experiments on three synthetic datasets with substantially different levels of structural complexity demonstrate that the proposed architecture consistently benefits from multiple ordered projections when hidden structural dependencies are present, while remaining competitive on simpler datasets. Since OSDH is independent of the underlying sequence-processing model, the proposed framework naturally extends beyond recurrent networks and may be instantiated using alternative architectures. The results suggest a general computational perspective for exploiting complementary ordered representations across diverse structured learning problems.

</details>


### [162] [Evaluating Generative Time-Series Models on Data with Point Masses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09692)
*Jian Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了生成式时间序列模型在零膨胀数据（如无雨、无订单等大量单值数据）上进行基准测试时存在的严重问题：滚动起点协议可能产生与数据集原子结构完全失配的评估窗口，导致结论反转；通过构造CRPS不变的对照实验，证明时序耦合对统计量的贡献可被精确量化；在匹配协议下，自回归hurdle模型在六个数据集中的五个上击败条件流模型（最高达153倍），且流模型的发生率统计量在不同训练种子间可变化高达62%；五种发生率统计量给出了不同的模型排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式时间序列模型所评测的许多数据集中，大量概率质量集中于单一值（如零值），但现有评估协议未充分考虑这种原子结构（atom structure），可能导致评估结果失真甚至反转研究结论。作者旨在系统揭示这一问题并提供更严格的评估方法。

Method: 作者进行了四项研究：(1) 检查标准滚动起点协议下评估窗口的原子结构与数据集本身的一致性；(2) 构造CRPS在构造上不变的对照实验以隔离时序耦合的影响；(3) 在匹配协议下对五个种子上的七个模型进行基准测试，比较自回归hurdle模型与条件流模型的性能；(4) 使用五种不同的发生率统计量对模型进行排序，考察排序一致性。

Result: (1) 滚动起点协议严重失真：一个数据集42%的零值在评估窗口中只有13%，另一个数据集47%的零值在评估窗口只有5%；这导致作者自己的一项结论被反转。(2) 构造了CRPS不变的对照实验，可精确量化时序耦合对所选统计量的贡献。(3) 自回归hurdle模型在六个数据集中的五个上优于条件流模型，最高达153倍；流模型的发生率统计量在不同种子间变化高达62%。(4) 五种发生率统计量给出的模型排序不一致，且不共享构造的两个统计量之间的一致性最差。

Conclusion: 零膨胀数据的时间序列评估中存在被忽视的系统性问题：标准协议可能在结构失配的窗口上评分，导致错误结论；发生率统计量的选择会显著影响模型排序；评估时需同时匹配数据原子结构、使用多种发生率统计量，并考虑模型随机性（多种子）带来的发生率方差。

Abstract: Many of the series that generative time-series models are benchmarked on place a large probability mass on a single value --- it does not rain, no ride is requested, no part is ordered. We report what happens when such data is evaluated carefully. First, the standard rolling-origin protocol can score a model on a window whose atom structure bears no resemblance to the dataset: on one benchmark the dataset is $42\%$ zeros and the evaluation windows are $13\%$, on another $47\%$ against $5\%$. This is not a cosmetic problem --- it reversed one of our own conclusions, turning the strongest occurrence model in our study into what looked like a cautionary tale. Second, we give a control in which CRPS is invariant \emph{by construction} while the temporal coupling is destroyed, which measures exactly how much that coupling contributes to a chosen statistic. Third, benchmarking seven models on a matched protocol over five seeds, an autoregressive hurdle beats a conditional flow on five of six datasets, by up to a factor of $153$, while the flow's own occurrence statistics vary by up to $62\%$ across training seeds and every baseline is deterministic. Finally, the model ordering is not the same under five different occurrence statistics, and the two that do not share a construction agree with each other least.

</details>


### [163] [PET/CT Radiogenomic Mutation Prediction in Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer Using Multi-Label Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09721)
*Mona Furukawa,Sai Hyne,Daniel R. McGowan,Bartłomiej W. Papież*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究首次系统探索了基于深度学习的PET/CT影像基因组学方法预测NSCLC中EGFR、TP53和KRAS基因突变，并评估了成对多标签学习相比传统单基因分类在突变预测中的效果。实验在英国新型放射基因组学队列上进行，发现多标签学习的有效性依赖于具体基因突变组合，KRAS/TP53联合预测有显著提升，而EGFR/TP53组合无改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌仍是全球癌症相关死亡的主要原因之一。虽然靶向治疗改善了NSCLC患者的预后，但依赖组织活检进行突变检测这一侵入性手段存在诸多局限。因此，亟需开发基于无创影像（PET/CT）的深度学习方法来预测基因突变状态，并探索更优的预测建模策略。

Method: 本研究采用PET/CT影像基因组学方法，利用深度学习对NSCLC患者的EGFR、TP53和KRAS三种基因突变进行预测。实验在新型英国放射基因组学队列上进行，将成对多标签学习（pairwise multi-label learning）与传统单基因分类方法进行对比，评估联合预测对突变检测性能的影响。

Result: KRAS与TP53的联合预测将KRAS的AUC从0.58提升至0.64，TP53的AUC从0.69提升至0.71。对于EGFR/KRAS组合，仅EGFR从联合学习中获益；而EGFR/TP53组合未观察到任何改善。

Conclusion: 多标签学习的有效性高度依赖于所建模的具体基因突变组合，并非所有组合都能从中获益。研究提示，对于PET/CT影像基因组学突变预测，采用突变特异性（mutation-specific）的建模策略可能优于通用的一刀切方法。

Abstract: Lung cancer remains one of the leading causes of cancer- related mortality worldwide. Although targeted therapies have improved outcomes for patients with non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC), they rely on mutation profiling through tissue biopsy, an invasive procedure with several limitations. This study investigates PET/CT-based radio- genomic prediction of epidermal growth factor receptor (EGFR), tumour protein 53 (TP53), and Kirsten rat sarcoma viral oncogene (KRAS) mutations using deep learning. We further evaluate whether pairwise multi-label learning improves mutation prediction compared with conventional single-gene classification. To the best of our knowledge, this is among the first studies to systematically investigate multi-label learning for PET/CT radiogenomic mutation prediction in NSCLC. Experiments were conducted on a novel UK-based radiogenomics cohort. Joint pre- diction of KRAS and TP53 improved AUC from 0.58 to 0.64 for KRAS and from 0.69 to 0.71 for TP53. For the EGFR/KRAS pair, only EGFR benefited from joint learning, while no improvement was observed for the EGFR/TP53 pair. These findings demonstrate that the effectiveness of multi-label learning depends on the specific combination of gene mutations being modelled, suggesting that mutation-specific modelling strategies may be preferable for PET/CT radiogenomic prediction.

</details>


### [164] [Rethinking Factor Sharing in Federated LoRA: A Rank-Aware Adaptive Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09742)
*Xinyi Xu,Bingnan Xiao,Shuang Qin,Gang Feng,Tony Q. S. Quek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedAS-LoRA，一种自适应因子共享的联邦低秩适配方法，通过RSS指标在训练前自动选择共享A或共享B的策略，以最小化跨客户端的投影残差，从而提升联邦微调性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA在联邦学习中存在两种因子共享策略（Share-A/Local-B和Share-B/Local-A），二者因LoRA因子（A和B)的非对称角色会产生不同的投影残差，目前缺乏对何时选择哪种共享策略的深入分析。

Method: 通过最小二乘代理模型揭示两种共享策略分别要求客户端共享输入侧或输出侧秩r空间，从而导致不同投影残差；据此提出RSS指标，利用冻结LLM骨干提取的表示来评估共享秩r输入子空间是否足以覆盖本地数据分布，在训练前自适应选择共享侧。

Result: 实验覆盖不同任务、数据分布、LoRA秩和客户端参与设置，验证了RSS指标的有效性以及FedAS-LoRA在联邦微调性能上的优越性。

Conclusion: 共享策略的选择应由聚合的投影残差决定，FedAS-LoRA通过在训练前准确评估共享子空间充分性来自适应选择最优共享侧，显著提升联邦LoRA微调效果。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) represents large language model (LLM) updates with two compact matrix factors, i.e., $A$ and $B$, providing an efficient way to fine-tune large models in federated learning paradigm. Inspired by the asymmetric roles of the LoRA factors, we study whether $A$ should be shared across clients while $B$ remains client-specific (Share-A/Local-B), or whether $B$ should instead be shared while $A$ remains client-specific (Share-B/Local-A). With a least-squares surrogate, we reveal that Share-A/Local-B requires the client-specific LoRA update matrices to use a common rank-$r$ input-side space, whereas Share-B/Local-A requires a common rank-$r$ output-side space. The two strategies therefore incur different projection residuals, indicating that the preferred strategy is the one with the smaller aggregate residual across clients. With this insight, we propose Federated Adaptive Factor Sharing Low-Rank Adaptation (FedAS-LoRA), which selects the sharing side before training to enhance fine-tuning performance. To enable adaptive factor selection before training, we design a Rank-Aware Shared-Subspace Sufficiency (RSS) metric, which effectively assesses whether a shared rank-$r$ input subspace is sufficient for the local data distributions using representations extracted from a frozen LLM backbone. Experiments across different tasks, data distributions, LoRA ranks, and participation settings confirm the effectiveness of RSS and the superior performance of FedAS-LoRA.

</details>


### [165] [SR-OPSD: Self-Referenced On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09745)
*Zhuo Sun,Entong Li,Yanlong Zhao,Xiaoyuan Cheng,Wenxuan Yuan,Kaiyu Li,Che Liu,Huihang Liu,Harrison Bo Hua Zhu,Li Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SR-OPSD通过在自蒸馏过程中引入参考策略与Rényi散度族，解决了OPSD中教师策略与目标分布动态演化导致的优化不稳定性问题，在科学评估、数学推理和代码生成等任务上达到SOTA或竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD利用密集的token级监督信号补充稀疏结果奖励，但其自教师策略与学生策略及上下文分布共同演化，是一个移动目标，直接以固定投影目标匹配会导致优化不稳定或过度的分布集中问题。

Method: SR-OPSD在固定的学生生成上下文下，通过token级变分表征将有效蒸馏目标识别为自教师策略与参考策略之间的几何插值；同时引入Rényi散度族推广投影几何。插值系数控制自适应目标的底层位置，Rényi阶数则控制投影几何及其对token级密度比的敏感性，从而将"目标放置在哪里"与"学生如何投影到目标"两个维度解耦。

Result: 在科学评估、数学推理和代码生成任务上，使用多种大语言模型进行的广泛实验表明，SR-OPSD在各种设置下达到了SOTA或竞争性的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种理论驱动的OPSD改进方案，通过参考锚点与灵活投影几何的引入，有效解决了移动目标蒸馏的不稳定性问题，为自蒸馏在LLM上的应用提供了更稳健的框架。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) converts feedback into dense token-level supervision on trajectories generated by the policy to be optimized, providing a useful complement to reinforcement learning with sparse outcome rewards. However, the self-teacher policy used in OPSD is typically a stop-gradient or exponential-moving-average copy of the policy conditioned on additional context information, and thus co-evolves with both the student policy and its on-policy context distribution. Directly matching such a moving target with a fixed projection objective can lead to unstable optimization or excessive distributional concentration. This nature of OPSD motivates the proposed \emph{Self-Referenced On-Policy Self-Distillation (SR-OPSD)}. At fixed student-generated contexts, a token-level variational characterization identifies the effective distillation target as a geometric interpolation between the self-teacher policy and a reference policy. Meanwhile, we use the Rényi divergence family to generalize the projection geometry. This formulation separates \emph{where} the adaptive target is placed from \emph{how} the student is projected toward it: the interpolation coefficient controls underlying target, while the Rényi order controls the projection geometry and its sensitivity to token-level density ratios. Extensive experiments across scientific evaluation, mathematical reasoning, and coding generation tasks with multiple large language models show that SR-OPSD achieves the state-of-the-art or competitive performance across various settings.

</details>


### [166] [MoNo: Multiscale Optimal Transport Neural Operator for Solving PDEs on General Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09764)
*Zijiang Yang,Xiaomeng Wu,Dongmei Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MoNo（多尺度最优传输神经算子），一种通过CoTAP（跨尺度最优传输分配与投影）构建稳定多层潜在空间的神经算子，用于高效求解一般几何上的偏微分方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的神经算子在将观测点投影到潜在token时，可学习的投影机制无法保证稳定且均衡的分配，导致部分token过度分配而其他token利用率不足；且分配不平衡在多尺度层级架构中会被持续继承和放大，导致深层空间出现严重的token塌缩，限制了层次化架构的设计。

Method: 提出CoTAP方法，将相邻潜在空间之间的跨空间分配建模为熵正则化的最优传输问题，从而构建均衡的双向投影和稳定的潜在空间；CoTAP确保信息在多个潜在空间间的稳定传递，进而支持在一般几何上构建多尺度架构，以更高效地学习长程物理相互作用。

Result: 大量实验表明，MoNo在预测性能和计算效率两方面均优于现有的最先进神经算子。

Conclusion: MoNo通过在潜在空间构建中引入最优传输理论，有效解决了token分配失衡与塌缩问题，在一般几何上的PDE求解中取得了显著的性能与效率提升。

Abstract: Transformer-based neural operators have achieved substantial progress in solving Partial Differential Equations (PDEs) by projecting spatial observations into compact latent tokens and learning physical interactions in latent spaces. However, we reveal that existing learnable projection mechanisms cannot ensure stable and balanced assignments from observation points to latent tokens, causing some latent tokens to be over-assigned while others remain underutilized. This limitation further restricts the design of hierarchical architectures, as assignment imbalance is continuously inherited and amplified across latent spaces, eventually causing severe token collapse in deeper spaces. To address these issues, we propose MoNo (Multiscale Optimal Transport Neural Operator), a progressive multiscale neural operator that efficiently solves PDEs on general geometries through stable latent-space construction. At its core is CoTAP (Cross-scale Optimal Transport Assignment and Projection), a novel latent-space construction method that formulates cross-space assignment between adjacent spaces as an entropy-regularized optimal transport problem, thereby constructing balanced bidirectional projections and stable latent spaces. CoTAP also ensures stable information transfer across multiple latent spaces, further enabling multiscale architectures on general geometries, which in turn support more efficient learning of long-range physical interactions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MoNo outperforms existing state-of-the-art neural operators in both prediction performance and computational efficiency. Code is available at https://github.com/ZijiangY1116/MoNo.

</details>


### [167] [ReliableNet: A Chance-Constrained Approach to Trustworthy Classification in Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09768)
*Ange-Clément Akazan,Ineza Remy Mugenga,Abebe Geletu,Jean Medard Ngnotchouye,Issa Karambal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReliableNet，通过机会约束ERM直接限制"自信且错误"（JCW）联合概率低于用户指定风险预算α，解决了传统ERM、校准、不确定性估计、共形风险控制和选择性预测等方法无法在训练中直接约束该联合失效事件的问题。在四个表格和两个图像数据集上，ReliableNet是唯一在所有种子和分布内设置下均通过JCW预算认证的方法，并在多种分布偏移下取得最低经验JCW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既自信又错误的预测是关键的可靠性失效，因为当模型出错时它恰恰会绕过弃权和人工审查。ERM仅控制平均损失，校准、不确定性估计、共形风险控制和选择性预测等方法虽针对相关可靠性属性，但未能在训练过程中直接约束联合失效事件，即预测同时自信且错误的概率。

Method: 将JCW约束建模为机会约束ERM问题，采用保守的光滑内近似，其总体可行性蕴含原始JCW约束的满足。通过优化算法在训练过程中显式地将JCW概率约束在用户指定的风险预算α之下。

Result: 在四个表格和两个图像数据集上，与ERM、事后校准、共形风险控制和选择性预测等基线相比，ReliableNet是唯一在所有数据集和随机种子下内在分布中通过JCW预算认证的方法。在人口统计、模糊性、虚假相关、新类别和协变量偏移下，它取得最低的经验JCW，同时在准确率、覆盖率、校准和选择性预测方面保持很强竞争力。风险覆盖率结果进一步表明其在多数数据集上具有更好的选择性排序能力。

Conclusion: ReliableNet为可信分类提供了一种有原则的方法，能够直接在训练过程中约束"自信且错误"的风险，在保持整体性能竞争力的同时显著提升可靠的失效控制能力。

Abstract: A prediction that is both confident and wrong is a critical reliability failure because it can bypass abstention and human review precisely when the model is mistaken. Empirical risk minimization (ERM) controls average loss but not this failure directly, while calibration, uncertainty estimation, conformal risk control, and selective prediction methods target related reliability properties rather than bounding the joint failure event during training. We propose ReliableNet, which constrains the Joint Confident-Wrong (JCW) probability, the probability that a prediction is simultaneously confident and incorrect, below a user-specified risk budget $α\in(0,1)$. We formulate this as a chance-constrained ERM problem, use a conservative smooth inner approximation whose population feasibility implies the original JCW constraint. Across four tabular and two image datasets, ReliableNet is the only method certified within the JCW budget for every dataset and seed in distribution, when compared against baselines spanning ERM, post-hoc calibration, conformal risk control, and selective prediction. Under demographic, ambiguity, spurious-correlation, novel-class, and covariate shifts, it achieves the lowest empirical JCW among the compared methods while remaining very competitive in accuracy, coverage, calibration, and selective prediction. Risk-coverage results further indicate that ReliableNet achieves better selective ranking than the benchmark methods on most datasets. Overall, ReliableNet provides a principled approach to trustworthy classification.

</details>


### [168] [Parameter Exploration for RLVR via Variational Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09805)
*Vatsal Venkatkrishna,Nico Daheim,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出参数空间探索方法族3PO（Perturbed Parameter Policy Optimization），通过在参数空间中对策略采样生成不同rollout来补充动作空间的探索控制，在OLMo-3-1025-7B和Qwen2.5-Math-7B上的数学推理与代码生成任务中一致优于标准GRPO，且FLOPs开销相近，同时减少零优势组和畸形rollout的出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM强化学习探索方法大多在动作空间进行（如温度缩放），但这些方法只能影响输出分布的方差，无法重新排序token，限制了探索能力，可能导致训练发散或停滞。因此需要探索参数空间的探索机制作为补充控制杠杆。

Method: 提出3PO方法族，核心做法是从后验中采样多个不同的策略参数（扰动参数），用不同参数策略生成各异的rollout，并通过不同的采样策略和rollout分组策略来估计奖励。通过调节参数采样的多样性，实现对探索强度的互补控制。

Result: 在OLMo-3-1025-7B和Qwen2.5-Math-7B上的数学推理和代码生成实验中，3PO方法一致提升平均下游性能，且在相近FLOPs成本下优于标准GRPO。多个参数样本显著减少了训练中零优势组以及畸形或错误rollout的数量。

Conclusion: 参数空间探索能够有效改善LLM强化学习，且与动作空间探索互补，为LLM RL的探索策略提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Exploration has been a focus of reinforcement learning research for a long time. Recently, there has been growing evidence that it is also an important ingredient in LLM reinforcement learning recipes that can significantly impact downstream performance. Many existing methods control exploration in the action-space, for example, using temperature scaling. However, these methods cannot reorder tokens but only influence the variance in the output distribution. This limits exploration and can lead to divergence or stalled training. Here, we investigate parameter-space exploration, where rollouts are generated by sampling different policies from a posterior that may each explore different rollouts. Sampling less or more diverse policies is then a complementary control lever over exploration. We introduce a family of methods called Perturbed Parameter Policy Optimization (3PO) which use different sampling strategies and different rollout grouping for reward estimation. Experiments on OLMo-3-1025-7B and Qwen2.5-Math-7B across mathematical reasoning and code generation tasks show that these approaches consistently improve average downstream performance over standard GRPO at a near-identical FLOPs cost. Moreover, using multiple parameter samples consistently produces fewer zero-advantage groups and malformed or incorrect rollouts during training than GRPO and action-space baselines. Overall, our work presents evidence that parameter-space exploration can improve reinforcement learning for LLMs.

</details>


### [169] [Macaron-V1: Towards Open Continual Learning with Self-Improvement and Mixture-of-LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09819)
*Mind Lab,:,Vin Bo,Asher Cai,Jingwei Cao,Song Cao,Vic Cao,Amelia Chen,Andrew Chen,Kaijie Chen,Cleon Cheng,Steven Chiang,Kaixuan Fan,Hera Feng,Huan Feng,Arthur Fu,Jun Gao,Pyke Han,Nolan Ho,Ori Hong,Hailee Hou,Piers Hua,Charles Huang,Miles Jiang,Nora Jiang,Yuyi Jiang,Qiuyu Jin,Fancy Kong,Kuss Koo,Jaron Lee,Andrew Lei,Alexy Li,Dawn Li,Lucian Li,Ray Li,Ricardo Li,Smith Li,Theo Li,Allen Lin,Elliot Lin,Fan Lin,Chen Ling,Kairus Liu,Kieran Liu,Logan Liu,Neo Liu,Xiang Liu,Yuxin Lu,Maeve Luo,Pony Ma,Verity Niu,Cole Qiao,Guian Qiu,Vince Qu,Sentry,Niko Song,Vincent Wang,Bo Wu,Rio Yang,Evelyn Ye,Fiona Ye,Ina Ye,Regis Ye,Josh Ying,Atlas Zeng,Danney Zeng,Salmon Zhan,Anya Zhang,Di Zhang,Mia Zhang,Sueky Zhang,Wei Zhao,Ada Zhou,Adrian Zhou,Yuhua Zhou,Juno Zhu,Murphy Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Macaron-V1是一个面向"体验智能"的开放智能体模型家族，通过版本化模型-控制器对的递归改进实现持续适应，并通过Mixture-of-LoRA（MoL）架构实现多专家协作。旗舰版Venti基于744B GLM-5.2基础模型搭配四个LoRA专家（聊天、智能体、编程、GenUI），小版本Tall（50B）基于Qwen3.6用于本地部署。系统在个人智能、GenUI和通用能力基准上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体模型缺乏从真实环境体验中持续学习的能力，部署后无法适应新任务和环境变化。同时，单一模型难以同时兼顾多领域专长，需要一种既能跨领域协作又支持持续学习的模型架构与训练范式。

Method: 本文提出两个系统设计目标并通过三方面协同实现：（1）适应：通过版本化模型-控制器对（Model-Harness Co-design）递归改进，在外部合约（HCP）下评估经验并构建后继版本；（2）协作：采用Mixture-of-LoRA（MoL）架构，冻结基础模型、组合专职LoRA适配器，每轮用户交互选择一个LoRA。算法层面整合了递归自我改进循环、UI4A组件级GenUI控制器、状态化行为基座、版本化HCP合约及MindForge智能体RL框架。基础设施包括MinT后训练平台、LongStraw长上下文RL方法以及针对稀疏MoE和DSA基础模型的稳定性技术。

Result: Macaron-V1在个人智能、GenUI和通用能力基准上与前沿基线对比，实验结果验证了当前系统设计的有效性。持续学习和集体智能带来的复合增益被识别为有待开放探索的问题。

Conclusion: Macaron-V1展示了以架构、算法和基础设施协同设计的方式实现体验智能的可行性，MoL架构为持续学习提供了可扩展的LoRA专家机制。未来的关键方向在于持续学习的复合增益与集体智能效应，这两方面仍属开放问题。该工作还强调了模型规格（744B vs 50B）与应用场景（云端vs本地）的适配设计。

Abstract: Macaron-V1 is an open agent-model family for experiential intelligence: learning from experience in real environments and continuing to learn after deployment. It is organized around two system goals. Adaptation is pursued through recursive improvement of versioned model-harness pairs, where experience from one configuration is evaluated under an external contract and used to construct its successor. Collaboration is pursued via the Mixture-of-LoRA (MoL) architecture that freezes a base model, composes specialist LoRA adapters, and selects one LoRA per user turn. The flagship Macaron-V1-Venti combines a 744B GLM-5.2 base with four LoRAs for chat, agent, coding, and GenUI; the Qwen3.6-based Macaron-V1-Tall (50B) uses the same design for local deployment. This report presents Macaron-V1 as a co-designed system spanning architecture, algorithms, and infrastructure. The MoL architecture supports continual learning through extensible LoRA specialists.
