The algorithm combines Model-Harness Co-design and recursive self-improvement loop, including the UI4A component-native GenUI harness, a stateful action substrate, versioned HCP contract, and the agentic RL framework MindForge. The supporting infrastructure includes the post-training platform MinT, the long-context RL method LongStraw, and stability techniques for sparse MoE and DSA base models. We evaluate Macaron-V1 on Personal Intelligence, GenUI, and general capability benchmarks against frontier baselines. Our results validate the current system, while compounding gains from continual learning and collective intelligence remain open questions.

</details>


### [170] [Distill Skills into Weights, Not Prompts: Abstract Skills as Privileged Signals for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09826)
*Yubo Jiang,Fengying Xie,Zhiguo Jiang,Haopeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SKALD（技能锚定潜在蒸馏）框架，解决基于可验证奖励的强化学习中rollout组全部正确或全部错误时（占实验组的63.0-68.0%）无法提供组间相对信号的问题。SKALD通过问句仅学生模型与技能卡条件教师模型的双视图自蒸馏，在0.6B、1.7B和4B规模上分别比GRPO提升avg@8达+2.46、+4.85和+12.01。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（如GRPO）依赖组内相对信号进行优势估计，但当rollout组全部正确或全部错误时（占63.0-68.0%的实验组），无法产生有效的组间相对信号，导致训练信号稀疏甚至失效。现有方法缺乏在奖励完全无区分性的情况下持续提供学习信号的手段。

Method: SKALD是一种on-policy自蒸馏框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 双上下文视图：仅基于问题的学生模型与基于抽象技能卡（经显式答案过滤）的教师模型，共享同一Qwen3-Base参数；(2) 退火指数倾斜目标：通过衰减的倾斜因子降低教师偏好但学生似然极低的token权重，倾斜消失时收敛到教师交叉熵并恢复前向KL学生梯度，以稳定上下文导致的分布不匹配；(3) 经验门控：仅当验证rollout估计出正教师优势时才激活蒸馏。

Result: 在五个数学基准的held-out测试中，SKALD相比GRPO在avg@8上分别提升+2.46（0.6B）、+4.85（1.7B）和+12.01（4B）。在1.7B规模上，仅零方差组蒸馏即可恢复完整收益的84.7%；SKALD比FLOP匹配的GRPO高出+4.06，比上下文技能暴露方法高出+3.77。

Conclusion: 抽象技能卡能在组间相对奖励失去信息量的场景下提供密集监督信号。SKALD通过自蒸馏将技能优势转化为共享参数，无需在测试时使用特权输入，同时用退火倾斜目标和经验门控保证训练稳定性，验证了技能锚定蒸馏作为一种有效的补充训练范式的价值。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards yields no group-relative signal when rollout groups are uniformly correct or uniformly wrong, which account for 63.0-68.0% of groups in our experiments. We propose SKALD (Skill-Anchored Latent Distillation), an on-policy self-distillation framework that uses two context views of the same Qwen3-Base model: a question-only student and a teacher conditioned on an abstract, explicit-answer-filtered skill card. The student is trained on its own prefixes, transferring the skill-induced advantage into shared parameters without privileged input at test time. To stabilize context-induced distribution mismatch, SKALD employs an annealed exponentially tilted objective that downweights teacher-preferred tokens with very low student likelihood; as the tilt vanishes, it converges to teacher cross-entropy and recovers the forward-KL student gradient. An empirical gate activates distillation only when verified rollouts estimate a positive teacher advantage. Across five held-out mathematics benchmarks, SKALD improves overall avg@8 over GRPO by +2.46, +4.85, and +12.01 at 0.6B, 1.7B, and 4B, respectively. At 1.7B, zero-variance-only distillation recovers 84.7% of the full gain, while SKALD remains +4.06 above FLOP-matched GRPO and exceeds contextual skill exposure by +3.77. These results show that abstract skills provide dense supervision where group-relative rewards become uninformative.

</details>


### [171] [Multi-Agent AI Safety as an Institutional Design Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09828)
*Abdullah X*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: POLIS研究项目的第一篇论文，通过冻结的5,280集研究套件系统性地探究多智能体系统中AI制度的哪些部分产生安全性。研究发现：详细宪法提示词和溯源感知可执行守卫均实现0/384违规，但机制不同；本地状态守卫在洗钱场景中22/96违规，而溯源执行为0/96（p=4.77×10^-7）；揭示资源上限数值会改变智能体请求行为。结论是相同违规率可隐藏不同机制，制度中权威状态和封禁后的路径同样关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体越来越多地在委派任务、移动信息、执行行动和共享资源等方面受制度约束工作，已有研究表明部署规则可以改变集体行为，但尚不清楚AI制度的哪些部分产生安全性以及如何产生安全。

Method: 采用预注册的实验设计，构建冻结的5,280集研究套件。主委派实验覆盖四个模型家族，另增三个模型端点用于高冲突诊断。在匹配的结构化工作流中，模型看到不同规则表述，守卫咨询不同权威状态；同时改变即时合规内部回退的吸引力，并允许被阻止的工作流继续。测试了详细宪法提示词、溯源感知可执行守卫和本地状态守卫等不同的安全保障机制。

Result: 详细宪法提示词实现0/384违规；溯源感知可执行守卫同样0/384违规（在51/384集阻止非法尝试，其中44/51后续安全完成）；本地状态守卫的失败集中在普通变换改变可见策略但原始权威固定的场景。在匹配的洗钱场景中，本地状态守卫22/96违规，溯源强制0/96（p=4.77×10^-7）。资源分配实验显示揭示数值上限会改变智能体请求。

Conclusion: 相同的最终违规率可能隐藏截然不同的机制。规则本身只是制度的一部分：系统信任的权威状态至关重要，封禁后可供走的路径也同样重要。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly work inside systems that govern how they delegate tasks, move information, execute actions, and use shared resources. Recent work already shows that deployment rules can change collective behavior. Here we ask which parts of an AI institution produce safety and how they do it. This is the first paper from POLIS, an ongoing research programme studying algorithmic institutions for multi-agent systems. We report a frozen 5,280-episode study suite. The main pre-specified delegation experiment spans four model families; a targeted high-conflict diagnostic adds three additional model endpoints. In matched structured workflows, the model sees different rule formulations and guards consult different authority states. We also vary the attractiveness of the immediate compliant internal/self fallback and allow blocked workflows to continue. A detailed constitutional prompt produces 0/384 realized violations. A provenance-aware executable guard also produces 0/384, although it blocks prohibited attempts in 51/384 episodes; 44/51 of those episodes later complete safely. The local-state guard's failures concentrate in scenarios where an ordinary transformation changes visible policy while originating authority stays fixed. In matched laundering scenarios, that guard admits violations in 22/96 episodes and provenance enforcement in 0/96 (p = 4.77 x 10^-7). A separate resource-allocation experiment shows that revealing the numerical value of an otherwise identical cap changes agent requests. In these structured workflows, the same final violation rate can hide very different mechanisms. The rule itself is only part of the institution. The authority state the system trusts matters, and so does the path available after a block.

</details>


### [172] [Deep Multimodal Wearable Sensor Fusion for Detection of Body-Focused Repetitive Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09830)
*Samaneh Rezaeimanesh,Mohsen Behradfar,Mohammad Fili,Guiping Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种多模态深度学习框架，利用腕戴式传感器（Helios）的惯性测量单元、热电堆传感器和飞行时间传感器数据，对躯体聚焦重复行为（如拔发、抠皮肤）进行检测和分类。模型在二元检测中达到F1=0.985、AUC=0.997，在九分类任务中达到宏平均F1=0.700、AUC=0.963，并验证了多模态融合的优越性及可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 躯体聚焦重复行为（如拔发、抠皮肤）是常见的强迫性运动行为，与强迫症和焦虑障碍密切相关。但由于这些动作细微且与正常手势重叠，早期客观检测十分困难，亟需一种能够从佩戴式传感器中自动识别和分类这些行为的解决方案。

Method: 框架采用卷积神经网络（CNN）结合门控循环单元（GRU）作为核心架构，配合模态特定的自编码器和晚期融合分类器，以利用时序和空间动态信息。输入数据来自Child Mind Institute使用Helios腕戴设备收集的多模态传感器信号，涵盖运动学（IMU）、热学（热电堆）和近距（ToF）信息。还使用基于Shapley加性解释（SHAP）的事后可解释性分析和层次聚类来分析模型决策和错误模式。

Result: 二元检测任务中F1分数为0.985，ROC曲线下面积（AUC）为0.997；九分类方案（区分每种行为与单个分组的非目标类）中宏平均F1为0.700，AUC为0.963，优于单模态基线。可解释性分析显示飞行时间和惯性模态占主导判别力，层次聚类揭示误分类主要由手势的解剖学区域驱动。

Conclusion: 多模态传感器融合能够实现对躯体聚焦重复行为的准确、客观和连续监测。该工作为实时可穿戴辅助心理健康诊断和个性化干预奠定了坚实基础，在生物医学研究和临床护理中具有广阔应用前景。

Abstract: Body-focused repetitive behaviors, such as hair pulling and skin picking, are compulsive motor actions commonly associated with obsessive-compulsive and anxiety disorders. Their early, objective detection remains difficult because the movements are subtle and overlap with ordinary, non-pathological gestures. We developed and evaluated a multimodal deep learning framework to detect and classify these behaviors from wrist-worn sensor data. The data, collected by the Child Mind Institute using the Helios wrist-worn device, combine inertial measurement units, thermopile sensors, and time-of-flight sensors, capturing kinematic, thermal, and proximity information. The framework combined a convolutional neural network with a gated recurrent unit, alongside modality-specific autoencoders and a late-fusion classifier, to exploit temporal and spatial dynamics. It achieved an F1 score of 0.985 and an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.997 for binary detection, distinguishing these behaviors from other activities, and a macro-averaged F1 score of 0.700 with an area under the curve of 0.963 across a nine-class scheme that distinguished each individual behavior from a single grouped Non-Target class, improving over single-modality baselines. Post-hoc interpretability based on Shapley additive explanations showed that the time-of-flight and inertial modalities dominated discriminative power by capturing spatial proximity and dynamic movement, while hierarchical clustering indicated that misclassifications were driven primarily by the anatomical region of the gesture. These findings demonstrate that multimodal sensor fusion enables accurate, objective, and continuous behavioral monitoring. This work establishes a foundation for real-time, wearable-assisted mental health diagnostics and personalized interventions in biomedical research and clinical care.

</details>


### [173] [Real-Time Climate Risk Assessment for Supply Chain Resilience: A Data-Driven Nowcasting Framework for Colombian Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09846)
*Hernan J. Silva-Sosa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数据驱动临近预报技术（nowcasting）的实时气候风险评估方法论框架，用于提升哥伦比亚农业供应链的韧性。该框架将短期气候预报与供应链风险建模相结合，构建了概念性早期预警系统架构，并通过受控计算环境中的原型实现验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哥伦比亚气候变异性显著（降雨不规则、温度波动、极端事件频发），对农业生产和物流尤其是时效敏感作物产生直接影响，现有方法缺乏将实时气候临近预报与供应链决策集成的系统性框架。

Method: 该方法整合了基于历史气象观测的短期气候预报与供应链风险建模，通过显式风险映射、阈值分类和面向利益相关者的风险信号，建立了概念性早期预警系统架构。原型基于官方统计和再分析产品的时间序列数据实现，不依赖卫星影像或计算机视觉组件。

Result: 合成数据与历史数据实验表明，短期降水临近预报可转化为农业供应链的可行动风险指标，支持库存、采购和运输相关的预期性决策。

Conclusion: 研究表明该框架能够将气候临近预报与供应链决策有效集成，为哥伦比亚农业供应链的气候风险实时评估提供了可行路径，有助于提升供应链韧性并支持前瞻性决策。

Abstract: This paper presents a methodological framework for real-time climate risk assessment using data-driven nowcasting techniques to enhance supply chain resilience in Colombian agricultural contexts. Climate variability in Colombia, characterized by irregular rainfall, temperature fluctuations, and recurrent extreme events, has a direct impact on agricultural production and logistics, particularly for time sensitive crops. The proposed approach integrates short term climate forecasting based on historical meteorological observations with supply chain risk modeling to establish a conceptual early warning system architecture. A prototype implementation developed in a controlled computational environment demonstrates the feasibility of the framework using historical meteorological and agricultural time series derived from official statistics and reanalysis products, without reliance on satellite imagery or computer vision components. The methodology addresses the integration of climate nowcasting with supply chain decision making through explicit risk mapping, threshold-based categorization, and stakeholder-oriented risk signals. Results from synthetic and historical data experiments indicate that short term precipitation nowcasts can be translated into actionable risk indicators for agricultural supply chains, supporting anticipatory decisions related to inventory, sourcing, and transport.

</details>


### [174] [Fairness in Link Prediction Beyond Demographic Parity: A Reproducibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09899)
*Valentijn Oldenburg,Floris de Kam,Stef de Wildt,Jarno Nilson Balk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究复现并验证了Mattos等人（2025）的观点：人口统计均等（Δ_DP）无法检测排序中的曝光偏差，而排序感知的NDKL指标能够发现此类偏差；同时复现了MORAL后处理方法在曝光公平性上的有效性，并通过合成同质性设置、分类敏感属性及多种公平性/效用指标进行了鲁棒性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平排名链接预测中广泛使用Δ_DP作为公平性指标，但Mattos等人指出该指标忽略了链接在排名中的位置，无法检测曝光偏差。本文旨在复现这一主张并扩展验证，同时评估MORAL方法的有效性与鲁棒性，以确保相关工作结论的可靠性和可推广性。

Method: 通过实验复现Δ_DP在系统性地将某些子群对链接排名更低时仍显示总体均等的现象；评估排序感知的NDKL指标对这类偏差的检测能力；复现MORAL后处理方法；并在合成同质性设置、分类敏感属性等多样化场景下，引入子群对适应的AWRF等额外公平性和效用指标进行鲁棒性测试。

Result: 结果表明，曝光类指标能够揭示Δ_DP未能发现的隐藏偏差；MORAL在多种设置和数据集上均能显著降低曝光偏差，且仅带来极小的效用损失。

Conclusion: 研究确认了Δ_DP在暴露偏差检测上的不足，验证了NDKL等曝光感知指标的优越性，并确认MORAL作为一种有效的公平性后处理方法具有良好泛化能力；同时提供了经修正的可复现实现代码。

Abstract: In fair ranked link prediction, demographic parity ($Δ_\mathrm{DP}$) is a common fairness metric. Yet, Mattos et al. (2025) argue that it fails to detect exposure bias because it ignores where links appear in the ranking. In this study, we reproduce this claim by showing that $Δ_\mathrm{DP}$ can indicate aggregate parity even when some subgroup-pair links are systematically ranked lower than others. The proposed rank-aware Normalized Discounted KL-divergence (NDKL), however, does detect such disparities. We also reproduce the effectiveness of MORAL, a post-processing method that improves exposure-based fairness while maintaining competitive utility. Beyond reproduction, we assess robustness using synthetic homophily settings, categorical sensitive attributes, and additional fairness and utility metrics, including subgroup-pair-adapted Attention-Weighted Rank Fairness (AWRF). Overall, our results show that exposure-based metrics uncover biases hidden by $Δ_\mathrm{DP}$ and that MORAL reduces these biases with minimal utility loss across diverse settings and datasets. We release a corrected, reproducible implementation at https://github.com/Floris93100/reproducing-MORAL.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [An evolutionary model of animats with VLM-based subjective evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07537)
*Shota Miyazaki,Takaya Arita,Reiji Suzuki*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本研究提出一个将视觉语言模型（VLM）的主观评估融入遗传算法的适应度评价与选择过程的框架，通过对虚拟软体机器人的形态与运动进行成对比较（基于如"可爱"、"怪异"等主观评价词）来实现进化驱动，实验表明该方法比随机选择更快收敛，且不同评价词催生出相应独特的形态与运动模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统进化计算依赖客观适应度函数，难以处理主观评价驱动的进化问题。现有工作缺乏将人类或模型的主观语言评价直接作为进化选择压力的系统性框架，作者希望探索主观语言表达如何映射到具身表型（embodied phenotypes），并可视化这一进化过程。

Method: 提出一个混合框架：以虚拟软体机器人（具有柔性形态和运动能力）为进化对象，将VLM作为主观评估器，向其呈现两个个体运动过程的序列图像，通过成对比较（基于"adorably"、"weirdly"等主观评价词）产生选择压力，并将比较结果作为遗传算法中的适应度信号，同时进化形态与运动。辅助实验由人类参与者进行相同成对比较以验证VLM评估的合理性。

Result: 主观选择比随机选择的种群收敛速度更快；不同评价词催生出对应其语义的独特形态与运动；人类参与者与VLM的逐对选择一致性仅部分吻合，但整体形态和运动趋势定性相似，且人类重复评估产生明显疲劳；不同评价词下产生相似的进化结果，说明VLM并非字面理解这些词，而是在判断时将其分解为多个内部评价标准。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了主观语言表达映射到具身表型的进化过程，为分析VLM主观判断结构提供了基础框架，有望推动基于主观评价的进化计算与人工生命研究的新发展。

Abstract: In this study, we propose a framework that incorporates subjective evaluations provided by a Vision-Language Model (VLM) into the fitness evaluation and selection processes of a genetic algorithm. As the target of evolution, we employ virtual soft robots with flexible morphologies and locomotion and present the VLM with sequence images representing the locomotion of two individuals. Selection is performed via pairwise comparisons based on subjective evaluation terms such as adorably and weirdly. The outcomes of these comparisons are used as selection pressure within the genetic algorithm, enabling the simultaneous evolution of morphology and locomotion. Experimental results demonstrate that subjective selection by the VLM accelerates population convergence compared to random selection, while also giving rise to distinctive morphologies and motions corresponding to each evaluation term. An auxiliary experiment with human participants further showed that, although individual pairwise choices only partly agreed with the VLM selections, the resulting morphological and locomotion tendencies were qualitatively similar and repeated human evaluations imposed noticeable fatigue. Moreover, the observation that similar evolutionary outcomes emerged across different evaluation terms suggests that the VLM does not apply these terms in a purely literal manner but instead decomposes them into multiple internal evaluation criteria when making judgments. This work visualizes the evolutionary process through which subjective linguistic expressions are mapped onto embodied phenotypes and provides a foundational framework for analyzing the structure of subjective judgment in VLMs. The proposed approach is expected to contribute to new developments in evolutionary computation and artificial life research based on subjective evaluation.

</details>


### [176] [AutoPSO: A Meta-Framework for Automated Particle Swarm Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07539)
*Xinmeng Yu,Jiaxin Gao,Jianguo Zhang,Dongmei Jiang,Ran Cheng*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoPSO，一个高度自动化的元框架，用于构建定制化的粒子群优化（PSO）算法。AutoPSO将PSO优化建模为双层过程：外层搜索探索有效的PSO组件组合空间，内层实例化候选变体求解目标任务并提供反馈。通过EvoX实现种群张量化和批量评估，AutoPSO能够在实际时间预算内高效评估数千个粒子，在数值基准和神经进化机器人控制任务上持续发现优于强基线的新型PSO变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PSO变体研究存在两大挑战：一是大多数方法针对特定问题手工设计，跨任务泛化能力差，设计空间庞大且难以系统复用已有机制；二是主流实现受限于CPU计算，可扩展性差且实际应用计算成本高。

Method: AutoPSO将PSO优化建模为双层优化过程：外层搜索在开放设计组件池中探索PSO有效组件的联合空间；内层循环实例化候选变体以求解目标任务并提供反馈。外搜索支持灵活替换组件集合和外层优化器，利用EvoX进行种群张量化和批量评估以加速计算。

Result: 在数值基准和神经进化机器人控制任务上的综合实验表明，AutoPSO能持续发现显著优于强基线的新型PSO变体。消融实验验证了各算法组件的贡献，可扩展性研究确认AutoPSO在更大种群规模下性能增益递增。

Conclusion: AutoPSO有效解决了PSO变体设计的自动化和可扩展性问题，展示了元学习驱动的自动化算法设计范式的潜力。源码将公开以供复现和进一步研究。

Abstract: Particle swarm optimization (PSO) is a widely used metaheuristic, prized for its simplicity and small parameter set. Although decades of research have produced numerous PSO variants that improve performance by modifying key components (e.g., parameter schedules, swarm topologies, or updating rules), two fundamental challenges persist. First, most existing approaches are problem-specific and hand-crafted, leading to poor cross-task generalization and forcing practitioners to navigate an impractically large design space, which also hinders systematic reuse of prior effective mechanisms. Second, mainstream implementations remain CPU-bound, constraining scalability and substantially increasing computational cost in real-world applications. To address these challenges, we propose \emph{AutoPSO}, a highly automated meta-framework for constructing customized PSO algorithms. AutoPSO formulates PSO-based optimization as a bi-level process: an outer search explores the joint space of effective PSO components, while an inner loop instantiates candidate variants to solve the target task and provide feedback. The outer search operates over a curated, open-design component pool, supporting flexible replacement of the component set and the outer optimizer. Crucially, by leveraging EvoX for population tensorization and batched evaluations, AutoPSO can efficiently assess thousands of particles within practical time budgets. Comprehensive experiments on numerical benchmarks and neuroevolution robotic control tasks demonstrate that AutoPSO consistently discovers novel PSO variants that significantly outperform strong baselines. Ablation and scalability studies further highlight the contribution of individual algorithmic components and confirm that AutoPSO achieves increasing performance gains with larger swarm sizes. Source code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [177] [MOSAIC: Adversarial Co-evolution of Specialist Heuristics and Problem Instances for LLM-based Automated Heuristic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07544)
*Oguzhan Gungordu,Siheng Xiong,Faramarz Fekri*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出MOSAIC框架，通过对抗性共同进化问题实例与专家启发式算法，在质量-多样性（QD）档案中按结构特征组织，使启发式算法能针对不同实例分布区域特化，最终提取互补的启发式组合，在组合优化问题上超越现有LLM驱动的自动启发式设计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的自动启发式设计（AHD）框架存在两个关键缺陷：1）仅在固定小数据集上优化平均性能，忽视了不同实例分布下没有单一启发式能占优的事实；2）用标量好/坏反馈引导搜索，只能告诉LLM启发式是否改进，却无法说明在实例空间的哪个区域以及为何改进。需要一种能感知实例分布差异并理解改进原因的AHD方法。

Method: MOSAIC采用网格基的QD档案结构，按结构实例特征组织。核心机制包括：1）对抗性共同进化——实例进化以暴露当前启发式的弱点，启发式进化以特化到新暴露的区域；2）每个档案单元维护专家启发式、代表实例和区域洞察的持久记忆；3）对远距离网格区域采样的启发式对，LLM引导的进化循环生成判别性实例，决策树识别各启发式胜出的特征空间区域；4）反思LLM对比两个启发式产生多方向洞察，指导交叉和变异操作。档案同时作为共进化的判别性实例基准和区域专家启发式池，通过贪心选择提取紧凑互补组合。

Result: 在多个组合优化问题、多种测试规模和不同LLM骨干下，MOSAIC提取的启发式组合一致优于最先进的基于LLM的AHD方法；共进化的实例在特征空间覆盖率和启发式判别力上均优于现有进化实例生成基线。

Conclusion: 通过将实例与启发式的对抗性共同进化与QD档案结合，并利用决策树和反思LLM生成结构性、多方向的洞察，MOSAIC解决了标量反馈信息不足和单启发式难以通吃所有实例分布的根本问题，为LLM驱动的AHD提供了更强大、更具可解释性的新范式。

Abstract: Automated heuristic design (AHD) with large language models (LLMs) has produced strong heuristics for combinatorial optimization problems (COPs). Yet existing frameworks optimize for average performance on a small fixed dataset and steer the search with "verbal gradients" distilled from scalar better/worse feedback. No single heuristic dominates across instance distributions, and scalar feedback tells the LLM whether a heuristic improved, but not where in the instance space or why. We propose MOSAIC, a grid-based framework that adversarially co-evolves problem instances and specialist heuristics inside a Quality-Diversity (QD) archive indexed by structural instance features. Instances evolve to expose weaknesses of the current heuristics, and heuristics evolve to eliminate them by specializing to the newly exposed regions. Each archive cell keeps a specialist heuristic, representative instances, and insights explaining what works in its region, forming a persistent memory that accumulates over the evolutionary search. For each heuristic pair sampled from distant grid regions, an LLM-guided evolutionary loop generates discriminative instances, and a decision tree identifies the feature-space regions where each heuristic wins. A reflection LLM then contrasts the two heuristics to produce multi-directional insights that persist in those regions and guide crossover and mutation. The archive is simultaneously a co-evolved benchmark of discriminative instances and a pool of region specialist heuristics, from which greedy selection extracts a compact complementary portfolio. Across COPs, test sizes, and LLM backbones, the portfolio consistently outperforms state-of-the-art LLM-based AHD methods, and the co-evolved instances attain higher feature-space coverage and stronger heuristic discrimination than evolutionary instance-generation baselines.

</details>


### [178] [DarwinX: Evolving Agent Harnesses Through Natural Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07545)
*Yifan Zhang,Yutong Dai,Juntao Tan,Luyu Yang,Rishi Mullur,Thai Hoang,Zhiyuan Hu,James Zhu,Phil Mui,Silvio Savarese,Ran Xu,Zeyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: DarwinX提出了一种将LLM智能体的自我进化视为"句柄种群选择"的框架——冻结模型权重，通过对"句柄"（prompt、工具、技能、控制流）的种群进行保留与扩展式选择来实现持续进化，在四个基准上平均提升约17个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的能力不仅取决于模型权重，还取决于其"句柄"（prompt、工具、技能、控制流）。已有的自我改进循环仅进行单谱系搜索，存在路径依赖问题，且局部改进往往导致其他任务回退。需要一个更稳健的进化机制来避免这些缺陷。

Method: DarwinX将自我进化视为冻结模型下对句柄种群的"选择"。核心技术包括：(1) 保留与扩展契约——只接受扩展覆盖范围且不造成回退的变体；(2) 档案机制——保留替代谱系以供重组；(3) 失败、教师和自身衍生的证据共享同一编辑接口；(4) 适应度由各基准自带的验证器计算，无需gold解或手工挑选。

Result: 在四个随进化信号与测试逐步分离的基准上平均提升约17个点：Terminal-Bench 2.1在匹配基线上+7.7至83.2%，在更强基线上达84.7%的验证前沿；TerminalWorld留存测试集达68.3%超过所有现成agent；WebArena-Infinity真实任务pass@1从43.5%提升至93.0%（审计干净）；Terminal-Bench 2.1句柄可无修改迁移至SWE-bench Verified。

Conclusion: DarwinX证明进化的是一般性智能体能力而非基准特定的补丁，因此能在任务、验证器和基座模型变化时保持稳健。冻结的模型不必是固定的智能体——句柄选择能将评估算力转化为持久的智能能力。

Abstract: An LLM agent's capability depends not only on model weights but on its harness: prompts, tools, skills, and control flow. Self-improvement loops already edit harnesses, yet single-lineage search is path-dependent and local wins often regress other tasks. We introduce DarwinX, which treats self-evolution as selection over a population of harnesses with the model frozen: a preserve-and-extend contract admits only variants that extend coverage without regressing, an archive keeps alternative lineages for recombination, and failure-, teacher-, and self-derived evidence share one edit interface. Fitness comes from each benchmark's own verifier: no gold solutions, no hand-picked winners. Across four benchmarks that progressively separate the evolution signal from the test, one loop adds about 17 points on average: Terminal-Bench 2.1 rises +7.7 to 83.2% on a matched base and to the verified frontier at 84.7% on a stronger one; TerminalWorld's held-out split reaches 68.3%, ahead of every off-the-shelf agent; WebArena-Infinity real-task pass@1 rises from 43.5% to 93.0% audit-clean; and a Terminal-Bench 2.1 harness transfers unchanged to SWE-bench Verified. What evolves is general agent competence, not benchmark-specific patches, so it survives changes of task, verifier, and base model. A frozen model need not be a fixed agent: harness selection turns evaluation compute into durable capability.

</details>


### [179] [HPSO: Particle Swarm Optimization with Hypergraph-Based Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07587)
*Wenbin Pei,Xi Luo,Bing Xue,Mengjie Zhang,Qiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于超图的粒子群优化变体HPSO，利用超边连接多个粒子以捕捉高阶社交关系，并通过自适应超图更新策略保持种群多样性。在IEEE CEC'17基准测试中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PSO及其变体中常用的群体拓扑（如Ring和Star）本质上是图结构，每条边仅连接两个粒子，只能实现粒子对之间的直接交互，无法直接捕获复杂搜索空间中导航所必需的高阶社交关系，限制了算法的搜索性能。

Method: 提出HPSO（Hypergraph-assisted Particle Swarm Optimization）。核心设计包括：(1) 采用超图建模粒子群拓扑，超边可连接多个粒子，使多个粒子能直接交互；(2) 设计自适应超图更新策略，基于累积平均粒子位移周期性地重建拓扑，以在整个进化过程中维持种群多样性。

Result: 在IEEE CEC'17基准套件上验证了HPSO的有效性，实验表明HPSO在各类函数上均取得了有竞争力的性能。消融实验进一步证明HPSO具有优秀的搜索能力。

Conclusion: 超图拓扑能够更有效地建模粒子间的高阶交互关系，结合自适应拓扑更新策略，HPSO在复杂优化问题上展现出优越的搜索性能，为PSO的拓扑设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Particle swarm optimization (PSO) has been widely applied to solve complex optimization problems from real-world applications due to its efficient exploration of large solution spaces and the ability to converge towards optimal solutions without requiring gradient information. Common swarm topologies in standard PSO and its variants, e.g., Ring and Star, can be regarded as graphs, where each edge connects only two particles. Such topology structures allow direct interactions only between connected particle pairs, and thus often fail to directly capture the higher-order social relationships that are necessary for navigating complex search landscapes. Therefore, this article proposes a novel PSO variant termed Hypergraph-assisted Particle Swarm Optimization (HPSO). In HPSO, the topology of the particles in a swarm is modeled by a hypergraph, in which hyperedges are used to connect multiple particles. This allows multiple particles within a hyperedge to interact directly. Furthermore, an adaptive hypergraph updating strategy is designed to periodically reconstruct the topology based on cumulative average particle displacement, thereby maintaining swarm diversity throughout the evolutionary process. In the experiments, the effectiveness of HPSO is verified on the IEEE CEC'17 benchmark suite, and the results demonstrate that HPSO achieves promising performance across various types of functions. Furthermore, the ablation experiment demonstrates that HPSO has excellent search capabilities.

</details>


### [180] [Phase State Space Models: Parallel, Surrogate-Free Training of Spiking Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07754)
*Wilkie Olin-Ammentorp*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的态空间模型（SSM）框架，用于解释和实现共振-放电（R&F）神经网络，该框架兼容实值与脉冲输入、并行与循环执行，与超维（HD）计算有清晰联系，并保持生物真实性特征，同时实现了集成STFT、循环记忆和注意力特征的单脉冲兼容网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 状态空间模型（SSM）为递归网络的并行训练提供了强大的理论框架，本文旨在将SSM扩展到脉冲神经网络（特别是R&F神经元模型），以弥合理论框架与生物合理脉冲计算之间的差距，并为R&F网络提供新的理论解释。

Method: 本文将SSM框架应用于共振-放电（R&F）神经元的脉冲模型，形成一个新的解释框架，同时支持实值输入和脉冲输入，允许并行与循环两种执行模式。作者进一步实现了一个集成短时傅里叶变换（STFT）、循环记忆和注意力机制的单一脉冲兼容网络。

Result: 该方法的创新点在于：1）为R&F神经网络提供了与SSM兼容的新解释框架；2）统一了真实输入和脉冲输入的处理；3）支持并行训练和循环执行；4）与HD计算建立了明确联系；5）在实际实现中成功将STFT、循环记忆和注意力特征整合到单一脉冲兼容网络中。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了SSM在脉冲模型中的应用，为R&F网络提供了兼具理论严谨性和生物真实性的统一框架，同时展示了将信号处理（STFT）与记忆和注意力机制集成于脉冲网络中的可行性。

Abstract: State-space models (SSMs) provide a powerful theoretical framework to enable parallel training of recurrent networks. We expand on previous work adapting SSMs to spiking models to provide a novel interpretation of resonate-and-fire (R\&F) neural networks which is compatible both with real and spiking inputs, parallel and recurrent execution, has clear connections to hyperdimensional (HD) computing, and maintains biologically-realistic features. We demonstrate an implementation of this approach which integrates an STFT, recurrent memory, and attentional features within a single spike-compatible network.

</details>


### [181] [RotaryQuant: Fitting 120B MoE Models on Consumer Hardware via Fused Compressed-Space Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08081)
*Anthony. Lui,Mohamed. Elsaied,N. P. Savani*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: RotaryQuant 是一个三轴压缩系统，通过混合精度权重量化（dense 层4-bit、路由专家2-bit、共享专家8-bit）、LRU专家磁盘卸载和 IsoQuant KV缓存压缩（Walsh-Hadamard变换+块对角SO(4)旋转+3-bit标量量化），使26B-120B参数的MoE大模型能在16-32GB设备内存上以9-19 tok/s交互式运行，且近乎无损（ΔPPL ≤ +0.0012，32K上下文100%检索准确率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型MoE语言模型（26-120B参数）在消费级设备上受三重内存压力制约：常驻权重矩阵、随上下文线性增长的KV缓存，以及大量需按需分页的专家子层。现有方案无法同时解决这三个瓶颈，需要一种统一的三轴压缩方法来实现消费级设备上的MOE模型运行。

Method: RotaryQuant包含三个核心组件：(1) 混合精度权重量化，按架构角色分配位宽——dense层4-bit、路由专家2-bit、共享专家8-bit（因其高激活尖峰分布难以压缩）；(2) LRU专家卸载，在真实内存压力下将非驻留专家分页到磁盘；(3) IsoQuant KV缓存压缩，采用Walsh-Hadamard变换配合块对角SO(4)旋转将激活分布各向同性化后进行3-bit标量量化，仅需O(d log d)运算和每头256个存储参数（对比稠密旋转方法的O(d²)和16384）。此外，融合四核Metal GPU管线直接在打包的3-bit张量上执行注意力，无需物化全精度KV状态。

Result: 系统将Gemma 4-26B-A4B和Qwen3-30B-A3B适配到16GB内存预算内，Nemotron-H 120B适配到32GB内，实现9-19 tok/s的交互式推理速度，近乎零困惑度退化（ΔPPL ≤ +0.0012），并在32K上下文下保持100%检索准确率。

Conclusion: RotaryQuant证明通过三轴协同压缩（权重、专家、KV缓存），超大MoE模型可以在消费级设备上以接近无损的质量进行交互式推理，其中IsoQuant作为一种不同于传统纯量化方案的新执行模型，为KV缓存压缩提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Large mixture-of-experts (MoE) language models with 26--120 billion parameters exceed the memory capacity of consumer devices through three simultaneous pressures: resident weight matrices, key-value (KV) cache state that grows linearly with context, and dozens of expert sublayers that must be paged on demand. We present RotaryQuant, a three-axis compression system that addresses all three. Mixed-precision weight quantization assigns bit-widths by architectural role: 4-bit for dense layers, 2-bit for routed experts, and 8-bit for the shared expert whose high activation kurtosis resists aggressive compression. LRU expert offloading pages non-resident experts to disk under genuine memory pressure. The novel axis is IsoQuant, a KV cache compression method that applies a Walsh--Hadamard transform followed by block-diagonal SO(4) rotations to isotropize activation distributions before 3-bit scalar quantization, requiring $O(d \log d)$ operations and 256 stored parameters per head versus $O(d^2)$ and 16{,}384 for dense rotation methods. A fused four-kernel Metal GPU pipeline performs attention directly on packed 3-bit tensors without materializing full-precision KV state---a different execution model, not just a quantization scheme. The combined system fits Gemma 4-26B-A4B and Qwen3-30B-A3B within a 16\,GB budget and Nemotron-H 120B within 32\,GB, running interactively at 9--19 tok/s with near-zero perplexity degradation ($Δ$PPL $\leq +0.0012$) and 100\% retrieval accuracy at 32K context.

</details>


### [182] [SuperNeuroMAT: An Efficient Matrix-based Simulator for Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08479)
*Prasanna Date,Kevin Zhu,Shruti Kulkarni,Ashish Gautam,Chathika Gunaratne,Robert Patton,Tyler Nitzsche,Ian Mulet,Zachary Johnson-Scott,Addison Helms,Duncan Rowden,Simon Weston,Maryam Parsa,Catherine Schuman,Thomas Potok*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SuperNeuroMAT，一个基于Python的开源SNN仿真器，通过新颖的矩阵方法来建模LIF神经元动力学，原生支持稠密和稀疏执行模式，在标准硬件上能高效模拟约10,000（稠密）和100,000（稀疏）个神经元，在速度和内存方面一致优于NEST、Brian2、BindsNET和snnTorch四个既有仿真器，并广泛应用于机器学习基准、神经形态事件视觉任务以及通用计算任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络（SNN）在能效AI和类脑计算方面具有巨大潜力，但缺乏快速、易用且多功能的仿真框架，严重阻碍了其广泛采用。现有SNN仿真器在速度、可扩展性、易用性等方面存在局限，亟需一种高效且低门槛的仿真工具来加速神经形态计算领域的发展。

Method: 作者提出一种基于矩阵的新方法建模LIF（leaky integrate-and-fire）神经元动力学，原生支持稠密（dense）和稀疏（sparse）两种执行模式。该仿真器使用Python编写，开源且可在PyPI上安装，无需专门硬件即可在标准笔记本和台式机上运行。

Result: SuperNeuroMAT在稠密模式下可模拟约10,000个神经元，稀疏模式下可模拟约100,000个神经元。在两项性能指标（执行速度和峰值内存占用）以及多种网络规模和连接概率下，一致胜过NEST、Brian2、BindsNET和snnTorch四个已有SNN仿真器。此外，它不仅在Digits和引文网络等传统机器学习基准以及N-CARS和ASL-DVS等神经形态视觉任务上表现出色，还能扩展支持通用计算任务，包括实现了神经形态最短路径算法以及加法和乘法两种算术基本操作。

Conclusion: SuperNeuroMAT提供了一个快速、可扩展且多功能的SNN仿真框架，通过PyPI分发显著降低了进入神经形态计算领域的门槛，有望加速神经形态算法的更广泛开发与应用。该工作证明了基于矩阵的SNN仿真方法在性能和通用性上的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) offer a promising pathway to energy-efficient AI and brain-inspired computing. However, their widespread adoption is hindered by a lack of fast, accessible, and versatile simulation frameworks. In this paper, we introduce SuperNeuroMAT, an open-source, scalable, and highly efficient Python-based SNN simulator. We devise a novel matrix-based approach to model the leaky integrate-and-fire (LIF) neuron dynamics and natively support dense and sparse execution modes. This enables fast simulation of approximately 10,000 neurons in dense mode and 100,000 neurons in sparse mode on standard laptops and desktops without requiring specialized hardware. We demonstrate that SuperNeuroMAT consistently outperforms four established SNN simulators---NEST, Brian2, BindsNET, and snnTorch---on two performance metrics (execution speed and peak resident memory) and across various network sizes and connection probabilities. Furthermore, we demonstrate SuperNeuroMAT's applicability across a diverse set of problems. SuperNeuroMAT can efficiently handle conventional machine learning benchmarks such as the Digits and citation network datasets as well as neuromorphic event-based vision tasks such as N-CARS and ASL-DVS. Moreover, it can be extended beyond machine learning workloads and facilitate general-purpose workloads. We validated this by implementing the neuromorphic shortest path algorithm and two arithmetic primitives (addition and multiplication). SuperNeuroMAT can be installed via the Python Package Index (PyPI), thereby lowering the barrier to entry into the field of neuromorphic computing and accelerating the broader development of neuromorphic algorithms.

</details>


### [183] [A New Approach to Characterising Optimisation Problems Using Programmatic Representation and Complexity Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08898)
*Marcus Gallagher,Katherine M. Malan*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于程序代码表示的问题实例特征化方法，利用Halstead体积和程序熵作为代码复杂度度量，这些度量与算法性能呈负相关，可作为算法选择和其他问题分析的预测性元特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化问题实例的特征化对于理解算法行为和性能、以及算法选择和配置至关重要。现有的问题特征化方法通常需要对搜索空间进行采样，计算成本高且可能受变换影响，因此需要一种无需采样、快速计算且具有不变性的新方法。

Method: 提出基于目标函数程序实现的问题特征化方法。以Halstead体积作为代码复杂度的度量（可视为程序熵的简化版本），利用现有库快速计算目标函数代码的Halstead体积和熵。将该方法应用于BBOB优化问题套件和前馈神经网络训练任务，并分析这些度量与算法性能之间的相关性。

Result: 在BBOB问题套件和神经网络训练任务上验证了所提出的复杂度度量。结果表明这些度量与算法性能呈负相关，展现了作为算法选择和问题分析的预测性元特征的潜力。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于代码复杂度的度量可作为其他问题特征化方法的补充工具，具有无需采样搜索空间、对变换不变、自动计算快速的优点。

Abstract: Characterising optimisation problem instances is a fundamental part of understanding the behaviour and performance of different algorithms as well as providing information for algorithm selection and configuration. In this paper we propose a novel approach to problem characterisation based on the representation of instances when implemented as a program. The intuition is that the complexity of the code required to express an objective function should relate to the complexity of the search landscape. We identify the Halstead volume as a measure of code complexity, which can be seen as a simplified version of the entropy of the program. Given a code implementation of the objective function, the Halstead volume and entropy can be quickly calculated using existing libraries. We apply the proposed complexity measures to the well-known BBOB optimisation problem suite and the simple feed-forward neural network training task. We also show that the measures are negatively correlated with algorithm performance and therefore show potential as predictive meta-features for algorithm selection and other problem analysis. We envisage the proposed measures as complementary to other problem characterisation approaches, but with the advantages of not requiring any sampling of the search space, being invariant to transformations, and being very quick to calculate automatically.

</details>


### [184] [Identifying potentiating events in evolutionary search using replay experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09833)
*Austin J. Ferguson,Alexander Lalejini*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文首次将"重放实验"（replay experiments）引入进化计算领域，提供了一套设计该实验的逐步指南，并通过遗传编程实例证明种群在问题求解成功率方面的增强潜力与其适应度增长并不必然对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化计算领域缺少一种系统化的实证方法来识别那些提高某个进化结果出现可能性的"增强事件"（potentiating events）。重放实验源于实验室实验进化，能够通过从不同历史时间点重新启动种群进化来量化历史对种群未来进化潜力的影响，本文希望将此方法引入进化计算以拓展其理论基础。

Method: 作者提出了一个在进化计算系统中设计重放实验的逐步指南，并给出了一个示范性实验：在遗传编程种群中，通过从不同历史时间点重放进化过程，测量问题求解成功潜力的变化情况。

Result: 示范性实验表明，种群在问题求解成功率上的增强潜力与种群适应度的增加并不一定同步对应，即适应度提升并不必然意味着未来成功潜力的增强。

Conclusion: 分析性重放实验是扩充进化计算理论基础的有力工具，本文为未来的研究方向提供了建议，包括利用该方法探索进化历史对多种进化结果的影响。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce analytical replay experiments to the evolutionary computing community. Replay experiments originated in the context of laboratory experimental evolution as an empirical approach to identifying potentiating events that increased the likelihood of an observed evolutionary outcome. By restarting a population's evolution from different historical time points, replay experiments sample the distribution of what could have evolved from different points in time, which allows us to quantify how a population's potential for different evolutionary outcomes changed as a result of that population's history. In this work, we give a step-by-step guide to designing replay experiments for evolutionary computing systems. We then provide a demonstrative example replay experiment that measures how potentiation for problem-solving success changed in an evolved genetic programming population, showing that increases in potential for success do not necessarily correspond with increases in a population's fitness. Broadly, we argue that analytical replay experiments can be a powerful tool for expanding the theoretical foundations of evolutionary computing, and we offer suggestions for promising future research directions enabled by replay experiments.

</details>


### [185] [BDH-CQ: In-Context Learning with Recurrent Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09888)
*Björn Engdahl,Adrian Kosowski,Jan Chorowski,Zuzanna Stamirowska,Przemysław Uznański,Junlin Jiang,Rohan Phadke,Remigiusz Kinas,Richard Zhong*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出了BDH-CQ，一种结合上下文学习与循环潜在推理的推理模型，在ARC-AGI-1基准上以150M参数配置达到29.5% pass@2，单任务推理成本仅\$0.0007，突破了该基准的成本-精度帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理模型要么依赖昂贵的显式思维链（verbalized reasoning），要么在ARC-AGI-1基准上难以在成本与精度之间取得良好平衡。作者希望通过让推理在隐式潜在空间中进行（而非口头表达），并结合上下文学习来持续更新记忆，从而以更低成本实现更强的推理能力。

Method: BDH-CQ将推理时的输入通过上下文学习持续更新模型的循环记忆（recurrent memory），随后在高层潜在空间中以迭代计算方式求解查询，整个过程不将中间推理步骤进行语言化表达。模型在公开的ARC-AGI-1测试集上评估，并设计了受控ARC类干预实验来系统研究其从示范中学习的内容、推断变换的一致性以及难以掌握的概念。

Result: 150M参数配置在ARC-AGI-1上达到29.5% pass@2@1，单任务计算成本为\$0.0007，突破了此前报告的ARC-AGI-1成本-精度帕累托前沿，建立了该基准上成本效率的新最先进水平。

Conclusion: 证明了无需显式思维链、通过在潜在空间中进行循环推理并结合上下文学习，可以在极低计算成本下取得具有竞争力的推理性能，为低成本推理模型的设计开辟了新方向。

Abstract: We introduce BDH-CQ, a reasoning model that combines in-context learning with recurrent latent reasoning. Inputs presented at inference time continuously update the model's recurrent memory; the model then solves a query through iterative computation in a high-dimensional latent space, without verbalizing its intermediate reasoning. We evaluate the model on the public ARC-AGI-1 evaluation set and use controlled ARC-like interventions to study what it learns from demonstrations, how consistently it applies an inferred transformation, and which concepts remain difficult. A 150M-parameter configuration reaches 29.5% pass@2 at a computed inference cost of \$0.0007 per task. This operating point breaks through the previously reported ARC-AGI-1 cost-accuracy Pareto frontier, establishing a new state of the art in benchmark cost efficiency.

</details>
